### [1] [PEACE-TEST-PROTOCOL: Polarization-Keyed Examination Papers and Mandatory Issued Eyewear that Displace AI Glasses and Extend Universal Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31299)
*Dohyeon Kwon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 PEACE-TEST-PROTOCOL：让考场统一发放偏光眼镜，试卷文字以圆偏振手性编码，裸眼与无滤波相机只见均匀灰色，仅凭发放的圆偏振分析器可读，从而在"谁拥有眼镜"层面阻断相机眼镜作弊。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防作弊手段中，设备检测针对"持有设备"，模型侧防护（companion work SEOYUN）针对模型，但都未改变相机AI眼镜这一形式本身的可利用性；需要从"眼镜归属权"入手，使考场内唯一存在的近眼设备由考试机构提供并回收，任何其他位置相机都必须自带分析器才能成像。

Method: 以电影立体成像原理为类比，用图案化层压片将文本编码进圆偏振的旋向而非亮度；推导亮度与对比度预算，论证圆偏振键控对头部倾斜的容忍性；用光谱Mueller矩阵模型分析倾斜不变性、对线偏振相机的不可见性及轴配准误差泄漏；在英文与韩文文本上做"拍摄-OCR"实验考察视频平均恢复弱泄漏；并以151名观察者、28台相机的数据检验光谱（同色异谱）密钥的可感知性。

Result: Mueller模型确认倾斜不变且对线偏振相机不可见，但1°轴配准失配会泄漏数个百分点对比度；拍摄-OCR实验表明视频帧平均可恢复单帧不可读的泄漏，故泄漏须对抗手的"总光子预算"设限；151名观察者与28台相机均能看见光谱（同色异谱）密钥。所有结果均为仿真，无原型、物理测量或人体实验数据。

Conclusion: 该协议把智能眼镜的形态反向利用，通过"强制配发+偏振编码"将相机位置收归主办方控制，并对残障考生提供处方插片、离线辅助MR与盲文/音频/读屏等替代通道；但密钥本身不具保密性，带滤波的隐蔽相机仍可能成像，且结论尚停留在仿真层面，需原型与实测验证。

Abstract: Camera-equipped AI glasses turn an examination page into a query for a remote vision-language model. Device detection targets possession, and our companion work SEOYUN targets the model. PEACE-TEST-PROTOCOL instead changes who owns the eyewear. Every candidate wears authority-issued glasses for the whole session, and the question paper is printed so that it can be read only through them. As in cinema stereoscopy, a patterned laminate encodes the text in the handedness of circular polarization rather than in brightness. The page looks uniformly grey to the naked eye and to unfiltered cameras, and the issued circular analyzer reveals the text. Compulsory issued eyewear turns the smart-glasses form factor against itself. The face position is occupied by a device the authority supplies and collects, and a camera anywhere else needs its own analyzer. Spectacle wearers receive prescription inserts in the issued frame, low-vision candidates may use an offline assistive mixed-reality tier, and blind candidates keep braille, audio, and screen-reader formats. We derive the luminance and contrast budget and show why circular keying tolerates head tilt. A simulation study supports the analysis. A spectral Mueller model confirms tilt invariance and invisibility to linear-polarization cameras, but a 1 degree axis misregistration leaks a few percent of contrast. A capture-and-OCR experiment on English and Korean text shows that video averaging can recover leakage too weak to read in a single frame, so leakage must be bounded against the adversary's total photon budget. Data from 151 observers and 28 cameras show that spectral (metameric) keys are visible to all of them. The key carries no secrecy, and filtered covert cameras remain possible. All results are simulations; no prototypes, physical measurements, or human-subject results are reported.

</details>


### [2] [Nemotron-Labs-VisualCreation: Pixel as Code for Agentic Visual Creation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31358)
*Guo Chen,Fuxiao Liu,Haoqin Tu,Yiyang Zhou,Andrew Tao,Bryan Catanzaro,Cihang Xie,Yuyin Zhou,Huaxiu Yao,Didong Lu,Zongxia Li,Guilin Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Nemotron-Labs-VisualCreation 智能体框架，以"像素即代码"（Pixel as Code）连接光栅图像与结构化产物，并配套自研 tokenizer、自回归图像生成模型与结构化代码模型，在图、矢量图、网页与文档上取得具竞争力性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉创作既需要富有表现力的图像，又需要对文本、版式与结构进行精确控制；单一模态生成模型难以同时满足二者。

Method: 使用学习到的离散视觉 token 表示光栅图像，结构化产物用显式程序表示；共享的"生成—渲染"接口向图像、矢量图形、网页和文档提供视觉反馈；Nemotron 原生 harness 负责规划与光栅候选选择，并对可编辑的结构化/混合产物做定向修复。配套组件：Nemotron-Labs-Image-Tokenizer、自回归生成模型 Nemotron-Labs-Image-30B-A3B、结构化代码模型族 Nemotron-Labs-VisionCode。

Result: 在约178K张、覆盖六个视觉域的图像上，tokenizer 重建 PSNR 由 Emu3.5 IBQ 的26.08 dB 提升至29.57 dB；A100 批大小1时编码快7.13倍、解码快10.93倍。VisionCode 某变体在 Chart2Code、MMSVG、Design2Code、Image2Struct 综合指标上超过 Kimi K3.1 Pro。系统层面，同款光栅生成器下 Nemotron 原生配置将 GenEval2 Soft-TIFA GM 从31.71提升至52.56；最佳设置下多数可比较基准上与 GPT Image 2、Nano Banana Pro 竞争力相当。

Conclusion: 通过统一的光栅 token 与结构化程序表示及智能体式规划—修复回路，可在保持图像表达力的同时获得精确的结构控制，验证了"像素即代码"范式的有效性。

Abstract: Visual creation demands expressive imagery together with precise control over text, layout, and structure. We introduce Nemotron-Labs-VisualCreation, an agentic framework that connects these capabilities through Pixel as Code. Raster images are represented by learned discrete visual tokens, while structured artifacts use explicit programs. A shared generation-and-rendering interface connects these representations to visual feedback across images, vector graphics, webpages, and documents. A Nemotron-native harness coordinates planning and raster candidate selection, with targeted repair for editable structured and hybrid artifacts. To support this interface, we develop Nemotron-Labs-Image-Tokenizer, the autoregressive raster generator Nemotron-Labs-Image-30B-A3B, and the structured-code model family Nemotron-Labs-VisionCode. On approximately 178K images spanning six visual domains, our tokenizer improves reconstruction PSNR from 26.08 to 29.57 dB over Emu3.5 IBQ. It also provides 7.13x faster encoding and 10.93x faster decoding at batch size one on A100. In standalone evaluation, one Nemotron-Labs-VisionCode variant surpasses Kimi K3.1 Pro on the aggregate across Chart2Code, MMSVG, Design2Code, and Image2Struct. At the system level, the Nemotron-native configuration raises GenEval2 Soft-TIFA GM from 31.71 to 52.56 using the same raster generator. With our best setting, Nemotron-Labs-VisualCreation achieves performance competitive with GPT Image 2 and Nano Banana Pro across most benchmarks with available comparisons.

</details>


### [3] [Look Where Camouflage Breaks: Concealment-Guided Observation for Camouflaged Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31392)
*Dingming Zhang,Fengyang Xiao,Jingjia Feng,Chunming He,Sina Farsiu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CamoBreak主动式伪装目标检测框架，以"隐避谱"跨尺度度量区域相似性与边界模糊性，指导裁剪、识别与证据更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 伪装目标在某尺度上与背景融合、在另一尺度上可区分，但"区域外观模糊"本身无法指示哪种观测能解除歧义；核心挑战是把隐蔽的形式与尺度同"新视角可揭示的证据"联系起来。

Method: 构建由Concealment Spectrum（隐避谱）驱动的主动框架：基于图像与当前预测，在多比较尺度上度量区域相似度和边界模糊度，区分持续性隐蔽与在更细/更粗尺度上出现的分离；据此指导从已获取图像中裁剪选区和对照全局前景/背景参考的识别；再用目标条件空间偏好选择局部证据更新预测与特征记忆的位置，保留目标修正、抑制背景干扰；被接受的修正反过来重塑下一次度量，并以预测的分割收益决定是否再次观测。

Result: 在多种编码器家族及相关分割任务上取得一致性提升；针对性分析支持小目标恢复、多目标覆盖和选择性证据整合的改进。

Conclusion: 以跨尺度隐避度量连接隐蔽形式与可揭示证据，是实现主动式伪装目标检测的有效途径；代码将开源。

Abstract: A camouflaged object can blend into its surroundings at one scale and become distinguishable at another. Yet a region's apparent ambiguity gives limited guidance on which observation would resolve it. The challenge is to connect the form and scale of concealment to the evidence that a new view can reveal. We introduce CamoBreak, an active framework for camouflaged object detection guided by a Concealment Spectrum. From the image and current predictions, the spectrum measures regional resemblance and boundary ambiguity across comparison scales, distinguishing persistent concealment from separation emerging at finer or broader scales. These measurements guide crop selection from the already-captured image and recognition against global foreground and background references. An object-conditioned spatial preference then selects where local evidence should update the prediction and feature memory, retaining target corrections while suppressing background distractions. Accepted corrections reshape the next measurement, while predicted segmentation benefit determines whether to observe again. Experiments across encoder families and related segmentation tasks show consistent improvements. Targeted analyses support improved small-target recovery, multiple-object coverage, and selective evidence integration. Code will be released.

</details>


### [4] [CoRe: Jointly Learning Contextual Representations for Understanding and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31397)
*Hangke Sui,Chang Li,Yuyan Li,Mohammad Haris Baig,Daniel Ulbricht*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 CoRe：图像与文本各用一份"理解+生成"共同塑造的上下文表征，从零训练；生成以连续流在高斯噪声上在该表征空间内完成。383M 参数、仅用 CC12M 训练、推理时无预训练语言模型/文本编码器/VAE，即在全局理解上追平同数据纯对比基线，并达到 COCO-30K FID 10.10，媲美基于预训练组件的大型生成器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言模型通常把"理解用表征"与"生成用表征"分开：生成依赖预训练组件（LM、文本编码器、VAE），理解表征被冻结后用于生成，导致组件冗余、信息受限。作者假设单一表征可同时服务理解与生成，并系统检验生成对表征的正向作用。

Method: 为图像和文本各学习一份上下文表征，训练中三者协同：该表征被读出用于理解任务、作为条件调节另一模态、并作为该模态从高斯噪声出发的连续流生成的目标空间。模型 383M 参数，CC12M 训练，推理不含预训练语言模型、文本编码器或 VAE。设置等数据等架构的对照实验：(1) 仅理解塑造后冻结的表征；(2) 理解不塑造的生成空间。并用注意力分割探针、单目深度、冻结语言模型读出等更强读出方式评估，及少步蒸馏测试。

Result: 全局理解追平同数据纯对比基线；COCO-30K FID 10.10。等数据等架构下，双向生成（文生图、图生文）均优于"仅理解后冻结"表征；提示遵循优于理解不塑造的生成空间。在下游上超过纯对比基线：注意力分割探针、单目深度、冻结语言模型读出。因生成在理解表征内进行，冻结的真实图像探针可读取并操控中间状态，模型无需额外训练即可对自身样本排序；两模态均用连续流，均可蒸馏至少步。

Conclusion: 对照实验揭示了单一表征足够的原因：理解使表征语义有序（semantic organization），生成使其保留更多信息（information retention），二者互补而非冲突。CoRe 表明无需预训练组件即可实现有竞争力的理解与生成，并为表征可控性、自评估与少步采样提供便利。

Abstract: We introduce CoRe, in which understanding and generation jointly shape one contextual representation for images and one for text, trained from scratch. Each representation is read for understanding, conditions the other modality, and is the space in which that modality is generated by a continuous flow from Gaussian noise. With 383M parameters trained on CC12M and no pretrained language model, text encoder, or VAE at inference, CoRe matches a same-data contrastive-only baseline in global understanding and reaches COCO-30K FID 10.10, comparable to much larger generators built on pretrained components. In controlled comparisons at equal data and architecture, CoRe generates better in both directions (text to image and image to text) than a representation shaped by understanding alone and then frozen, and follows prompts better than generating in a space that understanding does not shape. In the other direction, with an attentive segmentation probe, on monocular depth, and when read by a frozen language model, CoRe surpasses the contrastive-only baseline: generation adds information that stronger readouts reach. Because generation runs inside the understanding representation, frozen real-image probes read and steer intermediate states and the model ranks its own samples without extra training. Because both modalities use continuous flows, both also distill to a few steps. Our controls indicate why one representation suffices: understanding organizes the representation semantically, while generation makes it retain more information.

</details>


### [5] [Enhancing Sparse-View Gaussian Splatting with Feed-Forward Priors via Spectral Fusion and Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31428)
*Weikai Lin,Jun Dai,Sheng Zhao,Junjie Zhao,Yuhao Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出即插即用的频谱融合方法，用预训练前馈NVS模型蒸馏增强稀疏视角高斯溅射，在不牺牲渲染速度的前提下提升质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏训练视角下的高斯溅射在训练视角附近渲染质量高，但远处欠采样区域重建质量下降；前馈NVS模型虽有强先验可从差异大的视角重采样，却渲染慢十倍以上且对域外场景产生高频伪影。二者在频域上互补。

Method: 观察到先验在低频更准确、高斯在高频更准确，且先验相对高斯的可靠性随图像频率升高而下降，故最优融合权重是一阶低通滤波器（单参数可拟合）。据此智能融合两者可靠部分；为避免渲染时使用慢速前馈模型，将融合图像作为教师蒸馏回高斯，并与输入视角监督平衡。

Result: 提升多种稀疏视角高斯方法的质量，且不牺牲渲染速度；该分析对不同先验模型均有效。

Conclusion: 通过频域可靠性分析实现先验与高斯的互补融合，并以蒸馏方式将教师收益固化到高斯表示中，形成通用、可即插即用的稀疏视角NVS增强方案。

Abstract: Gaussian splatting from sparse training views renders high-quality images near training views, but reconstruction quality degrades at distant regions, which are undersampled by the training set. Feed-forward novel view synthesis (NVS) models use strong learned priors to resample the scene from substantially different viewpoints, but they take more than ten times as long to render and produce high-frequency artifacts on out-of-domain scenes. We propose a plug-and-play method that enhances an existing sparse-view Gaussian model with a pretrained feed-forward NVS model. We observe that the two are complementary in the frequency domain: the prior is more accurate at low frequencies, and the Gaussians at high frequencies. The reliability of the prior relative to the Gaussians is inversely proportional to the image frequency, so the optimal fusion weight is a first-order low-pass filter with one parameter that can be fitted. Our key idea is then to intelligently fuse the reliable part of each of the two methods. To remove the slow feed-forward model at render time, we use these fused images as teachers and distill them back into Gaussians, balanced with supervision from input views. Our method improves the quality of various sparse-view Gaussian methods without sacrificing rendering speed, and our analysis works on different prior models. Project page: https://linwk20.github.io/spectral-fusion-gs/

</details>


### [6] [Tornado4D: Physics-Guided 4D Gaussian Reconstruction of a Laboratory Tornado from Sparse One-Sided Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31435)
*Yucheng Zhu,Maria J. Molina,Christopher Metzler*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Tornado4D：利用封闭几何与旋转气流场先验，从稀疏单侧视频重建实验室龙卷风的时间分辨4D高斯表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 龙卷风的时空演化对理解与防灾至关重要，但实验室单侧稀疏视频重建高度欠约束：外观视角依赖、运动非刚性、未观测体积存在多解。

Method: 构建Tornado4D物理引导的4D高斯框架：借助封闭腔几何引入旋转气流场先验，约束3D结构随时间的演化，从而重建未观测结构。

Result: 在留出相机视角上以新视角合成精度评估，显著优于逐帧3DGS与基于视频的4DGS。

Conclusion: 物理先验能有效缓解稀疏单侧视角下的欠约束问题，实现实验室龙卷风的时间分辨三维重建。

Abstract: Characterizing how tornadoes evolve over space and time is critically important for understanding and preparing for these highly destructive weather events. Towards this end, we develop algorithms capable of forming time-resolved 3D reconstructions of laboratory smoke tornadoes from sparse one-sided videos. Even in a controlled environment, reconstruction is severely underconstrained: appearance is view dependent, motion is non-rigid, and the unobserved volume admits many geometries. We present Tornado4D, a physics-guided 4D Gaussian framework capable of reconstructing laboratory tornadoes from sparse, one-sided videos. Tornado4D uses knowledge of the enclosure geometry to introduce rotational airflow field priors that constrain how the 3D structure evolves over time. These rotational priors allow us to reconstruct otherwise unobserved structure. We evaluate reconstruction accuracy by computing novel-view synthesis accuracy on a held out camera view: Tornado4D outperforms frame-by-frame 3DGS and video-based 4DGS by a considerable margin.

</details>


### [7] [Which Ellipses Are Outlines of a Given Ellipsoid? A Ratio Test with Unknown Focal Length](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31437)
*Michael Werman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究在方像素、零倾斜、已知主点的相机模型下，给定图像中的椭圆何时是给定半轴 $a\le b\le c$ 椭球体的轮廓。结论是：轮廓判定只取决于由两个特征值计算的锥比 $\kappa(f)$ 与 $a/c$ 的比较，中间半轴 $b$ 不起作用；焦距未知时可行焦距构成区间，区间是否为空由 $\kappa(f)$ 的最小值决定，该最小值可由显式四次方程求得，且仅当主点更靠近椭圆长轴时区间才有上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像中一个椭圆能否由某个三维椭球体投影产生，是单目视觉中形状与位姿推断的基础问题。已有方法多关注给定相机内参下的位姿估计，而对“给定焦距不确定或未知时，哪些椭球体可被该椭圆合理排除”缺乏简洁的判据。作者希望给出一个仅依赖少量特征量的充要条件，从而在无需完整重建的情况下直接排除不可能的椭球形状。

Method: 在方像素、零倾斜、主点已知的相机模型下，将椭圆与椭球体的投影关系转化为二次锥（cone）约束；由椭圆的两个特征值构造随焦距 $f$ 变化的锥比 $\kappa(f)$，证明该椭圆是椭圆轮廓当且仅当 $\kappa(f)\le a/c$。对焦距未知的情形，分析 $\kappa(f)$ 在允许范围内的取值，证明可行焦距集合为区间，其非空性由 $\min_f \kappa(f)$ 决定，并通过显式四次方程刻画该最小值的取到条件；进一步分析可行区间有上界的几何条件（主点相对椭圆长轴与短轴的距离关系）。最后以仿真与 45 幅土星卫星的 Cassini 图像进行验证。

Result: 理论上得到简洁的充要判据：$\kappa(f)\le a/c$ 等价于该椭圆为给定椭球体在某焦距下的轮廓，且中间半轴 $b$ 不影响判定；焦距未知时可行焦距为区间，其是否为空由最小锥比决定并可经显式四次方程计算，区间仅当主点更靠近椭圆长轴时才有上界。据此可得不对称的排除能力：单个椭圆能够排除“过于接近球形”的椭球体，但不能排除“过于细长”的；若主点未知，则任何椭球体都无法被排除。仿真实验与 45 幅 Cassini 土星卫星图像支持该检验方法。

Conclusion: 该工作为“图像椭圆是否为某椭球体轮廓”提供了基于锥比的简洁充要判据与可计算的焦距可行性区间分析，揭示了投影约束在形状排除上的单侧性（只能排除过圆、不能排除过细长）以及主点信息对判别能力的关键作用，并在真实天体图像上得到验证，具有用于形状先验筛选与焦距约束的实用价值。

Abstract: When is an image ellipse the outline of a given ellipsoid with semi-axes $a <= b <= c? For a camera with square pixels, zero skew and known principal point, we show that it is an outline at focal length $f$ exactly when a cone ratio kappa(f), computed from two eigenvalues, is at most a/c; the middle semi-axis b plays no role. With f unknown, the admissible focal lengths form an interval; whether it is empty is decided by the smallest cone ratio over $f$, which, when attained, is obtained from an explicit quartic. The interval is bounded above only when the principal point is closer to the long axis of the ellipse than to its short axis. Thus one ellipse can rule out ellipsoids that are too round, never ones that are too elongated, and none if the principal point is unknown. Simulations and 45 Cassini images of Saturn's moons support the test.

</details>


### [8] [Ground, Guide, Act: Improving VLA Spatial Generalization with Multimodal Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31452)
*Guangting Zheng,Shaoxuan Wang,Yu Yuan,Zhipeng Tang,Jiajun Deng,Yanyong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出选择性协作框架 MFM-VLA：由多模态基础模型负责目标定位与空间引导、控制器执行自由空间运动、VLA 保留精细操作，并用能力路由器决定是否介入，显著提升空间泛化成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型能从示教中学到动作策略，但对物体位置变化与任务目标变化泛化差；通用多模态基础模型（MFM）具备场景理解与空间推理能力，却慢且不可靠，难以直接做细粒度控制。作者希望厘清 MFM 应以何种方式补足 VLA。

Method: 先做系统性能力分析：在多种 VLA 架构上用原子阶段干预，定位失败来源（目标定位/ grounding 弱、全局空间路径规划差，局部操作技能基本可用）。据此提出 MFM-VLA：MFM 提供目标 grounding 与空间引导，控制器负责自由空间移动，VLA 负责细粒度操作；以 action-chunk 级稠密几何描述与扰动状态恢复数据微调 VLA；并用开发集校准的能力路由器在直接执行与 MFM 辅助执行间选择，避免不必要介入。

Result: 在 LIBERO-PRO、ALOHA-Sim-PRO、RoboCasa365 及真实化学实验任务上，成功率分别比对应基座 VLA 提升 14.9、74.0、3.6、30.9 个百分点。

Conclusion: VLA 的空间泛化瓶颈主要在于高层空间路由与目标 grounding，而非局部操作；按能力边界进行模型间选择性分工是有效且高收益的改进路径，配合任务内微调与路由器可兼顾鲁棒性与效率。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models effectively learn action policies from robot demonstrations, but struggle to generalize to changes in object positions and task goals. General multimodal foundation models (MFMs) offer complementary scene understanding and spatial reasoning, but remain slow and unreliable for direct fine-grained control. To understand how MFMs can improve VLA spatial generalization, we conduct a systematic capability analysis across multiple VLA architectures using atomic-stage interventions, revealing that many failures stem from poor target grounding and global spatial routing, while local manipulation skills remain largely usable. Based on these findings, we propose MFM-VLA, a selective collaboration framework in which an MFM provides target grounding and spatial guidance, controllers execute free-space motion, and the VLA retains control over fine-grained manipulation. We fine-tune the VLA with action-chunk-level dense geometric captions and perturbed-state recovery data to improve local guidance following and recovery from unfamiliar handoff states. A development-calibrated capability router selects between direct VLA execution and MFM-assisted execution to avoid unnecessary intervention. Across LIBERO-PRO, ALOHA-Sim-PRO, RoboCasa365, and real-world chemistry tasks, MFM-VLA improves success rates by 14.9, 74.0, 3.6, and 30.9 percentage points, respectively, over the corresponding base VLA. The project page is available at https://tom-zgt.github.io/MFM-VLA

</details>


### [9] [A dataset of clinically similar image pairs from citation-linked case reports for medical image retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31465)
*Guy Shmueli,Keno K. Bressem,Tom Hope*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文构建了PMC-PaIR数据集（来自56,013篇开放获取PMC病例报告的86,144对临床相似图像对），并提出基于引用追踪与LLM层级问答引导的图像匹配方法，用其微调BiomedCLIP后显著提升跨模态、跨解剖与跨诊断检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床相似患者图像的检索可辅助诊断、教学与研究，但训练与评估检索系统需要知道不同患者的哪些图像显示相同征象，而现有数据集规模小、覆盖范围窄。病例报告常记录罕见或疑难表现，且引用常连接相似病例，可被利用来低成本构建大规模相似图像对。

Method: 1) 构建PMC-PaIR数据集：从56,013篇开放获取PubMed Central病例报告中抽取86,144对临床相似图像，通过追踪病例报告间的引用关系获得候选配对。2) 设计LLM引导的层级问答流程：从成像模态到诊断、征象逐层生成并回答问题，判断两篇被引用病例在临床上共享什么信息，并据此用caption进行图像匹配。3) 在该数据集上微调BiomedCLIP，评估图像单模态查询与"图像+简短文本命名征象"查询下的检索表现。

Result: 专家评审表明所构建图像对准确率高；问答引导的匹配方法找到的正确配对数量是直接匹配的两倍；在PMC-PaIR上微调后的BiomedCLIP在所有测试的模态、解剖部位和诊断类别上均取得检索性能提升，且对纯图像查询和图文混合查询均有效。

Conclusion: 引用追踪结合LLM层级问答是构建大规模临床相似图像对的有效自动化路径；PMC-PaIR数据集与自动化流水线已公开，并可随新病例报告发表持续扩展，为临床相似图像检索的训练与评估提供可扩展基础。

Abstract: Retrieving images of patients clinically similar to a given patient can support diagnosis, teaching and research. Training and evaluating retrieval systems requires knowing which images of different patients show the same finding, and existing datasets are small or narrow in scope. We construct PMC-PaIR, a dataset of 86,144 pairs of clinically similar images from 56,013 open-access PubMed Central case reports, which often document rare or challenging presentations. We develop an approach that follows citations, which often link case reports of similar patients. To determine what two linked cases share clinically, it prompts large language models to generate and answer hierarchical questions, from modality to diagnosis and findings, and to match images by caption using the answers. Experts find the pairs highly accurate, and this question-guided approach finds twice as many correct pairs as direct matching. Fine-tuning BiomedCLIP on PMC-PaIR improves retrieval in all modality, anatomy and diagnosis categories tested, for image-only queries and image queries with a short text naming the finding. We release the dataset and the automated pipeline, which can extend PMC-PaIR as new case reports are published.

</details>


### [10] [AdProducer: Towards Agentic Advertising Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31466)
*Kunyang Li,Chaoyang Wang,Mubarak Shah,Yuzhang Shang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AdProducer：首个端到端广告视频生成智能体框架，将创意简述转化为成品广告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频生成技术使广告自动化生产成为可能，但有效广告需忠实呈现产品并通过有说服力的视觉叙事传达卖点，这要求产品理解与呈现策略决策，而非单纯视觉合成；且产品调研与创意设计紧密耦合。

Method: 构建多模态智能体，通过网页与图像搜索自主调研产品，将结果转化为卖点驱动的分镜脚本以指导冻结的视频生成器；设计专用数据构建流程，产出 AdProducer-SFT 与 AdProducer-RL 两个训练集；采用监督微调后接强化学习，联合优化分镜级与视频级奖励；提出 AdCreation-Bench（305 条简述、8 个领域）及 PG-Score 与 Ad-Score 两项指标。

Result: 将 MiniMax H3 的 PG-Score 从 17.3 提升至 51.3；无需额外训练即可泛化至 Veo 3.1，分数由 28.0 升至 35.9；Ad-Score、VideoScore2 及人工评估均有稳定提升。

Conclusion: 产品调研与创意设计的联合建模对广告视频生成至关重要，所提框架、数据与评测基准可有效提升产品正确性与广告效果，并具备跨生成器的泛化能力。

Abstract: Recent advances in video generation have made automated advertising production increasingly feasible. However, an effective advertisement must faithfully represent the product and communicate its selling points through compelling visual storytelling, which requires more than visual synthesis: it calls for deeper product understanding and strategic decisions about how to present product value. Crucially, product research and creative design are closely intertwined, as the information gathered about a product determines which features can be faithfully demonstrated and how they should be presented. In this paper, we introduce AdProducer, the first end-to-end agentic framework for advertising video creation, transforming concise creative briefs into finished advertisements. Our multimodal agent autonomously researches products through web and image search and translates its findings into selling-point-driven storyboards that guide a frozen video generator. To achieve this, we develop a dedicated data construction pipeline that yields two training datasets, AdProducer-SFT and AdProducer-RL, and train the agent through supervised fine-tuning followed by reinforcement learning with joint optimization of storyboard- and video-level rewards. We further introduce AdCreation-Bench, comprising 305 advertising briefs across eight domains, with two complementary metrics: PG-Score for product correctness and creative execution, and Ad-Score for overall advertising effectiveness assessed by an independent judge. Experiments demonstrate that AdProducer improves the PG-Score of MiniMax H3 from 17.3 to 51.3 and generalizes to Veo 3.1 without additional training, improving its score from 28.0 to 35.9, with consistent gains in Ad-Score, VideoScore2, and human evaluations. Code, models, and data are released.

</details>


### [11] [PromptSMC: Enabling Particle Exploration in Flow Matching via LLM-Based Prompt Rejuvenation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31471)
*Wenyi Lian,Zheng Zhao,Thomas B. Schön*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 PromptSMC：在推理阶段用序贯蒙特卡洛（SMC）结合基于 LLM 的提示词"复新"，实现文本到图像流匹配模型的无梯度、奖励引导的提示-图像对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散/流匹配模型在推理时常常出现生成图像与原始提示词语义不一致的问题。现有改进手段多依赖额外噪声注入、奖励梯度反传或模型微调，代价高且可能破坏生成质量，因此需要一个无需训练、无需梯度的原则性推理期对齐框架。

Method: 以序贯蒙特卡洛作为采样框架，沿流匹配的扩散时间维度进行粒子探索。在共享中间隐状态的基础上复制粒子，用预训练 LLM 在语义保持约束下动态改写（rejuvenate）各粒子的提示词；改写后的提示诱导出不同的条件速度场，使粒子从同一中间状态探索不同生成轨迹。再用奖励模型评估每个粒子与原始提示的对齐度，并以其得分进行重采样。

Result: 在多个基准与多种骨干模型上的实验及消融表明，PromptSMC 能有效提升文本到图像流匹配的提示-图像对齐效果，且无需额外噪声注入、模型训练或奖励梯度。

Conclusion: 将基于提示的轨迹探索与奖励引导采样相结合，是在推理期实现忠实提示-图像对齐的可行且轻量方案；代码已开源。

Abstract: This paper is concerned with prompt-image alignment at inference time through exploration of generative trajectories and dynamical prompt rejuvenation. We leverage sequential Monte Carlo (SMC), to offer a gradient-free and principled framework for this purpose through reward-guided trajectory selection. More specifically, we introduce LLM-based prompt rejuvenation, allowing for efficient particle exploration in SMC samplers along the diffusion time in flow matching models. The proposed method, PromptSMC, retains the selected latent states and uses a pretrained LLM to dynamically revise the prompts of duplicated particles under semantic-preservation constraints. These revised prompts induce alternative conditional velocity fields, allowing particles to explore different continuations from a shared intermediate state. The method further uses a reward model to evaluate each particle's alignment with the original prompt, and the resulting scores guide particles through resampling. PromptSMC thus integrates prompt-based exploration with reward-guided sampling, achieving faithful prompt-image alignment without additional noise injection, model training, or reward gradients. Our experiments and ablation studies across multiple benchmarks and backbones demonstrate the effectiveness of the method for text-to-image flow matching. Code is available at https://github.com/shermanlian/PromptSMC.

</details>


### [12] [Which Way Is Up? Rotated Photos Silently Hurt Multi-View 3D Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31478)
*Yukun Shi,Minglun Gong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文系统测量了照片EXIF方向标签被忽略（竖拍横存）对五种多视图3D基础模型的相机位姿与点云估计的影响，发现少量旋转照片即显著降低精度，且失败是"静默"的——模型置信度无法可靠报警，主流官方图像加载器本身忽略该标签，测试的补救措施只能部分修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多视图3D基础模型（一次前向即可从照片集估计相机位姿与3D点）已成为主流范式，但真实数据中大量竖拍照片以横向存储、依赖EXIF方向标签旋转显示。该标签可能被忽略、丢失或错误，而现有评测与流程通常假设输入图像方向正确，这一被普遍忽视的输入污���风险缺乏量化评估。

Method: 在三个数据集上对五种多视图3D基础模型进行受控实验：人为注入旋转照片，量化相机位姿精度的退化；同时检验标准基准中已存在的横置照片是否产生同样的退化；分析模型置信度能否指示失败；并审计主流官方图像加载器对方向标签的处理；最后测试多种修复方案（如预处理校正、加载器修正等）并评估其覆盖范围。

Result: 少量旋转照片即使所有模型的相机位姿精度下降；标准基准中本就存在的横置照片造成同等退化；即便最鲁棒的模型仍有精度损失。失败具有隐蔽性：模型置信度不能可靠标记该错误，且多数官方图像加载器忽略方向标签。所测试的各项修复只能纠正问题的一部分。

Conclusion: 图像方向是影响多视图3D重建的可测量且被系统性忽视的失效来源。作者呼吁在重建流水线与基准评测中显式加入方向检查与标准化处理，并推动方向鲁棒性成为模型评测的标准维度。

Abstract: Multi-view 3D foundation models estimate camera poses and 3D points from a set of photos in one forward pass. Many cameras store a portrait shot sideways, with an orientation tag telling viewers how to turn it, and this tag can be ignored, lost or wrong. We measure how rotated photos affect five such models on three datasets. A few rotated photos lower camera pose accuracy for every model, and the sideways photos already in a standard benchmark do the same. Even the most robust model still loses accuracy. The failure is also silent: the models' confidence does not reliably flag it, and most official image loaders ignore the tag. The fixes we test each correct only part of the problem. These findings call for orientation checks in reconstruction pipelines and benchmarks.

</details>


### [13] [GDD: A Remote Sensing Image Deepfake Detection and Manipulation Localization Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31487)
*Jacob Arndt,Philipe Dias,Debvrat Varshney,Sudarshan Srinivasan,Dalton Lunga*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出地理空间深度伪造数据集GDD，聚焦遥感图像伪造检测与篡改定位，并引入分布外（OOD）评估协议，证明紧凑高质量数据集比大体积未精选基准具有更强的跨数据集泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥感领域图像真实性验证需求迫切，但生成式AI使合成遥感数据愈发逼真。现有RS深度伪造基准追求数据量而牺牲视觉真实性与多样性，且缺乏严格的OOD测试协议，难以评估真实场景鲁棒性。

Method: 构建GDD基准，涵盖多种现代生成模型与真实图像源，并包含扩散修复（inpainting）、拼接（splicing）和复制-移动（copy-move）三类篡改操作。设立由未见真实图像源与未见生成模型组成的OOD评估协议。在域内与跨数据集两种设定下评测5个检测器与2个定位模型。

Result: 在高容量未精选基准上训练的检测器会过拟合数据集特定伪影；而在紧凑的GDD上训练可获得更优的跨数据集泛化性能。评测同时揭示了当前SOTA方法的关键失效模式。

Conclusion: GDD及其评估协议为该安全攸关的遥感领域提供了高质量标准，用于开发与压力测试检测和定位模型，数据集已公开。

Abstract: Verifying image authenticity is a critical need for a variety of applications and decision-making in the remote sensing (RS) domain, yet it is becoming more challenging as advancements in generative AI enable producing increasingly realistic synthetic overhead data. However, existing RS deepfake benchmarks prioritize sheer volume at the expense of visual realism and diversity, while also lacking rigorous out-of-distribution (OOD) testing protocols to evaluate real-world robustness. To address this, we introduce the Geospatial Deepfakes Dataset (GDD), a benchmark for RS deepfake detection and manipulation localization. Rather than relying on uncurated, redundant synthetic generation, GDD comprises images across diverse modern generative models and real image sources, alongside manipulations from diffusion inpainting, splicing, and copy-move operations. Crucially, GDD establishes an OOD evaluation protocol for deepfake detection comprising unseen real image sources and generative models. We benchmark five deepfake detectors and two localization models across intra- and cross-dataset regimes and reveal that detectors trained on high-volume, uncurated benchmarks overfit to dataset-specific artifacts, whereas training on GDD yields superior cross-dataset generalization despite its compact scale. This benchmarking also exposes critical failure modes in current state-of-the-art methods. Combined, GDD and its evaluation protocol establish a high-quality standard for developing and stress-testing detection and localization models in this safety-critical RS domain. The dataset is publicly available.

</details>


### [14] [HyMTS: A Multiscale Tensor Factorization-Based Network for Hyperspectral Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31506)
*Lei Xu,Fahad Sohrab,Moncef Gabbouj,Mehmet Yamac*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HyMTS：以多尺度张量求和（MTS）分解算子为骨干的高光谱图像复原网络，将低秩先验直接嵌入网络算子中，结合因子化层与输入自适应层的统一模块，在去噪与超分辨任务上取得优越或相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高光谱图像具有强谱-空冗余，干净信号通常可由低维谱子空间与局部低秩空间结构表示。现有方法主要依赖稠密注意力或跨谱带特征混合，低秩先验多通过辅助损失或对重建输出的约束间接施加，未直接体现在网络算子中。

Method: 提出 HyMTS 网络，骨干基于 Multi-scale Tensorial Summation (MTS) 分解算子；堆叠统一模块，将因子化层（factorized layers）与输入自适应层（input-adaptive layers）耦合，以同时捕获谱维与空维的局部及全局依赖；低秩先验作为架构内在机制嵌入算子，而非依赖辅助损失或输出约束。

Result: 在高斯噪声去噪、真实噪声去噪及单图超分辨的标准基准上，不使用辅助数据的广泛实验表明，HyMTS 以高效计算达到优于或可比于多种最先进方法的表现。

Conclusion: 将低秩先验内嵌于分解型网络算子是高光谱复原的一种有效且高效的建模范式，可在无需辅助数据的情况下兼顾性能与效率。

Abstract: Hyperspectral image (HSI) restoration has been explored using deep learning techniques recently. Because hyperspectral images have strong spectral and spatial redundancy, the underlying clean signal is usually represented in a low-dimensional spectral subspace and locally low-rank spatial structures. Existing methods primarily explore dense attention or feature mixing across spectral bands. In this work, we propose HyMTS, a hyperspectral restoration network with a backbone built on the Multi-scale Tensorial Summation (MTS) factorization operator. HyMTS provides a natural architectural mechanism for exploiting a low-rank prior, embedded directly in the network operator rather than imposed via an auxiliary loss or a constraint on the reconstructed output. Specifically, HyMTS stacks a unified block that couples factorized layers with input-adaptive layers, capturing both local and global dependencies across the spectral and spatial dimensions. Extensive experiments without auxiliary data on standard benchmarks for Gaussian and real-noise denoising and single-image super-resolution show that HyMTS efficiently achieves superior or comparable performance to various state-of-the-art approaches.

</details>


### [15] [Synthetic Augmentation for Data-Scarce Object Detection: Allocation, Dose, and Filtering Across Six Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31515)
*João Félix,Atabak Dehban,Rodrigo Ventura*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文评估模块化合成增强流程，将"生成规划"与"生成后过滤"作为独立干预，在六个目标检测数据集上考察FLUX.1-Fill结合类别特定LoRA的效果；发现增强收益依赖数据集，过滤无稳定增益，且FID/KID/Precision/Recall/LPIPS等图像质量指标无法稳定反映检测器变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向非合作空间目标感知与多变工业产品检测的机器人视觉任务常缺乏充足标注图像，合成数据增强是潜在解法，但生成数据的效用及其筛选方式缺乏系统评估，且计算成本受限。

Method: 构建模块化合成增强流程，将生成规划（FLUX.1-Fill + 类别特定LoRA适配）与生成后过滤作为两项独立干预分别评估；在六个目标检测数据集上进行剂量效应实验，采用验证集选择增强方案，并将FID、KID、Precision、Recall、LPIPS与检测器性能变化进行对比。

Result: FLUX.1-Fill配合类别特定LoRA呈现数据集相关的剂量效应；经验证集选择的增强在四个数据集上提升mAP@50:95，在两个数据集上下降；生成后过滤未带来一致增益；FID、KID、Precision、Recall与LPIPS均无法稳定跟踪检测器性能变化。

Conclusion: 结果不支持"仅凭图像质量即可识别有用样本"的假设；作者主张建立标准化评估协议，并发展低成本的候选样本效用度量，以便在重训检测器前筛出有用样本，从而降低重复训练开销。

Abstract: Robotic perception for capturing uncooperative orbital objects and inspecting variable industrial products must often operate with limited labelled imagery. We examine a modular synthetic-augmentation pipeline, evaluating generation planning and post-generation filtering as separate interventions, with computational cost as a design constraint. Across six object-detection datasets, FLUX.1-Fill with class-specific LoRA adaptation produced dataset-dependent dose effects; the validation-selected augmentation improved mAP@50:95 on four datasets and reduced it on two. Filtering did not yield consistent gains, and FID, KID, precision, recall, and LPIPS did not consistently track detector changes. The results do not establish that image quality alone identifies useful samples. They motivate standardised evaluation and inexpensive candidate-utility measures that could identify useful samples before detector retraining, reducing repeated training cost.

</details>


### [16] [InitFree-SfM: When Initialization-Free Bundle Adjustment meets Learned Structure-from-Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31516)
*Simon Weber,Mateo de Mayo,Je Hyeong Hong,Carl Olsson,Daniel Cremers,Ronald Clark*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文指出免初始化BA中低目标值≠高重建精度，提出引入学习度量深度的完整流水线InitFree-SfM与深度增强OSE(dOSE)，在挑战性数据上显著优于现有InitFree BA并媲美成熟SfM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: InitFree BA（从随机初始化联合恢复位姿与结构）极具挑战；现有方法优化代理的Object Space Error目标，但低目标值不能保证精确重建，存在"优化—重建"鸿沟，且在现代SfM基准上精度不足，需提升其实用可行性。

Method: 构建InitFree-SfM完整流水线：学习对应关系+学习度量深度+深度一致的track构建+联合度量精化；在此框架下提出简单深度增强OSE(dOSE)，在OSE方向约束之外沿视线约束路标位置。

Result: 显著优于现有InitFree BA方法，在低重叠等挑战性图像集合上达到与成熟SfM流水线相当的精度。

Conclusion: 分析表明应弱化对代理目标的强调：几何约束在精确重建中起核心作用，OSE类优化提供进一步增益；度量深度可实质提升免初始化BA的实用性。

Abstract: Initialization-free bundle adjustment (InitFree BA) aims to jointly recover camera poses and scene structure without requiring an initial global estimate. It is a very challenging problem, especially compared to traditional BA where a good estimation is assumed. While recent methods optimize surrogate Object Space Error (OSE) objectives from random initialization, a low objective value does not necessarily yield an accurate reconstruction, revealing a gap between optimization and reconstruction. We show that existing InitFree BA pipelines struggle to produce accurate 3D reconstructions on modern SfM benchmarks, and demonstrate that incorporating learned metric depth substantially improves their practical feasibility. We introduce InitFree-SfM, a complete initialization-free SfM pipeline combining learned correspondences, metric depth, depth-consistent track construction, and joint metric refinement. Within this framework, we propose a simple depth-augmented OSE (dOSE), which complements the directional constraints of OSE by constraining landmark positions along their viewing rays. Our approach substantially outperforms existing InitFree BA methods and achieves competitive accuracy with established SfM pipelines, including on challenging low-overlap image collections. Importantly, our analysis challenges the prevailing emphasis on surrogate objectives in previous InitFree BA research, revealing that geometric constraints play a central role in accurate reconstruction, while OSE-based optimization provides further improvements.

</details>


### [17] [Your Codec, Your Generator: Idempotent Generative Compression without Retraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31520)
*Raz Haim,Assaf Shocher*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种可即插即用的图像压缩框架：用非学习操作包裹冻结编码器构造满射伪可逆网络，实现免重训练的自适应、循环一致重压缩，并在解码端可独立选择生成先验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预训练编解码器难以在不重训练/微调的情况下适配新用途，已编码图像的重压缩难以保持一致，且解码重建强绑定于编码器自带先验，无法在未来更换更强的生成模型。

Method: 以非学习操作包裹冻结编码器，使每个连续隐变量都有原像，得到满射伪可逆网络；由预训练解码器构造非线性右逆，满足 E'(D'(E'(x)))=E'(x)。为扩展到量化比特流，接收端搜索同时复现主符号与熵边信息的连续隐变量，并在确定性编码下精确校正，最终经真实编码路径校验；再将线性反投影 x'=x+A†(y−Ax) 推广为非线性形式，用独立选择的生成模型提议并强制满足传输码，通过调节起始噪声控制先验影响而不改变码。

Result: 首轮质量变化很小；在50次重压缩（含8位存储）下保持质量高于经训练的一致性编解码器；解码端可控制感知-失真权衡。

Conclusion: 将编解码器与其先验解耦，使接收端无需重训练或重新编码即可采用未来的生成模型。

Abstract: We introduce an image compression framework with three properties: (i) adaptation of pretrained codecs without retraining or fine-tuning, (ii) cycle-consistent recompression, even of already-encoded images, and (iii) decoding with independently chosen generative priors. We wrap the frozen encoder with non-learned operations so that every continuous latent has a preimage, yielding a surjective pseudo-invertible neural network. A nonlinear right inverse built from the pretrained decoder satisfies $E'(D'(E'(x)))=E'(x)$. To extend consistency to quantized bitstreams, the receiver searches for a continuous latent that reproduces both the transmitted main symbols and entropy side information. A successful search and an exact correction preserve both streams under deterministic coding; the final reconstruction is checked through the actual coding path. The encoder's pseudo-inverse enables a nonlinear counterpart of back-projection. In the linear case, back-projection has the closed-form solution $x'=x+A^\dagger(y-Ax)$, giving the closest reconstruction to $x$ satisfying $Ax'=y$. Our nonlinear formulation enforces the transmitted code on proposals from an independently chosen generative model. Adjusting the starting noise controls the prior's influence without changing that code. Experiments show small first-pass quality changes, higher retained quality than trained consistency codecs over 50 recompressions, also under 8-bit storage, and decoder-side control of the perception-distortion trade-off. Decoupling the codec from its prior lets the receiver adopt future generators without retraining or re-encoding.

</details>


### [18] [Fast Anatomy-Aware Diffeomorphic Augmentation for Brain MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31524)
*Tonmoy Hossain,Alexandra G. Roberts,Mark Olchanyi,Jonathan Williams Ramirez,Stefan Brandstätter,Juan Eugenio Iglesias*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BrainAug是一种无需群体影像数据的解剖感知数据增强框架，通过构图建模脑区空间依赖并采样相关区域体积变化，再转为微分同胚形变，生成解剖与外观独立变化的训练图像，在八个脑MRI数据集上提升分割泛化与配准表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑MRI训练数据有限，尤其针对特定分辨率或对比度的监督模型；传统随机非线性形变场虽简单，但无法刻画不同脑区形态变化之间的空间依赖关系。

Method: 构建编码脑区空间邻近性与双侧对应关系的解剖图，将其关系嵌入概率模型以采样相关联的区域体积变化，再把采样变化转化为保持拓扑的微分同胚变换；并与对比度无关的图像合成结合，实现解剖与外观的独立变化。

Result: 在八个脑MRI数据集上评估，分割模型在多样人群中的泛化性更强；配准实验显示在显著形态差异下对齐效果改善。

Conclusion: 解剖结构先验的可控形变增强无需大规模群体数据即可生成解剖协调的多样样本，是提升脑MRI分割与配准鲁棒性的有效手段。

Abstract: Data augmentation is essential for training deep learning models that are generalizable, particularly when training data are limited. In brain MRI, augmentation methods that synthesize anatomically diverse images without relying on large cohorts are particularly attractive, especially when training supervised models for scans with a specific resolution or MRI contrast. While conventional random nonlinear fields offer a simple means of achieving such diversity, they fail to capture the underlying spatial dependencies among morphological changes across brain regions. To address this limitation, we introduce BrainAug, an anatomy-aware augmentation framework that generates diverse, anatomically coordinated deformations without relying on population imaging data. BrainAug constructs an anatomical graph encoding spatial proximity and bilateral correspondence between brain regions, then incorporates these relationships into a probabilistic model designed to sample correlated regional volume changes. These sampled variations are translated into diffeomorphic transformations, enabling substantial structural changes while preserving anatomical topology. Combined with contrast-agnostic image synthesis, BrainAug generates training images with independently varying anatomy and appearance. Evaluations across eight brain MRI datasets demonstrate stronger segmentation generalization across diverse populations, while registration experiments show improved alignment under substantial morphological differences.

</details>


### [19] [Lit Up but Not Needed: Scoped Lesions Show Where Multimodal Mixture-of-Experts Depend on Their Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31576)
*Zhixuan Ge,Anqi Li,Kexin Geng,Wei Qiu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在三个 MoE 视觉语言模型中，3–12% 的专家为视觉专属专家，接收约四分之一图像 token 路由，但损伤（lesion）它们对性能的影响不超过随机同规模专家集；模型对专家的真实依赖主要集中在文本位置，唯一跨模型依赖图像位置专家的是图像内文字识别（OCR）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 认知神经科学表明"激活图不等于必要性图"：任务中活跃的区域未必是必需区域，只有损伤实验才能揭示必要性。现有 MoE 视觉语言模型天然附带激活图（路由器记录专家对图像/文本 token 的响应），近期工作据此读取"模态专属专家"，但缺乏必要性验证。本文据此提出对激活图结论做损伤式检验。

Method: 在 Qwen3-VL-30B-A3B、Kimi-VL-A3B、Gemma-4-26B-A4B 上统计模态专属专家（图像高路由、文本近乎为零）；对全部专属专家做损伤并与同规模随机专家集对照；进一步做位置受限损伤（仅在图像位置或仅在文本位置移除专家）以定位真实依赖；并用仅文本训练、负载均衡偏置、感知剥夺等分析专家来源与性质。

Result: 3–12% 专家为视觉专属，图像 token 路由速率达均匀分布 5 倍；Qwen3-VL 中其占全部图像 token 路由的四分之一，但全损仅使八项感知基准与自由 OCR 平均下降不足 1 个百分点，不高于随机集损伤。Qwen3-VL/Kimi-VL 的图像侧依赖为 2–3 点，Gemma-4 为 26 点；Qwen3-VL 在图像位置静默全部专家损失 3 点，文本位置则达 45 点，依赖集中于兼顾图像与文本的"两用专家"。图像内文字识别是所有模型中均需图像位置专家的能力。专属专家接收信息量大的 patch、输出规模正常、在感知剥夺下保持惰性，仅文本训练只在负载均衡强制时才招募它们；Kimi-VL 中多数专属专家由均衡偏置制造。

Conclusion: 模态专属专家的"专属"更多源于负载均衡而非功能必需；负载均衡恰在模型最不依赖的计算部分（图像位置）绘制模态图。专家选择、剪枝与跳算应以损伤结果而非激活图为依据。

Abstract: Cognitive neuroscience learned that an activation map is not a map of necessity. Regions that light up during a task are not always needed for it, and only lesions reveal which ones are. Mixture-of-experts (MoE) vision-language models come with an activation map for free, since the router records which experts respond to image and text tokens, and recent work reads modality-specialized experts off this map. We bring the lesion. In Qwen3-VL-30B-A3B, Kimi-VL-A3B and Gemma-4-26B-A4B, 3 to 12% of the experts are vision-exclusive: image tokens route to them at up to five times the uniform rate and text tokens almost never. In Qwen3-VL they receive a quarter of all image-token routing, and lesioning all of them costs less than one percentage point on average over eight perception benchmarks and free-form OCR, no more than lesioning a random set of the same size. In Kimi-VL and Gemma-4 the lesion also costs no more than a random set. Scoped lesions, which remove an expert set only at image positions or only at text positions, then show where the models do depend on their experts. In Qwen3-VL, silencing every expert at every image position costs 3 points, against 45 at text positions, where the dependence sits in dual-use experts that text-only tasks need as well. Across models the image-side dependence is 2 to 3 points in Qwen3-VL and Kimi-VL and 26 in Gemma-4. Reading text in images is the one ability that needs image-position experts in every model. The exclusive experts receive informative patches, produce outputs of ordinary size, and stay inert under sensory deprivation: text-only training recruits them only when load balancing forces it. In Kimi-VL the balancing bias itself manufactures most of them. Load balancing draws the modality map in the part of the computation the models depend on least. Expert selection, pruning and skipping should follow the lesion, not the map.

</details>


### [20] [Spec$^2$R: Recovering Emergent Properties in Supervised Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31589)
*Adam Wróbel,Jacek Tabor,Bartosz Zieliński,Marcin Przewięźlikowski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文通过谱分析揭示监督ViT的MLP层引入高频扰动破坏patch表示的空间组织，并提出轻量谱正则化方法Spec$^2$R恢复物体对齐的表示与注意力图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督ViT具有物体对齐的patch表示与注意力图等涌现特性，而监督ViT即使使用强数据增强也普遍缺失这些性质。理解这一差异的来源对于解释并控制物体级结构至关重要。

Method: 对监督与自监督模型进行谱分析，定位到监督ViT的MLP层会向patch表示引入高频变化、破坏其空间组织；据此提出Spec$^2$R，一种轻量级谱正则化方法，仅对预训练监督ViT的MLP层进行微调。

Result: Spec$^2$R恢复了物体对齐的patch表示与注意力图，同时提升了对抗鲁棒性，并保持了有竞争力的分类精度。

Conclusion: 监督ViT缺失物体级结构的根源在于MLP层的高频扰动；通过针对MLP层的谱正则化微调，可在不牺牲分类性能的前提下获得类似自监督ViT的结构化表示，并提供鲁棒性收益。

Abstract: Vision Transformers (ViTs) have achieved strong performance across computer vision tasks, but the quality of their learned representations differs drastically depending on the training paradigm. While self-supervised ViTs often demonstrate emergent properties, such as object-aligned patch representations and attention maps, supervised ViTs typically lack these properties, even when trained with strong data augmentations. Understanding the origin of this difference is important for explaining and controlling the object-level structure. To investigate it, we perform a spectral analysis of supervised and self-supervised models, and identify a key distinction: MLP layers in supervised ViTs introduce high-frequency variations into patch representations, disrupting their spatial organization. Motivated by this finding, we propose Spec$^2$R, a lightweight spectral regularization method that fine-tunes the MLP layers of pretrained supervised ViTs. Spec$^2$R recovers object-aligned patch representations and attention maps, while improving adversarial robustness and preserving competitive classification accuracy.

</details>


### [21] [GoldiBench: Right Tools, Wrong Amount -- Benchmarking Agents on Effect Judgement in Professional Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31590)
*Wang Yang,Xinpeng Li,Yanyan Zhang,Chaoda Song,Shouren Wang,Debargha Ganguly,Vikash Singh,Chuang Ma,Xiaoran Cai,Kai Ye,Xiaotian Han,Vipin Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出GoldiBench——500个跨5款专业软件（darktable、RawTherapee、GIMP、Inkscape、Blender）的编辑基准，用"START图像+TARGET图像"把编辑"量"固定为已知目标，从而拆解"操作能力"与"效果判断能力"。结果显示：给定工具和数值时模型近乎全通过，但只给工具时最好的模型仅通过19%（程序）/33%（GUI），且规模扩展无法弥补；以数值形式给出TARGET−START差异可使通过率翻倍以上，而以图像形式呈现则对开源模型无效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实编辑需求描述的是"想要的效果"而非"操作步骤"，因此智能体必须自行判断每个工具需要多大强度。现有基准无法测量这一能力：任务要么直接给出工具与数值，要么让"量"开放并用VLM评判，而VLM评判会让半数强度乃至三倍强度的结果有一半以上被判通过，度量失真。

Method: 构建GoldiBench，含5款专业应用中的500个编辑任务，每个任务由一段隐藏程序渲染出TARGET，并配有START图像，使"编辑量"由已知目标唯一确定。智能体可通过编写程序或操作GUI完成任务；其编辑结果被重新渲染并与TARGET比对。同一任务以三种形式呈现：给出工具+数值、仅给工具、仅给目标，从而分离"操作执行"与"效果量判断"。评测覆盖25个开源、GUI型与闭源模型。

Result: 程序化方式下，从4B模型起，在被告知数值时操作成功率可达99–100%；但仅给工具时性能骤降：最强模型在程序模式下通过19%，在GUI模式下通过33%，且模型规模扩大不能缩小差距。软件手册帮助甚微；直接给出实测的TARGET−START数值差异可使通过率提高一倍以上，但将同一差异以图像形式呈现对开源模型无增益。在允许四次渲染时，智能体几乎总在第一次后就停止；若强制进行对比，强模型通过率翻倍。

Conclusion: 智能体"会用工具"，但既不主动读取、也不校验编辑量。GoldiBench将"量"的判断从操作执行中剥离出来，揭示了当前模型在真实编辑需求下的核心短板，并指出以显式数值差异与强制渲染对比作为改进方向。

Abstract: Real editing requests describe a look, not the steps to reach it, so an agent that serves them must judge how much of each tool the look takes. Existing benchmarks do not measure this: their tasks either state the tools and values, or leave the amount open and are scored by VLM judges that pass more than half of half- and triple-strength results. We introduce GoldiBench, a benchmark of 500 editing tasks in five professional applications (darktable, RawTherapee, GIMP, Inkscape, Blender), solvable by writing a program or by operating the GUI. Each task pairs a START image with a TARGET rendered from a hidden program, so the amount is fixed by a known target; the agent's edit is re-rendered and compared with TARGET, and the same task is posed with the tools and values, the tools only, or the goal only, separating operation from effect judgement. Across 25 open, GUI and closed models, agents operate well (99-100% with programs from 4B up when told the values) but fall off a cliff when they must judge the amount: given only the tools, the best model passes 19% of the tasks with programs and 33% through the GUI, and scale does not close the gap. Manuals barely help; the measured TARGET-START difference more than doubles the pass rate, but the same difference shown as an image does not for open models. Allowed four renders, agents almost always stop after the first; forced to compare, the strong models double their pass rate. Agents know the tools, but neither read nor check the amount.

</details>


### [22] [RePT: 3D Geometry Emerges from 2D Relational Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31601)
*Xize Duan,Binke Hou,Xingqiao Huang,Xinyuan Guan,Jiaxin Huang,Kaiyuan Hu,Zipeng Sun,Hong Kang,Meng Bi,Xue Liu,Yili Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 RePT，通过对 2D 观测间不同物理点的关系进行跨视角点预测学习，无需 3D 预测目标，即可在表征中自发形成反映物理 3D 位移的几何组织（模长、角度、邻域）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空间表征学习多依赖显式预测几何量，而学习到的表征内部如何组织物理几何仍不清楚。作者追问：在没有 3D 预测目标的情况下，3D 结构能否仅从 2D 观测间的关系学习中涌现。

Method: 引入 RePT：以跨视角 2D 点预测为任务，学习不同物理点之间的紧凑关系（关系空间）。通过控制实验对比"异点关系预测"与"同点对应学习"，并考察加性关系、自适应场景上下文等因素的作用。

Result: 关系空间呈现几何组织：模长、角度、邻域反映真实 3D 位移。仅在 16 个场景上拟合的单一线性读出器，在 39 个留出场景上免重拟合即恢复位移长度与角度（Pearson r = 0.72 与 0.78）。ARKitScenes 训练的检查点在 ScanNet++ 上仍保持较强几何组织，显示泛化性。控制实验表明异点关系预测持续优于同点对应学习；该组织依赖加性关系与自适应场景上下文，且更高的点迁移精度本身并不带来更强的几何组织。

Conclusion: 3D 几何不必作为显式预测目标，而可作为"学习关联 2D 观测"所得表征的结构属性而涌现；关系预测是通向空间智能所需表征的一条互补路径。

Abstract: Learning spatial representations often relies on explicitly predicting geometric quantities, but how physical geometry is organized within learned representations remains less understood. We ask whether 3D structure can emerge from learning relations across 2D observations, without 3D prediction targets. We introduce RePT, which learns compact relations between distinct physical points through cross-view 2D point prediction. The learned relation space develops a geometric organization in which magnitudes, angles, and neighborhoods reflect physical 3D displacement. A single linear readout fitted on 16 scenes recovers displacement length and angle on 39 held-out scenes without refitting (Pearson r = 0.72 and 0.78). The ARKitScenes-trained checkpoint also retains strong geometric organization on ScanNet++, showcasing generalizability. In controlled comparisons, distinct-point relational prediction consistently yields stronger geometric organization than matched same-point correspondence learning. This organization depends on additive relations and adaptive scene context, and higher point-transfer accuracy does not by itself produce stronger organization. More broadly, our results suggest that 3D geometry need not be an explicit prediction target and can instead emerge as a structural property of representations trained to relate 2D observations, pointing to relational prediction as a complementary route to learning the representations that spatial intelligence builds on.

</details>


### [23] [Adapt, Label, Distill: Foundation-Model Supervision for Efficient Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31604)
*Zhengye Yang,Anthony Wang,Yuyang Li,Mehmet Kerem Turkcan,Beizhan Yan,Zoran Kostic*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 adapt-label-distill 流水线：用少量目标域标注微调 SAM3 作为教师，为无标注图像生成伪标签，并以置信度引导方式蒸馏到紧凑的 YOLOv11 学生模型，实现专业化且实时的目标检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标检测器向专门领域迁移效果差，这些领域框标注稀缺，同时部署要求模型紧凑、实时。视觉基础模型可提供离线监督，但其零样本预测与目标域对齐不佳；因此需要一种用有限标注把基础模型专业化、再把监督迁移到小模型的方案。

Method: 三阶段流水线：(1) 在少量目标域标注子集上适配 SAM3 得到领域特化的教师；(2) 用该教师为其余无标注图像生成伪标签；(3) 以置信度引导的监督训练 YOLOv11 学生。具体机制包括：教师置信度对伪标签损失加权；zero-gating 在标签分配阶段保留高不确定性假设但不施加其对应训练损失。

Result: 在六个目标域、三种学生容量上实验：部分领域显著提升，部分领域迁移有限或为负。共同硬标签训练配方下，教师对比支持选用 SAM3；与 EfficientTeacher 适配的对比提供反对在线 SSOD 基线的额外证据。YOLOv11m 上较 STAC 式自训练最高提升 15.3 AP，Garbage 达 51.6 AP（全监督为 54.1 AP）。Jetson AGX Orin 上学生达 188 FPS，SAM3 为 2.9 FPS。

Conclusion: 以离线方式获取基础模型的监督、再蒸馏至轻量检测器，可获得近乎全监督的精度而无需承担教师模型的部署开销；迁移收益具有领域依赖性，需针对目标域评估。

Abstract: Object detectors often transfer poorly to specialized domains, where box annotations are scarce and deployment requires compact, real-time models. Large vision foundation models can provide offline supervision, but their zero-shot predictions may be poorly aligned with the target domain. We present an adapt-label-distill pipeline that uses limited target annotations to specialize a foundation teacher and transfer its supervision to compact detectors. We adapt SAM3 on the labeled subset, generate pseudo-labels for the remaining unlabeled images, and train YOLOv11 students with confidence-guided supervision. Teacher confidence weights pseudo-label losses, while zero-gating retains uncertain hypotheses during assignment without applying their associated training losses. Experiments across six target domains and three student capacities show substantial gains in several domains, while revealing limited or negative transfer in others. Teacher comparisons under a common hard-label training recipe support the choice of SAM3, and comparisons with an EfficientTeacher adaptation provide additional evidence against an online SSOD baseline. With YOLOv11m, our pipeline improves over STAC-style self-training by up to 15.3 AP and reaches 51.6 AP on Garbage, compared with 54.1 under full supervision. On NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin, the deployed student achieves 188 FPS versus 2.9 FPS for SAM3, benefiting from foundation-model supervision without incurring the teacher's deployment cost. Code is available at https://github.com/zk2172-columbia/ProjectAdaptLabelDistill_ObjectDetection.

</details>


### [24] [What You See Is What You Certify: Concept-Based Certificates for Unseen Compositions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31627)
*Phuc Linh Ngo,Thang Nguyen Duc,Truong Thanh Hung Nguyen,Hung Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究概念瓶颈模型的决策何时能作为"证书"，揭示联合概念瓶颈/概念嵌入模型在未见组合上93-94%的错误认证率，并将其归因于跨概念捷径；提出最少干预的参考设计OCVE（每次前向仅从局部裁剪读取单个部件），在FunnyBirds未见类上以0.4%风险认证52%图像，风险显著低于各类基线，但在已见类上无收益且隔离读取牺牲精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概念模型通过显式本体解码概念来为决策提供解释，但概念预测存在标签信息泄漏、忽视局部性、对新属性组合缺乏接地等问题，导致决策即使满足本体约束仍可能是错的。作者因此追问：概念解码的决策在什么条件下才能充当可信证书。核心动机是把本体视为纠错码，刻画合格认证所需的证据条件。

Method: 以"本体作为错误检测码"为框架进行理论分析，给出认证的三个必要条件：可见证据须排除所有竞争类别、未见部件须被如实报告为未见、罕见误读不能由共享捷径驱动。据此提出最小参考设计OCVE：每个部件从独立局部裁剪中单独前向读取，避免部件间信息混合。在FunnyBirds（未见类）、CUB（未见物种）、真实交通标志上与联合概念瓶颈模型、概念嵌入模型、mask-pooled特征、全图部件读取器、属性原型模型及同意部件计数解码器等基线对比，并以认证覆盖率-风险曲线评估。

Result: 标准训练的联合模型与嵌入模型在未见组合上93-94%概率认证错误类别（仅用可见部件并由定位器门控的联合模型为60%），部件的错误更频繁复制共现取值（跨概念捷径）。OCVE在FunnyBirds未见类上以0.4%风险认证52%图像，mask-pooled特征为14%、全图部件读取器为33%（训练与门控一致时风险5.6%），零样本精度相近；最佳基线（含校准或限未见类）与仅统计同意部件的解码器风险仍高3-8倍。CUB上隔离区域读取器精度63.1%高于属性原型模型55.3%，认证未见物种风险约低6个百分点，比同关键点框内池化特征低约10个百分点。真实交通标志上认证风险由99.8%降至67%，仍过高，剩余错误多为图形符号误读。

Conclusion: 概念解码的正确性并不等于可认证性：认证需要可见证据排他、未见部件如实报告、误读不共享捷径，而标准联合训练系统性地违反后两点。OCVE表明隔离读取是满足这些条件的最小有效设计，可大幅降低未见组合上的认证风险；但隔离带来精度代价，且在已见类上本体相对公平训练的黑箱无额外收益，真实场景风险仍偏高。因此概念模型作为证书应被限定在组合泛化、高风险可弃权场景，并与隔离证据机制配合。

Abstract: Concept-based models justify a decision by concepts they claim to see, decoded through an explicit ontology. Yet concept predictions are known to leak label information, ignore locality, and be poorly grounded on new attribute combinations. A decision decoded from such concepts can satisfy the ontology and still be wrong, so we ask when it can serve as a certificate. On unseen combinations of familiar parts, a joint concept bottleneck model and a concept embedding model, trained as is standard, certify wrong classes 93-94% of the time despite a correct ontology (60% for a joint model trained only on visible parts and gated by a localizer). The joint model's part errors copy co-occurring values more often than chance (cross-concept shortcutting). Treating the ontology as an error-detecting code, we show that a certificate needs visible evidence excluding every rival, unseen parts reported as such, and rare misreads not driven by a shared shortcut. A minimal reference design, OCVE, reads each part from a local crop in its own forward pass. On unseen FunnyBirds classes, it certifies 52% of images at 0.4% risk, against 14% for mask-pooled features and 33% for full-image part readers (5.6% when trained and gated alike), at similar zero-shot accuracy. The best baselines, even calibrated or restricted to unseen classes, and a decoder counting only agreeing parts remain about 3-8 times riskier. On CUB, isolated region readers are more accurate than an attribute-prototype model (63.1% vs. 55.3%) and certify unseen species at about 6 points less risk than it and about 10 points less than features pooled in the same keypoint boxes. On real traffic signs, isolated readers lower certified risk from 99.8% to 67%, which is still far too high; most remaining errors are misread pictograms. On seen classes the ontology brings no gain over a fairly trained black box, and isolation costs accuracy.

</details>


### [25] [MSW: a Multi-Scale Sliced Wasserstein Distance Between Image Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31647)
*Youssef Haouchat,Stanislas Ducotterd,Bassam El Rawas,Michael Unser*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出多尺度切片Wasserstein（MSW）距离，在像素空间用固定DCT滤波器组与Wasserstein距离比较图像分布，并配以校准的精确两样本检验；相比依赖预训练网络的潜空间度量，MSW对更小扰动更敏感、所需样本更少，同时也能检测语义变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像分布度量大多在预训练网络的潜空间比较两个图像集，但潜空间对简单扰动近似不变，导致这些度量对细微扰动不敏感、需要大量图像。作者希望构建一个无需训练网络、在像素空间操作、既敏感又高效的分布距离度量。

Method: 在多个尺度上用固定的离散余弦（DCT）滤波器组对图像做卷积，对所有位置做池化，得到一维响应分布，再用Wasserstein距离比较这些一维分布，形成MSW距离；并搭配给出校准p值的精确两样本检验。

Result: MSW能检测到比潜空间度量更小的扰动，且所需图像数量远少；不使用学习特征仍能检测图像分布的语义变化；实验中MSW达到或超过现有基于Wasserstein的度量。

Conclusion: 像素空间的多尺度切片Wasserstein距离是一种无需训练、敏感度高、样本效率好且带校准统计检验的图像分布度量，可作为潜空间度量的有效替代。

Abstract: We propose the multi-scale sliced Wasserstein (MSW) distance between image distributions, which operates in pixel space and requires no trained network. MSW convolves images with a fixed bank of discrete cosine filters at several scales, pools the responses over all positions, and compares the resulting one-dimensional distributions using the Wasserstein distance. We pair MSW with an exact two-sample test that gives calibrated p-values. Most metrics compare two image sets in the latent space of a pretrained network. We show that these metrics are insensitive to simple perturbations of the images, because the latent space is nearly invariant to them. In contrast, MSW detects smaller perturbations than latent-space metrics and needs far fewer images to do so. Although it uses no learned features, it still detects semantic changes of the image distribution. Finally, MSW matches or outperforms existing Wasserstein-based metrics in our experiments.

</details>


### [26] [Anchoring VAE Fine-Tuning to Its Latent Diffusion Prior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31651)
*Rafael Bischof,Dominik Narnhofer,Julius Erbach,Michael Kraus,Konrad Schindler,Bernd Bickel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DPA：给VAE微调加一个score-distillation锚定正则项，把latent拉向预训练扩散模型能准确去噪的高密度流形，在深度、MRI、风场、法向四类任务上优于冻结VAE/仅解码器微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有做法冻结VAE编码器以保持latent分布不被破坏，但冻结编码器难以捕捉非RGB输入中的显著细节，且受限于三通道，无法适配目标域与RGB先验分布之间的偏移。

Method: 提出Diffusion Prior Anchoring（DPA），作为VAE微调时的正则化器；采用score-distillation式更新引导latents趋向预训练扩散模型能够高保真去噪的高密度流形，从而在放开编码器的同时约束分布漂移。

Result: 在FLUX 3、LTX-Video、Wan2.2三个图像/视频模型与四个任务（单目深度、MRI重建、风场模拟、表面法向）上验证：相对冻结VAE误差降低7.5%，相对仅解码器微调降低4.9%，相对无锚定VAE微调降低4.0%；增益随目标数据偏离RGB分布而增大（深度3.3%→复数MRI 7.2%）；可推广至剪枝与多通道VAE，四通道VAE（深度+法向）下深度误差降低9.3%。

Conclusion: DPA说明VAE编码器不必冻结，通过扩散先验锚定即可在保持latent可用性的同时吸收非RGB域的细节信息，是一种模型无关、可扩展到改造型VAE的通用微调正则化方案。

Abstract: Pretrained latent diffusion models are effective priors for transfer learning, from geometry estimation to physics simulation. Existing approaches fine-tune the diffusion backbone but freeze the encoder of the VAE to preserve the latent distribution. However, a frozen encoder may miss salient details in non-RGB inputs and is limited to three channels. We introduce Diffusion Prior Anchoring (DPA), a regularizer applied during VAE fine-tuning that uses a score-distillation update to steer latents toward the high-density manifold on which the pretrained diffusion model denoises accurately. We validate DPA across three image and video models (FLUX 3, LTX-Video, Wan2.2) and four tasks: monocular depth estimation, MRI reconstruction, wind-flow simulation, and surface normal estimation, where DPA lowers the prediction error by 7.5% relative to the frozen VAE, 4.9% to decoder-only fine-tuning, and 4.0% to unanchored VAE fine-tuning. Its gain grows the further the target data lies from the RGB training distribution, from 3.3% on monocular depth estimation to 7.2% on complex-valued MRI. DPA also applies to modified VAEs, including pruned and multi-channel variants. With a four-channel VAE that encodes depth and surface normals, it lowers depth estimation error by 9.3%.

</details>


### [27] [LineEX: Data Extraction from Scientific Line Charts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31664)
*VP Shivasankaran,Muhammad Yusuf Hassan,Mayank Singh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LINEEX 是一个从科学折线图中提取数据的系统，通过改造现有视觉Transformer与姿态检测方法并结合新损失函数，在最大规模（430K张）合成折线图数据集上显著超越现有SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学折线图中蕴含数据，但缺乏高效的自动数据提取手段；现有SOTA基线在折线图数据提取任务上性能不足，且缺少大规模训练数据与更优的损失设计。

Method: 提出 LINEEX：对现有视觉Transformer与姿态检测（pose detection）方法进行适配改造用于折线图数据提取；提出一种新的损失函数；合成构建目前最大规模的折线图数据集（430K图像）；代码开源。

Result: 相较现有SOTA基线取得显著性能提升；新损失函数相较已有损失函数表现出有效性；构建了430K图像的规模最大合成折线图数据集。

Conclusion: 将视觉Transformer与姿态检测范式迁移至折线图数据提取是有效的，配合专门设计的损失函数与大规模合成数据可显著提升性能；数据集与代码公开以促进后续研究。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce LINEEX that extracts data from scientific line charts. We adapt existing vision transformers and pose detection methods and showcase significant performance gains over existing SOTA baselines. We also propose a new loss function and present its effectiveness against existing loss functions. In addition, we synthetically created the largest line chart dataset comprising 430K images. The code is available at https://github.com/Shiva-sankaran/LineEX.

</details>


### [28] [A Piecewise-Linear Activation Function for Implicit Neural Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31666)
*Shahd Hekal,Joram Kim,Jeova Farias Sales Rocha Neto*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出JaSIN——无超越运算的有界分段线性激活族，介于ReLU与分段线性正弦之间，通过消融揭示有界值域、单峰、居中与振荡数各自对INR重建的贡献，并利用"可证不活跃单元"实现无损剪枝。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ReLU坐标网络在隐式神经表示（INR）任务上表现差，但SIREN、Gabor等有效激活均脱离了分段线性函数类，无法定位ReLU究竟缺少何种性质。

Method: 设计JaSIN族：有界、分段线性、仅用基本运算（无sin等超越函数）的激活，可在ReLU与分段线性正弦间连续插值，并逐项控制有界性、单峰性、居中性和振荡次数，做递进式消融分析。

Result: 有界值域使重建PSNR平均+0.06 dB，单峰+3.29 dB，峰居中再+3.64 dB，振荡数再+6.30 dB，使ReLU在Kodak上从24.0提升到37.3 dB，在11种激活中排第三，距SOTA差1.3 dB；但振荡超过4次后重建崩溃。此外，因INR在评估网格上拟合，预激活未触及激活支撑的单元可证在定义域内恒不活跃，无需微调即可无损删除，最多剪掉84%参数且dB无损、解码快3.2倍。

Conclusion: ReLU在INR中的性能缺陷可归因于有界性、单峰/居中形状与振荡能力等具体性质；JaSIN以纯分段线性方式复现其中大部分收益，同时提供基于支撑判定的可证明无损剪枝与加速。振荡数存在约4次的上限，为后续设计提供约束。

Abstract: Coordinate networks with ReLU activations perform poorly on implicit neural representation tasks, and the activations that address this, such as sinusoids and Gabor wavelets, all leave the piecewise-linear function class, so it remains unclear which property of ReLU is at fault. We introduce JaSIN, a family of bounded piecewise-linear activations that interpolates between ReLU and a piecewise-linear sinusoid without any the use of transcendental operation. Our results show that a bounded range improves image reconstruction PSNR on average by 0.06 dB, a single bump 3.29 dB, and centering that bump a further 3.64 dB. The oscillation count improves 6.30 dB more, raising ReLU from 24.0 to 37.3 dB on Kodak images, placing third over eleven activations, within 1.3 dB of the state-of-the-art, though beyond four oscillations the reconstruction collapses. Because an INR is fit on the grid on which it is evaluated, a unit whose pre-activations never reach the support of its activation is certifiably inactive over the entire domain; deleting such units is lossless without fine-tuning, removing up to 84% of parameters at zero loss in performance (dB) and decoding 3.2 times faster. Reference code and demos are available at https://github.com/jeovafarias/jasin.

</details>


### [29] [Medical Image Segmentation Is Now Just Another Coding Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31673)
*Shuheng Cao,Ruiqi Chen,Qizhen Lan,Edward Wang,Xiyun Hu,Yilei Wang,Juqy Chen,Weilin Huang,Anhong Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 医疗图像分割已可被现成的编程智能体当作普通编码任务自动完成；其自建分割器在2D任务上可达GPU训练nnU-Net的99% Dice，进一步增益的关键瓶颈是无参考质量估计（"质量盲区"）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期以来每个新分割任务都依赖专家、专用架构与专用框架（如nnU-Net）。作者希望检验：仅凭任务描述、标注数据和一台CPU（无框架、无预训练权重、无人工介入），通用编程智能体能否自主构建分割器，并探究其自我改进的上限与解锁条件。

Method: 四个现成编程智能体在无分割框架、无预训练权重、无人参与的CPU条件下，跨四种成像模态、13个任务自主编写分割程序。自改进阶段：智能体在未见过病例上无参考标签，改为检视自身预测的可视化渲染结果来迭代修订；另设"告知每次尝试真实Dice"的对照条件，比较两种质量信号的作用。

Result: 数小时内完成13个任务；9个2D任务上最佳程序达到GPU训练nnU-Net的99% Dice。仅靠可视化自检，最差十分之一病例提升0.09 Dice，HD95降低近四分之一；告知真实Dice后，14对匹配尝试全部提升，失败掩膜挽救率由30%升至85%，平均超越GPU训练的nnU-Net，且是"生成更好掩膜"而非"在多候选中挑选"。

Conclusion: "构建与修复"能力智能体已具备，但缺少可调用的质量信号：可视化检查、更多迭代轮次、智能体自训练的质量估计器均无法提供，作者称为"质量盲区"。下一步突破不在新的分割模型，而在编程智能体可调用的无参考质量估计。

Abstract: For decades, every new medical image segmentation task has needed an expert, a dedicated architecture, and a framework such as nnU-Net. This is no longer the case: medical image segmentation is now just another coding task. Given only a task description, labeled data, and a CPU (with no segmentation framework, no pretrained weights, and no human in the loop), four off-the-shelf coding agents built segmenters for 13 tasks across four imaging modalities within hours, an ability that has emerged rather than been built. On the nine 2D tasks, their best programs reach 99% of the Dice of a GPU-trained nnU-Net. Like any code, a segmenter improves by being revised and checked, so we ask how far the same agents can improve their own segmentations and what unlocks further gains. On unseen cases there is no reference, so the agents inspect renderings of their own predictions: this alone lifts the worst tenth of cases by 0.09 Dice and cuts boundary error (HD95) by almost a quarter. Far more is within reach. Told the true Dice of each attempt, the same agents improve in all 14 matched pairs, rescue 85% of failed masks instead of 30%, and surpass the GPU-trained nnU-Net on average, by producing better masks rather than by choosing among them. What unlocks these gains is a quality signal, which neither visual inspection, nor more rounds, nor a quality estimator trained by the agents themselves yet supplies: we call this the quality blind spot. The ability to build and to repair is already present; the next advance will come not from another segmentation model, but from reference-free quality estimation that coding agents can call.

</details>


### [30] [GeoMVGen: Joint Appearance-Geometry Modeling for Multi-View Video Generation in Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31689)
*Qiaoqiao Jia,Guile Wu,Dongfeng Bai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoMVGen：面向机器人操作的多视角RGB与点图联合视频生成框架


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作需要预测交互随时间的演化，涵盖外观与几何，并在多相机下保持跨视角3D一致性与动作/相机可控性。现有视频生成模型缺乏显式几何建模，而几何感知与多视角生成方法在多视角、时序一致的外观-几何联合生成上仍具挑战。

Method: 基于视频扩散Transformer，采用模态特定适配模块分别建模外观与几何特性，并通过跨模态残差连接进行信息交互；引入跨视角投影机制，配合点图一致性与几何基础模型引导的特征对齐两类互补几何监督。

Result: 在包含仿真与真实机器人操作场景的4D视频生成数据集上，GeoMVGen在视频质量、几何精度与跨视角一致性方面优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 联合多视角RGB与点图生成可有效实现外观时序一致、几何结构跨视角一致且可控的机器人操作场景预测。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation requires predicting how robot-object interactions evolve over time, encompassing both visual appearance and scene geometry. In multi-camera settings, such predictions need to capture the evolving 3D scene consistently across viewpoints, while supporting controllable generation under different robot actions and camera configurations. Existing video generation models provide strong priors for visual appearance and motion but typically lack explicit geometric modeling. Meanwhile, recent advances in geometry-aware and multi-view generation have explored joint appearance-geometry modeling and cross-view consistency, yet generating temporally coherent appearance and geometry across multiple viewpoints under robotic manipulation conditions remains challenging. In this work, we propose GeoMVGen, a framework for joint multi-view RGB and pointmap video generation in robotic manipulation. Built on a video diffusion transformer, our framework employs modality-specific adaptation modules to capture the distinct characteristics of appearance and geometry, while using cross-modal residual connections to facilitate information exchange between the two modalities. To establish geometric correspondences across viewpoints, we further introduce a cross-view projection mechanism with complementary geometric supervisions, combining pointmap consistency with geometry foundation model-guided feature alignment. These designs enable controllable generation of temporally evolving multi-view observations with coherent appearance and geometrically consistent scene structures. Extensive experiments on 4D video generation dataset comprising simulated and real-world robotic manipulation scenarios demonstrate that GeoMVGen outperforms state-of-the-art methods in video quality, geometric accuracy, and cross-view consistency.

</details>


### [31] [GRUN: Generalized Retinex Unfolding Network for Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31690)
*Rihan Zhang,Fengyang Xiao,Yuhan Chen,Chunming He,Sina Farsiu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出广义Retinex公式，将光照-反射分解推广为"内容-环境"因子分解先验，并构建MAP引导的深度展开网络GRUN与退化感知耦合正则DCR，在七类复原任务上以同一架构取得有竞争力结果，且该设计可跨架构集成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像复原需同时应对多样退化并保持场景结构与细节。经典Retinex的光照-反射分解提供了有益先验，但其假设（干净反射+平滑光照）难以直接刻画复杂退化与环境侧变化，因此需要将其推广为更一般的分解先验。

Method: 1) 广义Retinex公式：把图像分解为"内容主导响应（广义反射，刻画场景属性）"与"环境侧响应（广义光照，编码与退化相关的任务变化）"两个学习因子；2) GRUN：MAP引导的深度展开网络，在K个阶段交替进行反射复原、光照估计与渐进式退化推断，并使用退化条件化近端算子；3) DCR正则：结合互信息最小化、梯度去相关与尺度锚定，促使两因子表示互补。

Result: 在去噪、去雾、去雨、低光照增强、去模糊、水下增强、逆光增强共七项任务上，使用同一架构、仅替换任务权重，在多个基准上取得有竞争力（competitive）的结果；广义Retinex设计还可嵌入现有复原方法，验证跨架构适用性。

Conclusion: 经典Retinex先验经推广后可成为统一、可解释且具迁移性的复原范式：内容-环境因子分解配合MAP展开与耦合正则，实现了单架构多任务复原，并具备作为通用插件嵌入其他方法的潜力。

Abstract: Image restoration remains a fundamental challenge in low-level vision, requiring methods that handle diverse degradations while preserving scene structures and details. We revisit classical Retinex theory and propose a Generalized Retinex formulation that extends the illumination-reflectance decomposition into a Retinex-inspired content-environment factorization prior for image restoration. The key idea is to restore images using two learned factors: a content-dominant response (generalized reflectance) capturing scene properties and an environment-side response (generalized illumination) encoding task-dependent variations associated with the observed degradations. Based on this formulation, we develop GRUN (Generalized Retinex Unfolding Network), a MAP-guided deep unfolding framework that alternates reflectance restoration, illumination estimation, and progressive degradation inference across K stages with degradation-conditioned proximal operators. We further introduce a Degradation-aware Coupling Regularizer (DCR) that combines mutual information minimization, gradient decorrelation, and scale anchoring to promote complementary content-environment factor representations. Experiments on seven image restoration tasks, including denoising, dehazing, deraining, low-light enhancement, deblurring, underwater enhancement, and backlit enhancement, demonstrate competitive results on multiple benchmarks using the same architecture with task-specific weights. Moreover, the proposed Generalized Retinex design can be integrated into existing restoration methods, demonstrating its cross-architecture applicability. Code will be released.

</details>


### [32] [E-Waste-Bench: A Large-Scale Benchmark for Visual Triage of Data-Center Hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31703)
*Nader Lobandi,Mohammad Mehdi Hosseini,Lombe Chileshe,Mohammad Mahoor*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出E-Waste-Bench：32.8万张带类别、粒度、状态与印刷文本标注的电子废弃物图像基准，用于数据中心硬件分拣；ConvNeXt-T在该基准上top-1达64.3%，远高于仅用现有公开数据集的31.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 退役数据中心硬件可翻新或转售，但分拣需区分外观相似部件、判断破损与遮挡下的状态、读取印刷料号。作者审计9个公开电子废弃物数据集，发现仅10%图像标注为数据中心部件、无状态与印刷文本标签、42%为精确或增强重复样本，无法支撑上述需求。

Method: 构建E-Waste-Bench：从762K去重图像中取328K照片，标注24类类别、粒度、状态与印刷文本；数据源自既有数据集与网络，采用基于配方的爬虫与基于开放权重视觉语言模型的智能体协同采集，智能体负责审核新来源并解析爬虫无法处理的站点，二者均向稀缺类别倾斜；训练标签由模型生成，数据划分按近重复分组，并由人工标注近1万张测试集；基准覆盖分类、状态识别、检测、文本感知与跨数据集迁移。

Result: 在该测试集上，ConvNeXt-T的top-1为64.3%，仅用公开数据集时为31.5%；近重复泄漏在模型生成测试标签下使top-1虚高3.0个点，在人工标签下不足0.1个点，说明模型标注基准需按组划分；在电商与拍卖照片（硬件退出服役的最接近代理场景）上准确率降至42.1%。

Conclusion: 现有公开电子废弃物数据集在类别覆盖、标注维度与去重上存在明显缺陷；E-Waste-Bench通过分组划分与人工测试标注提供了更可靠的评测，揭示模型标注基准的近重复泄漏风险及真实流通场景下的显著性能下降。数据与代码将公开。

Abstract: Retired data-center hardware can often be refurbished or resold, but triage demands more than object categories. A model must tell visually similar parts apart, judge condition under damage and occlusion, and read the printed part number. We audit nine public e-waste collections. They cover mostly consumer devices (only 10% of images are labeled as a data-center part), label neither condition nor printed text, and contain 42% exact or augmented duplicates. We introduce E-Waste-Bench, 328K photographs drawn from 762K deduplicated images and labeled for class (24 classes), granularity, condition and printed text. Images come from those collections and the web, where a recipe-based crawler works beside an agent built on an open-weight vision-language model. The agent vets new sources and reads sites the crawler cannot parse, and both are steered toward under-represented classes. Training labels are model-made, splits follow near-duplicate groups, and human annotators label a test set of nearly 10,000 images. We benchmark classification, condition, detection, text perception and cross-dataset transfer. On this test set, a ConvNeXt-T trained on E-Waste-Bench reaches 64.3% top-1, against 31.5% with the public collections alone. Near-duplicate leakage inflates top-1 by 3.0 points under model-made test labels but by less than 0.1 under human labels, so model-labeled benchmarks need group splits. Accuracy falls to 42.1% on marketplace and auction photographs, the closest proxy for hardware leaving service. Data and code will be released.

</details>


### [33] [NOBODY Was There: Bridging the Human-VLM Gap in Unwarranted Person Attribution Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31714)
*Parsa Madinei,Ziqi Wen,Miguel Eckstein*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文揭示并命名了视觉语言模型（VLM）的"无依据人物归因偏差"：在人物缺席的图像描述中，VLM 比人类多出约四倍地提及人物；提出偏好数据集 NOBODY，经 DPO 对齐后模型的人物归因与人类一致性达到或超过人际一致性，并发布评测基准 NOBODY-Bench。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像描述不仅需要识别可见内容，还需判断哪些内容应被推断。现有 VLM 在人物缺席场景中倾向于将可见动作或事件归因于不在场的人，与人类书写习惯存在系统性差距，而现有公开偏好数据难以纠正该行为。

Method: 在 25 个 SOTA VLM 上量化该偏差（与人类撰写描述对比）；开展偏好研究验证人类与 VLM 均更偏好不进行此类归因的描述；构建针对该偏差的偏好数据集 NOBODY，并采用 DPO 对开源 VLM 进行对齐；对比 9 个公开偏好数据集的效果，并进行人类评判；推出 NOBODY-Bench 评测基准。

Result: 人物缺席场景中，VLM 描述提及人物的频率约为人类描述的 4 倍；DPO 对齐后，模型与人类描述在人物归因上的一致性达到或超过人际一致性，而 9 个现有公开偏好数据集几乎无法改变该行为；人类评审显著更偏好对齐后模型的描述。

Conclusion: 无依据人物归因偏差是 VLM 的普遍且系统性问题，可通过针对性的偏好数据与 DPO 有效缓解；NOBODY 与 NOBODY-Bench 为诊断和对齐该偏差提供了数据与评测工具。

Abstract: Describing an image requires not only recognizing what is visible but deciding what should be inferred. We investigate a systematic gap between humans and VLMs in their tendency to attribute visible actions or events to people who are not present in an image. We term this behavior unwarranted person attribution bias and find that, across 25 SOTA VLMs, captions of person-absent scenes mention a person four times as often as human-written captions of the same images. Through a preference study, we further show that both humans and VLMs prefer captions that avoid such attributions. Motivated by this, we introduce NOBODY, a preference dataset designed to bridge the gap between humans and VLMs in unwarranted person attribution. Through direct preference optimization (DPO), we align open VLMs on NOBODY and show that their agreement with human captions on person attribution reaches or exceeds inter-human agreement, whereas nine existing public preference datasets leave this behavior largely unchanged. Furthermore, we show that human judges preferred the aligned VLM captions significantly more than those of the baseline model. Alongside NOBODY, we release NOBODY-Bench, which evaluates VLMs for unwarranted person attribution bias and their alignment against human captions and preference. Project page: https://pmadinei.github.io/NOBODY/

</details>


### [34] [EventSSM: Causal Spatial-State Modelling for Asynchronous Events Event-Depth Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31720)
*Sadiq O. Macaulay,Dipon Ghosh,Hadfield Simon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EventSSM：一种因果空间状态框架，用按时序排列的事件包驱动循环记忆进行事件相机度量深度估计，并通过几何变换+可微渲染在参考时刻施加帧级监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机输出异步亮度变化，但度量深度监督只在稀疏采样的参考时间戳上可得；把事件聚合成类帧表示虽便于学习，却会破坏原始事件流的细粒度时间结构。因此需要一种因果、保留精确时间信息的深度估计框架。

Method: 以循环记忆（recurrent memory）按时间顺序处理事件包，维护持续空间上下文与物理时间戳的状态保持；为弥合异步预测与帧时刻监督之间的不匹配，利用标定相机位姿对预测的事件深度做几何变换（geometric transport），并在参考时间戳上可微渲染以计算损失。通过受控消融考察跨模态初始化、时间循环、个体状态、读出、监督各组件及事件包细分的影响。

Result: 基准评测中在多项深度误差指标上优于现有事件深度方法；消融实验验证了跨模态初始化、时间循环、个体状态、读出与监督各组件的有效性，并分析了事件包细分粒度的影响。

Conclusion: EventSSM 通过将持久空间上下文与物理时间对齐的状态保持相结合，是因果事件关联度量深度估计的有效框架。

Abstract: Event cameras report asynchronous brightness changes, yet metric-depth supervision is typically available only at sparsely sampled reference timestamps. Aggregating events into frame-like representations simplifies learning but can obscure the fine-grained temporal structure of the original event stream. We introduce EventSSM, a causal spatial-state framework for event-associated metric-depth estimation using a recurrent memory updated by chronological event packets. To bridge asynchronous predictions and frame-time supervision, predicted event depths are geometrically transported using calibrated camera poses and differentiably rendered at reference timestamps. Benchmark evaluations demonstrate improvements over established event-depth methods across multiple depth-error measures. Controlled ablations further examine cross-modal initialization, temporal recurrence, individual state, readout, and supervision components, and packet subdivision. Together, these findings establish EventSSM as an effective framework for causal event-associated metric-depth estimation, combining persistent spatial context with physically timed state retention.

</details>


### [35] [BEARING: Who Is Talking to Whom? Grounding Acoustic Direction in Vision for Online Multi-Party Conversation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31735)
*Nader Lobandi,Lombe Chileshe,Mohammad Mehdi Hosseini,Mohammad H. Mahoor*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出几何优先的在线多人对话理解框架BEARING，仅用麦克风阵列声源方向作为核心信号，实现说话人/受话人判定与对齐，方向提示将说话人准确率从54%提至74%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有群体对话理解系统多为单通道离线处理，依赖自评估奖励进行对齐，缺乏可靠的几何依据，难以在在线场景中判定"谁在对谁说"。

Method: 以麦克风阵列测量的声源方向为核心：用无标签在线标定将该方向配准到相机图像，并构建无需注册的人脸-语音-方向在线注册表；方向用于(i)作为视觉语言模型的视觉提示（说话人标记或屏幕外箭头），(ii)与视觉主动说话人检测进行可靠性加权融合，(iii)作为对齐的可验证奖励（说话人须位于声源方向、置信度须与校验一致、一律回答"group"不得分）。全部决策在线进行，前瞻窗口为显式参数。

Result: 在AMI三个站点与第一视角EasyCom共9,498条受话人标注语句上，方向提示使说话人准确率由54%升至74%，受话人准确率仅变化约2个百分点；AVDIAR同位置说话人场景下视觉主动说话人检测保持95%，仅用方向则降至59%；可验证奖励在GRPO、奖励排序微调与DPO下均优于自评估；前瞻从5s缩至0.5s在AMI上损失4.5 DER点；在线学习配准在前两分钟出现性能下降，随后超过纯视觉方案。

Conclusion: 声源方向是高效且可验证的几何先验，显著提升说话人定位与奖励对齐；但受话人判定更依赖语言内容而非空间位置，需视觉-方向融合与在线标定鲁棒性支撑，代码与评测协议将公开。

Abstract: Understanding a group conversation means deciding, from video and audio, who is speaking and to whom. Leading systems treat these tasks offline on single-channel clips and align models with a self-evaluation reward. We propose BEARING, a geometry-first alternative built on one measured signal: the direction from which a microphone array beside the camera hears a sound.
