A label-free calibration learned from the stream registers this direction into the camera image, and an online registry of faces, voices and directions needs no enrollment. The registered direction serves as (i) a visual prompt for a vision-language model, a marker on the speaker or an arrow toward an off-screen source; (ii) one input of a reliability-weighted fusion with visual active-speaker detection, for speakers in the same direction from the array; and (iii) a verifiable reward for alignment: the named speaker must sit where the sound comes from, the confidence must match that check, and always answering "group" earns nothing. Every decision is online, with the lookahead as an explicit parameter. On 9,498 addressee-labelled utterances from three AMI sites and egocentric EasyCom, the direction prompt raises speaker accuracy from 54% to 74%; addressee accuracy moves by about two points, because telling one addressee from the group depends on the words more than on where people are. For co-located speakers on AVDIAR, visual active-speaker detection holds 95% where direction alone falls to 59%. The verifiable reward beats self-evaluation under GRPO, reward-ranked fine-tuning and DPO; shrinking the lookahead from 5 s to 0.5 s costs 4.5 DER points on AMI, and learning the registration online costs a dip in the first two minutes before the system overtakes vision alone. Code and evaluation protocols will be released.

</details>


### [36] [LeanWAM: Learning Latent World Action Models from Frozen Video Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31740)
*Shaolong Li,Hongze Fu,Joyce Chai,Yinpei Dai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LeanWAM 通过两阶段训练将冻结视频生成器的先验压缩为少量预测 token 并蒸馏进轻量策略，以 1.4B 参数实现优于 VLA/WAM 基线的性能与 12.3–41.5× 的推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型（WAM）通常微调大型预训练视频生成器，联合预测未来观测与机器人动作，训练与部署成本高昂；且尚不清楚策略真正需要何种未来表示、能压缩到多紧凑。

Method: 探测研究表明互补的未来表示可压缩为少量 token 且保留大部分动作解码性能。据此提出 LeanWAM：第一阶段由 Future Extractor 将观测转移压缩为 32 个预测 token，在动作、RGB、语义、几何监督下联合塑造并对冻结视频生成器进行条件化，同时对生成器隐状态的紧凑读出提供互补 world token；第二阶段将两种表示蒸馏进策略，使其能从当前观测预测这些表示并生成动作。

Result: 1.4B 参数、无大规模具身预训练的条件下，在 LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus、RoboTwin 2.0 上优于强 VLA 与 WAM 基线；A100 上预测一个动作块仅需 25.1 ms，比代表性 WAM 快 12.3–41.5 倍。

Conclusion: 紧凑的预测性监督可将视频先验迁移至鲁棒且高效的控制策略，说明未来表示无需高维重建即可有效支撑动作生成。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) typically adapt large pretrained video generators to jointly predict future observations and robot actions, incurring substantial training and deployment costs. However, it remains unclear which future representations a policy actually needs and how compactly they can be represented. Our probing study shows that complementary future representations can be compressed into a few tokens while retaining most of their action-decoding performance. Motivated by this finding, we introduce LeanWAM, a latent WAM that transfers video priors from a frozen video generator to a lightweight policy via two-stage training. In the first stage, a Future Extractor compresses observed transitions into 32 predictive tokens. These tokens condition the frozen generator and are jointly shaped by action, RGB, semantic, and geometric supervision, while a compact readout of the generator's hidden states supplies complementary world tokens. The second stage distills both representations into the policy, which learns to predict them from current observations while generating actions. With 1.4B parameters and no large-scale embodied pretraining, LeanWAM outperforms strong vision-language-action (VLA) and WAM baselines on LIBERO, LIBERO-Plus, and RoboTwin~2.0. It predicts an action chunk in 25.1\,ms on an A100 GPU, 12.3--41.5$\times$ faster than representative WAMs. These results demonstrate that compact predictive supervision can transfer video priors to robust and efficient control.

</details>


### [37] [RVLM-X: Towards a Universal VLM Harness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31755)
*Aadi Palnitkar,Dmytro Kurdydyk,Xiaomin Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出任务通用的递归VLM框架RVLM-X，让模型在代码REPL中自行控制视觉上下文并递归处理高分辨率图像区域，在7个公开基准上优于单次调用，并引入MapGenBench，71.4% vs 最强基线55.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM高分辨率图像处理框架多采用固定流程（如zoom tree），预先决定观察位置与证据融合方式；这类流程弥补旧模型能力不足，但对能可靠规划与调用工具的前沿模型反而会降低精度。

Method: RVLM-X：借鉴Recursive Language Models的递归推理，VLM在持有全分辨率图像的代码REPL中程序化控制自身视觉上下文，并在自选区域上递归调用自身；跨照片、图表、地图任务保持不变，无需任务特定改造。所有方法均以同等成本效益的前沿模型为底座。

Result: 在全部7个公开基准上优于单次调用，汇总精度最高；新提出的MapGenBench（程序化生成、融合细粒度感知与多步推理的地图问答）上，留出集准确率71.4%，最强基线为55.5%。

Conclusion: 随着VLM规划与工具使用能力增强，让模型自行编写视觉搜索流程优于预先固定流程；现有基准多只考察单一细节发现，需更强调细节感知与多步推理结合的任务。

Abstract: Harnesses for vision-language models (VLMs) have grown with the models' capabilities, notably for images far larger than those seen in training, where downscaling loses small details. Most of them wrap the VLM in a fixed procedure, such as a zoom tree, that decides in advance where it looks and how the evidence is combined. These procedures make up for the weaknesses of older models by keeping them on a task-specific path, but with frontier models that reliably plan and use tools, they can lower accuracy. We propose RVLM-X, a task-generalizable recursive VLM harness that runs unchanged across photographs, charts and maps. Following the recursive inference of Recursive Language Models, the VLM programmatically controls its own visual context in a code REPL that holds the full-resolution image and calls itself recursively on regions that it chooses. With a cost-efficient frontier model as the base of every method, RVLM-X is more accurate than a single call on all 7 public benchmarks and has the highest pooled accuracy. Because existing benchmarks mostly test whether a model finds one small detail, we also introduce MapGenBench, procedurally generated maps whose questions combine fine-detail perception with multi-step reasoning. There, RVLM-X answers 71.4% of held-out questions against 55.5% for the strongest baseline. Overall, our findings suggest that as VLMs plan and use tools more reliably, letting the model write its own visual search works better than fixing one in advance.

</details>


### [38] [Xtream: An Explicit Metric State for Large-Scale Streaming Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31757)
*Ernesto Lozano,Alberto Jaenal,Javier Civera*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Xtream 是一种混合流式管线，以 3D 基础模型（3DFM）作为几何前端，并通过关键帧上的因子图维护显式度量状态，从而实现大规模在线重建并显著降低轨迹漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DFM 可前馈式稠密重建，但有效跨度受跨视图注意力限制。既有方案（视频块几何对齐+隐式记忆递归更新、位姿图）易漂移，根本原因是单目几何缺乏度量尺度可观测性。

Method: 提出 Xtream 混合流式管线：3DFM 作为几何前端，在关键帧上构建因子图以维护显式度量状态；度量信息有两条注入途径——3DFM 的度量尺度预测，或在可用时使用惯性测量。

Result: 在 VBR、Oxford Spires、KITTI 等大规模基准上，Xtream 在基于 3DFM 的在线重建中达到 SOTA，相较已发表基线及同期工作降低了平均绝对轨迹误差。

Conclusion: 将 3DFM 前端与因子图的显式度量状态相结合，可有效缓解漂移，为大规模在线重建提供可扩展的混合流式范式。

Abstract: 3D foundation models (3DFMs) enable dense reconstruction in a feed-forward pass, yet their effective span is limited by cross-view attention. Local consistency has been expanded through geometric alignment of video chunks and recursive updates of an implicit memory, and large-scale scenes with pose graphs. However, both strategies are prone to drift, largely due to the lack of metric-scale observability from monocular geometry. We present Xtream,a hybrid streaming pipeline that maintains an explicit metric state through a factor graph over keyframes, while using a 3DFM as a geometric front end. Xtream incorporates metric information in two ways: through metric scale predictions from 3DFMs; or through inertial measurements if available. Across the large-scale benchmarks of VBR, Oxford Spires and KITTI, Xtream achieves state-of-the-art performance in 3DFM-based online reconstruction, reducing average absolute trajectory errors compared with published baselines and concurrent work. https://ernestolozano.github.io/xtream

</details>


### [39] [Dreamcatcher: Remembering What Surprises a World Model in Video Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31765)
*Jinxi He,Ce Zhang,Wenhe Liu,Jianshu Zhang,Katia P. Sycara,Yaqi Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练框架 Dreamcatcher，利用独立预训练、冻结的视频世界模型的预测误差（temporal/spatial surprisal）指导流式视频问答的在线记忆形成与分段，并配合 query 时的 evidence gate 控制证据注入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直播视频流越来越长，反复编码与处理完整历史代价高昂；需要在未来问题未知前以单次前向构建视觉记忆。现有方法多保留与早期不同的 token 或匹配预先固定的替代查询，缺乏世界动态建模，难以区分细微的意外细节与可预期的变化。

Method: 免训练框架。以近期历史为条件，用冻结的视频世界模型预测后续特征；对预测误差做归一化，得到时间与空间 surprisal 信号，驱动在线分段并保留冻结 Video-LLM 的原生视觉 token，在随分段年龄衰减的预算下平衡意外细节与视觉覆盖。查询时，evidence gate 仅当 Video-LLM 判断某分段在当前视图之外提供增量证据时，才从相似度候选列表中接纳该分段。

Result: 在三个流式与三个离线长视频基准上评测。OVO-Bench 上，对四个冻结 Video-LLM 均在对比的免训练方法中取得最高总体准确率，Qwen3.8-27B 下达 78.9%；三个 Qwen Video-LLM 在 1 fps 下将答案生成的平均输入 token 相对基座模型减少超过 80%。

Conclusion: 预测性 surprisal 信号可替代固定启发式，作为免训练在线记忆形成的有效依据，在保持甚至提升准确率的同时大幅压缩输入 token。

Abstract: As live video streams grow longer, repeatedly encoding and processing their full histories becomes increasingly costly. Answering questions as they arrive therefore calls for visual memory built in one pass before future questions are known. Existing methods mainly keep tokens that differ from earlier ones or match surrogate queries fixed in advance. Without modeling world dynamics, these criteria struggle to tell subtle surprising details from expected change. We introduce Dreamcatcher, a training-free framework and, to our knowledge, the first to use predictive signals from an independently pretrained, frozen video world model for online memory formation in streaming video question answering. Conditioned on recent history, the world model predicts incoming features using learned world priors. Its normalized prediction errors yield temporal and spatial surprisal signals that guide online segmentation and the retention of a frozen Video-LLM's native visual tokens, balancing surprising details with broad visual coverage under budgets that decay with segment age. At query time, an evidence gate admits a segment from a similarity-based shortlist only when the Video-LLM judges that it adds evidence for the question beyond the current view. We evaluate Dreamcatcher on three streaming and three offline long-video benchmarks. On OVO-Bench, Dreamcatcher achieves the highest overall accuracy among the compared training-free methods for each of four frozen Video-LLMs, reaching 78.9% with Qwen3.8-27B. With the three Qwen Video-LLMs, it also reduces average input tokens for answer generation by more than 80% relative to their base models at 1 fps.

</details>


### [40] [RoMo Hands: A Large Scale Richly Organized Text to Hand Motion Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31766)
*Yizhak Ben-Shabat,Jiahao Zhang,Joseph Liu,Seonghyeon Moon,Young-Yoon Lee,Haomiao Jiang,Oren Jacob,Mubbasir Kapadia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RoMo Hands：约542K序列（884小时、95.5M帧@30fps）的大规模野外双手动作-文本数据集，配多层级文本标注与393维双手表示，并系统性基准评测三类生成范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯手部动作的文本驱动合成受数据瓶颈制约：现有数据集将手视为刚性末端执行器、缺乏细粒度文本描述、规模不足，难以支撑稳健泛化。

Method: 构建大规模野外文本配对数据集，采用严格灵巧度过滤器剔除低关节活动序列以聚焦复杂双手运动；每段配5条由全局语义标签到原子手指状态的多粒度描述，并按层级分类体系组织；提出393维双手表示，将冗余的身体运动建模范式适配到手部高自由度运动学；对扩散、掩码token与自回归三类生成器进行基准测试。

Result: 数据集规模较先前工作高一个数量级（884小时、95.5M帧@30fps）；基准揭示三类生成器在手部特定适配下的差异化权衡，尤其体现语义对齐与运动学合理性之间的显著背离。

Conclusion: 数据集、表示与基准共同为下一代灵巧手部动作合成奠定严格基础。

Abstract: While text-driven body motion generation has matured, pure-hand synthesis remains bottlenecked by data. Existing datasets often treat hands as rigid end-effectors, lack fine-grained captions, or are too limited in scale to support robust generalization. We introduce RoMo Hands, an in-the-wild, text-paired dataset of ~542K sequences (884 hours, 95.5M frames at 30 fps) which is an order of magnitude larger than prior work. To ensure high-quality supervision, a stringent dexterity filter discards low-articulation sequences, focusing the distribution on complex bimanual movement. Each clip is paired with five captions at increasing granularities from global semantic tags to atomic finger states, organized under a hierarchical taxonomy. To bridge the gap between body and hand architectures, we propose a novel 393-D bimanual representation that adapts redundant body-motion paradigms to the high-DoF kinematics of hands. Systematic benchmarking of diffusion, masked-token, and autoregressive generators reveals distinct trade-offs under these hand-specific adaptations, including a sharp divergence between semantic alignment and kinematic plausibility. Our dataset, representation, and benchmark establish a rigorous foundation for the next generation of dexterous motion synthesis. Project page: https://romodataset.github.io/romo-hands/.

</details>


### [41] [ET: Efficient Transfer from Softmax to Hybrid Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31770)
*Guangfu Guo,Xiaoqian Lu,Linsey Pang,Weiran Yao,Haolin Chen,Long Cheng,Kunpeng Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ET框架通过冻结预训练权重、仅对新增参数做交替最小二乘校准，将softmax Transformer高保真转换为线性复杂度注意力，几乎无性能损失且无需梯度微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: softmax Transformer转为高效注意力常导致显著性能下降，而恢复性能通常依赖昂贵的微调，需要一种低优化成本的高保真转换方法。

Method: ET组合精确局部注意力、共享键softmax注意力、均值锚定的无扫描全局线性校正与自适应门控，实现序列长度线性复杂度；冻结预训练权重，仅用少量无标签样本以交替最小二乘校准新增参数，无梯度更新。

Result: ImageNet-1K上DeiT-S/DeiT-B top-1为79.64%/81.55%，距softmax教师0.25个百分点内；768×768下较T^5高3.59个百分点；DiT-XL/2上FID-50K为2.59，仅250次LoRA更新；SD 3.5 Medium在1K分辨率匹配教师GenEval分数，0.73 A100 GPU小时，推理加速1.39×。

Conclusion: ET以无梯度、极小计算代价实现高保真注意力转换，在视觉分类与生成任务上均接近甚至匹配教师模型，兼具效率与保真度。

Abstract: Converting pretrained softmax Transformers to efficient attention often causes substantial performance degradation, while recovery typically requires costly fine-tuning. We propose ET (Efficient Transfer), a framework for high-fidelity attention conversion with minimal optimization cost. ET combines exact local and shared-key softmax attention with a mean-anchored, scan-free global linear correction and adaptive gating, achieving linear complexity in sequence length. To preserve pretrained knowledge, ET freezes pretrained weights and calibrates only newly introduced parameters through alternating least squares on a small set of unlabeled samples, without gradient updates. On ImageNet-1K, ET achieves 79.64% and 81.55% top-1 accuracy on DeiT-S and DeiT-B, respectively, within 0.25 percentage points of their softmax teachers. At $768\times768$, ET outperforms T$^5$ by 3.59 percentage points at comparable inference FLOPs, without gradient-based fine-tuning. On DiT-XL/2, ET achieves an FID-50K of 2.59 with only 250 LoRA updates. On Stable Diffusion 3.5 Medium, ET matches the teacher's GenEval score at 1K resolution with just 0.73 A100 GPU-hours of gradient-free conversion and a 1.39$\times$ inference speedup.

</details>


### [42] [BODYBENCH: Evaluating Adversarial Image Defenses Against AI Nudification Inpainting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31774)
*Li Qiwei,Salma Abdel Magid,Olga Russakovsky,Eric Gilbert,Sarita Schoenebeck*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 现有对抗扰动图像保护方法从未针对"裸化(nudification)"攻击进行评估，而该威胁模型与既有威胁模型存在结构性错配；作者提出BODYBENCH基准与三项伤害对齐指标，审计四种方法后发现仅一种方法在一种配置下有效，且PSNR等常规指标与裸化防护几乎不相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI生成的未经同意的私密影像(AIG-NCII)是生成式AI最受关注的危害之一，而裸化编辑可轻易通过inpainting实现，这正是保护方法的设计场景。但此前没有工作评估防护方法对抗裸化编辑的效果。作者认为这属于"问题形式化失败"：既有威胁模型（掩码形状、训练/测试提示分布、成功判据）与裸化攻击不对应，导致评估系统性高估防护效果。

Method: 提出BODYBENCH基准：648个完全合成的着衣成年受试者图像，每人配11种掩码变体，覆盖身体、面部与上下文区域。定义三项与伤害对齐的指标——性化程度(sexualization)、自然度(naturalness)与可识别性(recognizability)，共同判定inpainted输出是否构成成功的裸化攻击。在此基准上审计PhotoGuard、DiffusionGuard、AdvPaint、DiffVax四种近期防护方法，并分析PSNR等常规指标与裸化防护的相关性。

Result: 仅有一种方法的一种配置实现防护成功，且仅在防御方与攻击方掩码完全对齐时成立——这是真实防御方无法保证的条件，且作者证明该条件可被轻易、稳定地绕过。标准指标如PSNR与裸化防护效果基本不相关。

Conclusion: 既有图像保护方法的形式化与裸化伤害不对齐，现有评估高估防护效果。BODYBENCH使这一错配可度量，为以已记录伤害为基础的防御研究提供基础。

Abstract: Adversarial perturbation defenses protect images from unauthorized edits by adding imperceptible noise. Yet none of these methods have been evaluated against nudification, despite AI-generated non-consensual intimate imagery (AIG-NCII) being among the most documented harms of generative AI. Moreover, nudification edits are readily carried out through inpainting, the primary setting these protection methods are designed to defend against. We argue this is a problem-formulation failure since prior threat models do not correspond to nudification edits (also called attacks). The misalignment of existing approaches is structural, spanning mask shape, training and testing prompt distributions, and success criterion which causes existing evaluations to systematically overestimate protection effectiveness. We reformulate image protection against unauthorized editing around this documented harm. We introduce BODYBENCH, a benchmark of 648 fully synthetic clothed adult subjects, each paired with 11 mask variants spanning body, face, and context regions. We propose three harm-aligned metrics: sexualization, naturalness, and recognizability that jointly determine whether an inpainted output constitutes a successful nudification attack. Auditing four recent protection methods (PhotoGuard, DiffusionGuard, AdvPaint, DiffVax) on BODYBENCH, we find that protection succeeds in only one configuration of one method, and only under exact mask alignment between defender and attacker---a condition no real defender can guarantee and one that we show is easily always bypassed. We also show that standard evaluation metrics such as peak signal-to-noise ratio (PSNR) are essentially uncorrelated with nudification protection. BODYBENCH makes this misalignment measurable and provides a foundation for defenses grounded in documented harm.

</details>


### [43] [ReCAP: Sequential Concept Acquisition with Guarantees under Imperfect Concept Predictors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31776)
*Md Nahiduzzaman,Steven Korevaar,Alireza Bab-Hadiashar,Ruwan Tennakoon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReCAP 提出一种面向序贯概念采集的可信赖诊断框架，显式建模概念预测器的错误与弃权，用标签判别性目标替代条件信息增益，并证明其自适应单调与自适应子模性，从而为贪心固定预算采集提供 (1-1/e) 近似保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有序贯概念采集方法默认预测出的概念是正确的，导致"正确诊断可能建立在错误证据之上"，解释不可信；同时解释冗长。作者希望解释既能短小，又能在概念预测含错误与弃权时仍然可靠。

Method: 提出 ReCAP：在采集与推理中直接利用概念预测器报告的原始结果（含错误预测与弃权）；以标签判别性目标（区分需要不同诊断的病例）替代条件信息增益；理论证明该目标自适应单调且自适应子模，贪心固定预算采集获得 (1-1/e) 近似，并在学习估计下保证有界退化；结合弃权感知采集与诊断保持剪枝以缩短解释。

Result: 在五个医学影像数据集上，相对最强基线，将"至少由一个错误预测概念支撑的正确诊断"占比从 27.1% 降至 9.6%，同时使用不足一半的概念数量，并获得更高的诊断准确率。

Conclusion: 显式建模概念预测错误与弃权、并以标签判别性目标驱动采集，可在更少概念下同时提升解释可靠性与诊断性能；理论上具备子模近似保证，实践上适用性良好。

Abstract: Sequential concept acquisition enables interpretable diagnosis from a compact set of human-understandable findings. Such explanations are useful only if clinicians can trust them, yet most existing methods treat predicted concepts as correct, so a correct diagnosis can rest on incorrectly predicted evidence. We propose Reliable Concept Acquisition with Pruning (ReCAP), which reasons over what the concept predictor reports, including errors and abstentions, and replaces conditional information gain with a label-discriminating objective that separates cases requiring different diagnoses. We prove this objective adaptively monotone and adaptively submodular, yielding a (1-1/e) guarantee for greedy fixed-budget acquisition that degrades boundedly under learned estimates, and combine it with abstention-aware acquisition and diagnosis-preserving pruning to keep explanations short and reliable. Across five medical imaging datasets, ReCAP reduces the share of correct diagnoses supported by at least one incorrectly predicted concept from 27.1% to 9.6% relative to the strongest baseline, while using fewer than half as many concepts and achieving higher diagnostic accuracy.

</details>


### [44] [A Well-Posed Fourth-Order Navier-Stokes Model for Image Inpainting: Theory, Order Selection, and Stripe Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31777)
*Yiwen Dou,Yiqiang Ding,Zhongyun Bao,Senyan Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文为Bertalmío-Bertozzi-Sapiro的Navier-Stokes图像修复模型首次给出系统性的高阶扩展：指出直观的四阶修正不适定，构造了良定的"门控前向四阶项"，并辅以条带分解处理大面积缺失块，在无训练条件下取得优于经典基线与不逊于最强PDE求解器的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NS修复模型是PDE类方法中唯一的输运型范式，却始终缺乏高阶扩展；直接的四阶修正存在适定性问题，且大面积缺失块处修正确失效。

Method: 在涡量-流函数表述下引入带门控的四阶项：其符号至关重要，后向形式属Perona-Malik型反扩散，Hadamard不适定且在实验中一致有害；前向形式则一致抛物、能量稳定且一致有效。提出"阶数-耗散原理"（奇数阶为色散、偶数阶为耗散），据此判定四阶为最低可容许阶。针对大块缺失，采用条带分解：先Telea基础填充，再交错进行狭缝式NS迭代，把块状损伤转化为模型擅长的结构狭缝情形；该装置与基础填充无关，增益可与前向修正叠加。

Result: 在BSDS500、DIV2K与一个中国古画基准三个数据集，及裂纹、块状、文字遮挡三类损伤上，该免训练模型在结构主导损伤的每个样本上均超过所有经典基线；在42幅古画测试集上较Telea提升+1.02 dB PSNR，逐样本胜率42/42；与近期最强PDE求解器差距在0.4 dB内，同时取得整个PDE家族最佳感知质量；消费级GPU上单图耗时4–16秒。

Conclusion: 四阶前向门控修正为NS图像修复提供了良定且有效的高阶扩展，配合条带分解覆盖大面积缺失，形成通用、无需训练、兼顾保真与感知质量的PDE修复框架。

Abstract: The Navier-Stokes (NS) inpainting model of Bertalmío, Bertozzi and Sapiro is the only transport-type paradigm among PDE-based methods, yet it has never received a systematic higher-order extension. We show that the obvious fourth-order correction is ill-posed, and we construct the corrected, well-posed model. In the vorticity-streamfunction formulation we add a gated fourth-order term whose sign is decisive: the backward form is a Perona-Malik-type anti-diffusion, Hadamard ill-posed and consistently harmful in experiments, whereas the forward form is uniformly parabolic, energy-stable and consistently beneficial. An order-dissipation principle, stating that odd orders are dispersive and even orders dissipative, singles out the fourth order as the lowest admissible one. Large missing blocks pose a separate problem, because the correction gate stays silent there; we answer it with stripe decomposition, a Telea base fill followed by interleaved slit-wise NS passes, which converts block damage into the structural-slit regime where the model excels. The device is base-agnostic, and its gains add to those of the forward correction. On three datasets, namely BSDS500, DIV2K and a classical Chinese painting benchmark, and on three damage types, namely cracks, blocks and text occlusion, the training-free model beats every classical baseline on every sample of structure-dominated damage, gains +1.02 dB PSNR over Telea on the full 42-image painting test set with a 42/42 per-sample win rate, matches the strongest recent PDE solvers within 0.4 dB while attaining the best perceptual quality of the entire PDE family, and runs at 4-16 s per image on a consumer GPU.

</details>


### [45] [VISOR: A Vision-Language Selector for Multimodal Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31780)
*Seunghan Lee,Jun Seo,Junhyeok Kang,Jaehoon Lee,Sangjun Han,Sungdong Yoo,Minjae Kim,Tae Yoon Lim,Dongwan Kang,Hwanil Choi,Soonyoung Lee,Wonbin Ahn*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VISOR：用视觉-语言模型（VLM）从随机预测器采样的多样候选未来中“选一个”，而非取中位数/均值，实现多模态时间序列预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作两点不足：1）TSFM将预测分布压缩为中位数或均值，把多样未来抹平成“平稳线”；2）多模态TS预测通过TS-语言对齐引入文本，其对齐能力远弱于视觉-语言对齐。

Method: VISOR含两部分：1）无文本的随机预测器采样多样候选未来；2）VLM选择器将候选绘成单张图表图像，依据文本从中选出一个。为回避过度平滑的预测路径，用形状感知目标训练选择器，同时评估逐点误差与路径形状。

Result: 在覆盖十个数据集的三个基准上，VISOR在点误差与波动率误差上均取得最优平均排名，优于TSFM与多模态TS预测模型。

Conclusion: 以“选择候选未来”替代“分布平均”，并借VLM更强的视觉-语言对齐完成文本条件筛选，可同时改善点预测精度与波动率刻画。

Abstract: Recent time-series foundation models (TSFMs) forecast a distribution over futures, and multimodal time-series (TS) forecasting further exploits other modalities to improve forecasting accuracy. However, existing works have two limitations: 1) TSFMs reduce the predictive distribution to its median or mean, flattening diverse futures into a calm line, and 2) multimodal TS forecasting models incorporate text into the forecaster through TS-language alignment, which is substantially weaker than vision-language alignment. To address these limitations, we propose the VIsion-language SelectOR (VISOR), a multimodal TS forecasting framework that selects one plausible future instead of averaging across candidates, leveraging the alignment capabilities of vision-language models (VLMs) to select the most suitable candidate based on textual information. VISOR consists of two components: 1) a text-free stochastic predictor that samples diverse candidate futures, and 2) a VLM selector that reads the candidates as a single chart image and selects one based on the text. To avoid overly calm forecasting paths, we train the selector with a shape-aware target that evaluates each candidate based on both point-wise error and path shape. Across three benchmarks covering ten datasets, VISOR achieves the best average ranking in both point and volatility errors compared with TSFMs and multimodal TS forecasting models. Code: https://github.com/seunghan96/VISOR.

</details>


### [46] [SceneMemBench: A Benchmark for Scene Memory in Interactive Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31803)
*Yuheng Si,Linye Lyu,Jiawei Zhou,Lingyi Du,Nanfei Ye,Aobo Liu,Yu Liu,Yu Li,Cheng Zhuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 SceneMemBench，用于评测交互式视频世界模型的场景记忆；区分“已见视角回忆 vs 新视角恢复”和“受控诊断 vs 端到端交互”两个维度，含 600 条受控轨迹与 60 个全 rollout 案例，覆盖 33 个 Unreal Engine 场景和 8 个代表性模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆评测多聚焦已见视角重访与完整 rollout，导致跨视角场景恢复被忽视，且记忆能力与轨迹跟随误差、累积生成退化相互纠缠，难以分离诊断。

Method: 构造 SceneMemBench：引入 Continued Rollout（从共享的真值观测历史继续生成）与 Full Rollout（完全由模型自生成交互）两种协议；构建 600 条受控轨迹（33 个 Unreal Engine 场景）与 60 个 Full Rollout 案例；沿主体一致性、外观保真度、几何一致性三个指标评测场景恢复；覆盖 8 个开源与闭源模型。

Result: 新视角恢复普遍比已见视角回忆更具挑战；模型在受控历史下的能力画像与完整交互下的表现可存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 仅依赖已见视角重访或端到端 rollout 会掩盖互补的失效模式；SceneMemBench 通过正交的评测维度揭示这些被遮蔽的问题，为交互式视频世界模型的记忆能力提供更可诊断的评估框架。

Abstract: Interactive video world models generate navigable worlds frame by frame, but a persistent world requires previously observed scenes to remain recoverable as users move away and return. Existing memory evaluations, however, often focus on seen-view revisits and full rollouts, leaving cross-view scene recovery underexplored while entangling memory with trajectory-following errors and accumulated generation degradation. We introduce SceneMemBench, a benchmark for scene memory in interactive video world models. It evaluates two complementary dimensions: seen-view recall versus novel-view recovery, and controlled diagnosis versus end-to-end interaction. We introduce Continued Rollout from a shared ground-truth observation history, alongside Full Rollout over complete model-generated interactions. SceneMemBench contains 600 controlled trajectories across 33 Unreal Engine scenes and 60 Full Rollout cases, covering eight representative open- and closed-source models. We evaluate scene recovery along subject consistency, appearance fidelity, and geometric consistency. Results show that novel-view recovery is generally more challenging than seen-view recall, while model profiles under controlled histories can differ markedly from those observed in full interaction. Together, these findings reveal complementary failure modes obscured by seen-view revisits or end-to-end rollouts alone.

</details>


### [47] [Generalizable Feed-Forward 3D Hand-Object Reconstruction with Coarse-to-Fine Contact Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31812)
*Seonghwan Park,Yong-Hoon Kwon,Min-Gyu Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HORCRUX：统一前馈Transformer，粗到细接触估计，实现单图手-物联合3D重建与精确接触定位


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从单张图像重建手与物体的3D位姿及二者接触，是理解操作行为的基础。实用性要求：需泛化到未见物体，且接触需精确定位。现有联合前馈估计器的两大缺陷：依赖预定义物体集合；接触仅由图像特征预测，缺乏定位所需的显式几何。

Method: 提出HORCRUX，一个统一前馈Transformer，采用粗到细接触估计。（1）未见物体：用冻结的基础模型，从给定或图像重建的网格编码物体，而非物体特定知识；（2）用共享Transformer对物体与一只或两只手联合推理以保持对齐；（3）精确接触：由潜在特征得到的逐关节粗预测，结合重建几何在更精细的手部区域上细化求解。

Result: 在七个基准上，HORCRUX在联合估计器中取得最优的物体与手部位姿精度，并在细粒度接触上优于专门的接触估计器。

Conclusion: 接触最好与重建联合估计，因为重建几何可直接定位接触。

Abstract: Reconstructing the 3D poses of hands and objects from a single image, together with the contact between them, is fundamental to understanding manipulation. To be practical, the reconstruction must generalize to unseen objects, and the contact must be localized precisely. Yet joint feed-forward estimators often rely on predefined object sets, while contact is typically predicted from image features alone, without the explicit geometry needed to localize it. To address both limitations, we present HORCRUX, a unified feed-forward transformer with coarse-to-fine contact estimation. For unseen objects, a frozen foundation model encodes the object from a given or image-reconstructed mesh rather than from object-specific knowledge. A shared transformer then reasons jointly over the object and one or two hands to keep them aligned. For precise contact, coarse per-joint predictions from latent features are resolved over finer hand regions using the reconstructed geometry. Across seven benchmarks, HORCRUX achieves state-of-the-art object and hand pose accuracy among joint estimators and outperforms a dedicated contact estimator in fine-grained contact. These results suggest that contact is best estimated jointly with reconstruction, whose geometry directly localizes it.

</details>


### [48] [G0.7: Scaling Up a Fast World Action Model with Long Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31822)
*Tianyuan Yuan,Baijun Ye,Shicheng Cao,Xing Nie,Xiaopeng Yu,Ke Dong,Zijie Zhao,Shenling Qiu,Yizhao Xu,Feiyang Xue,Haonan Liu,Haoyu Zhang,Haobo Jia,Zheng Liu,Anqi Yang,Jiahui Niu,Yue Sun,Tailai Cheng,Jianpeng Yu,Yang Wen,Longhao Wei,Zibin Dong,Shiduo Zhang,Yicheng Liu,Hang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: G0.7 是一个沿"长上下文"与"大规模预训练"两轴扩展的通用世界动作模型（WAM）：训练时用视频预测塑造策略表征，部署时跳过未来帧生成，直接解码动作，从而在单卡 RTX 5090 上以 60 ms/16 步动作块实现实时闭环控制，并在仿真与真机上取得领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 WAM 通过联合预测未来相机帧与动作将视频先验迁移到操作策略，但"先想象再行动"的测试时逐动作生成导致推理过慢，难以闭环控制；同时多数策略受限于很短的观测历史，缺乏长时程上下文与大规模数据下的泛化能力。

Method: 基于 Fast-WAM 的发现——视频预测仅在训练阶段塑造策略表征、部署时可省去未来帧合成——构建 G0.7，并从两方面扩展：(1) 上下文窗口从单帧观测扩展至最长约 5 分钟历史；(2) 预训练规模扩展至含机器人遥操作数据与人类第一视角（ego）视频的大规模语料。推理上采用直接动作解码（Fast 模式），并与同检查点的 imagine-then-act（IDM）推理对比。

Result: Fast 模式与 IDM 性能相当，但每个 16 步动作块仅需 60 ms（单张 RTX 5090，比 IDM 快 7.6×），支持无停顿的实时分块推理。性能随预训练数据稳定提升，人类 ego 视频对分布外泛化贡献最大：五个仿真基准套件平均 70.8 分；Galaxea R1 Pro 与 R1 Lite 上零样本拾放成功率 48% 与 54%；视频条件上下文学习任务中分布内 96.7%、分布外 93.3%；七个真实世界后训练任务（含数分钟级叠箱、备饮、商品装袋）平均任务进度 86.9%。

Conclusion: 视频预测可作为训练期的表征正则/先验注入手段，而非测试期必需的计算步骤，使 WAM 兼具世界模型先验与实时控制效率；长历史上下文与人类 ego 视频的规模化预训练是提升通用性与分布外泛化的关键驱动因素。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) adapt pretrained video generators for robot control by jointly predicting future camera frames and actions, transferring physical priors learned from video into manipulation policies. However, generating future frames before every action makes test-time inference too slow for closed-loop control, while most existing policies remain restricted to short observation histories. Building on Fast-WAM, which showed that video prediction shapes policy representations during training so that future frame synthesis can be skipped at deployment, we present G0.7, a generalist world action model scaled along two axes: extending the context window from a single observation to up to five minutes of history, and scaling pretraining to a large-scale corpus of robot teleoperation and human egocentric (ego) video. Across public simulation suites and real-robot deployments, direct action decoding (Fast mode) matches imagine-then-act (IDM) inference from the same checkpoint while running in 60 ms per 16-step action chunk on a single RTX 5090 (7.6x faster than IDM) and supporting pause-free real-time chunking. Performance scales consistently with pretraining data, with human ego video driving the largest gains in out-of-distribution generalization: G0.7 achieves a 70.8 mean score across five simulation benchmark suites, 48% and 54% zero-shot pick-and-place success on Galaxea R1 Pro and R1 Lite, 96.7% in-distribution and 93.3% out-of-distribution success on video-conditioned in-context learning tasks, and 86.9% mean task progress across seven real-world post-training tasks including multi-minute box folding, drink preparation, and product bagging.

</details>


### [49] [Gaze World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31836)
*Jiawen Zhang,Houze Yang,Xu Cao,Yifan Shen,Zhongyi Zhou,Steve Wong,Ismini Lourentzou,Inki Kim,James M. Rehg*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Gaze World Model (GWM)，在冻结的V-JEPA 2.1编码器上联合训练潜在场景预测与人物条件化注视监督，实现注视目标估计、注视转移检测及未来注视/转移预测，转移检测四类AP较最强基线提升约10点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有注视目标估计方法基本逐帧处理，未建模注视在目标间的转移；而注视转移常由场景动态变化驱动，这类信息正是世界模型擅长编码的。难点在于这种关联很微弱——画面远处的事件可能只引起头部或眼部的细微运动。

Method: 在冻结的V-JEPA 2.1编码器上，将潜在场景预测目标与人物条件化的注视监督联合训练（co-train）。输入为视频片段与人物头部框，模型估计注视位置、检测当前片段内的注视转移，并基于预测的场景特征外推未来注视与转移。同时为VideoAttentionTarget和ChildPlay数据集增补事件级注视转移标注，用于训练与评测。

Result: 在两个基准上取得SOTA的注视转移检测性能，四类AP较最强基线高约10点；注视目标估计与最强图像类模型相当；在未来注视定位与转移预测任务上优于对比基线。消融显示潜在预测目标同时提升了转移检测与注视定位。

Conclusion: 将世界模型式的潜在场景预测引入注视建模，可有效捕捉由场景动态驱动的注视转移，并支持对未来的注视与转移预测；事件级转移标注为该方向提供了评测基础。

Abstract: Where a person looks indicates their focus of attention, and how their gaze shifts between targets reveals how that attention responds to a changing scene. Existing gaze target estimation methods largely operate frame by frame and do not model these shifts. Gaze shifts are often driven by dynamic changes in the scene, which is the kind of information world models are designed to encode. This relationship is subtle, however, since an event far across the frame may produce only a slight movement of the person's head or eyes. We therefore introduce the Gaze World Model (GWM), which co-trains latent scene prediction with person-conditioned gaze supervision on a frozen V-JEPA 2.1 encoder; in our ablations, the latent prediction objective improves both shift detection and gaze localization. Given a video clip and the person's head box, GWM estimates gaze locations and detects gaze shifts in the observed clip, and forecasts future gaze and shifts from predicted scene features. To train and evaluate these tasks, we augment VideoAttentionTarget and ChildPlay with event-level gaze-shift annotations. GWM achieves state-of-the-art shift detection on these benchmarks, about 10 points higher in four-class AP than the strongest baseline, while remaining comparable to the strongest image-based models in gaze target estimation, and outperforms the compared baselines on future gaze localization and shift forecasting.

</details>


### [50] [JECT: Addressing the Context Shortcut in Joint-Embedding Predictive Learning for Chest CT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31849)
*Hanbin Ko,Hong-Jun Choi,Jihun Kim,Young-Gon Kim,Dongheon Lee,Chang Min Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出JECT框架，指出联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）在胸部CT中因"上下文捷径"而忽略小病灶等不可由周边解剖预测的关键发现；为此将放射报告同时作为预测器的条件与编码器的预测目标（掩码片段文本预测），并采用分层三维编码器在各尺度预测。消融显示文本目标提升分类与定位，条件作用对编码器增益有限；在173k卷上从零训练即达到现有CT基础模型水平，且其预测器可零训练复用做报告句子定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在胸部CT中，决定诊断的发现（如小结节）常无法由周围解剖推断。JEPA式掩码特征预测会把隐藏病灶"预测成"正常解剖，损失函数不再奖励编码器表征它，即"上下文捷径"。虽可用报告作为预测器条件来修复预测，但此时报告直接提供了编码器本应学到的信息，编码器相对纯图像预训练增益甚微。

Method: JECT让报告承担双重角色：（1）作为预测器的条件；（2）通过掩码片段文本预测作为编码器的预测目标，即由未掩码扫描预测报告中的解剖章节文本。模型为分层三维编码器，在每个层级/尺度进行预测，以在各级保留临床语义。在173k卷CT上从零预训练，并通过消融分别检验文本目标与条件作用的贡献。

Result: 消融实验表明：文本目标提升分类与定位性能，而条件作用对编码器的额外收益很小。从零训练的模型在分类、检索、结节恶性度任务上达到现有CT基础模型水平；微调后在肺癌风险预测上达到任务专用模型水平。此外，条件化的预测器可无需额外训练直接复用，用于定位报告句子在图像上的作用区域，其变化最大处即为该句描述的发现。

Conclusion: 报告不应仅作为预测的条件，更应作为编码器的预测目标，才能迫使编码器学习临床相关发现、克服上下文捷径；跨尺度分层预测保持多尺度临床内容。该方法可作为CT通用基础模型的有效预训练范式，并天然支持报告—图像的可解释定位，代码与预训练权重已公开。

Abstract: Joint-embedding predictive architectures learn by predicting features of masked regions from visible context. In chest computed tomography, the findings that decide a diagnosis, such as a small nodule, are often not predictable from the surrounding anatomy. A hidden finding is then best predicted toward normal anatomy, and the prediction loss stops rewarding the encoder for representing it. We call this the context shortcut. Conditioning the predictor on the radiology report repairs the prediction, but in our setup the encoder gains little over image-only pretraining: the report supplies what the encoder never had to learn. JECT therefore gives the report two roles: a condition for the predictor and, through masked section-text prediction, a target for the encoder, which must predict the report's anatomical sections from the unmasked scan. A hierarchical three-dimensional encoder that predicts at every stage keeps this clinical content at every scale. In ablations, the text target improves classification and localization, while conditioning adds little to the encoder. Trained from scratch on 173k volumes, the recipe reaches the level of current computed tomography foundation models in classification, retrieval and nodule malignancy, and of task-specific models in lung-cancer risk prediction after fine-tuning. Conditioning still matters: its predictor can be reused, with no extra training, to show where a report sentence acts, changing most at the finding the sentence describes. Code and pretrained weights are available.

</details>


### [51] [Don't Miss the Forest for the Trees: Zero-Shot Skeleton Action Recognition with Hyperbolic Hierarchical Semantic Prototypes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31877)
*Zixiao Jin,Siyu Zhu,Feng Ding,Haisheng Fu,Xin Dong,Shankai Chen,Jie Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文指出现有零样本骨架动作识别仅在扁平叶子标签上评测，叶级对齐无法迁移到祖先概念；提出双曲层次语义原型框架H²SP，在Lorentz双曲空间中表示动作分类树每个节点，以递归父子测地插值合成原型，并通过软蕴含锥正则将运动接地传播至祖先概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本骨架动作识别的评测几乎只针对扁平叶子标签，尚不清楚表征是否也能在骨架运动中接地更上层的包含性概念。作者发现强叶级对齐并不能迁移到祖先概念：在独立解码下直接对祖先概念打分，八个扁平方法在四个NTU RGB+D划分上平均叶级Top-1为57.86–73.29%，但平均全路径准确率仅6.15–31.21%。

Method: 提出Hyperbolic Hierarchical Semantic Prototype (H²SP) 框架，在双曲空间Lorentz模型中表示动作分类树的每个节点。1）不逐节点学习自由向量，而是在单一树级门控下通过递归父子测地插值合成层次感知原型；2）在已见类别上做切空间语义与分布对齐，将叶原型锚定于骨架运动；3）引入软蕴含锥正则，鼓励有向的父子包含关系，从而把运动接地上传至祖先概念。评测涵盖独立解码与级联解码下的层次概念预测，以及无需重训练迁移到替代分类体系。

Result: 在四个划分上，H²SP在所评估方法中取得最佳的平均独立解码与级联解码全路径准确率，叶级Top-1为72.40%，具可比性。迁移到替代分类体系时，在每一划分与每种解码模式下均优于扁平基线及同树欧氏对照，表明其概念级对应关系可超越训练所用分类体系。

Conclusion: 扁平叶级对齐不足以支撑层次概念识别，需在双曲空间中显式建模层次结构。H²SP通过测地插值原型与蕴含锥正则实现叶级到祖先概念的接地传播，在层次全路径准确率与跨分类体系迁移上均表现优越。

Abstract: Zero-shot skeleton-based action recognition is evaluated almost exclusively on flat leaf labels, which leaves open whether a representation also grounds the broader concepts that contain an action in skeleton motion. We find that strong leaf alignment does not carry over to them: when ancestor concepts are scored directly under independent decoding, eight flat methods with 57.86-73.29% mean leaf Top-1 reach only 6.15-31.21% mean full-path accuracy across four NTU RGB+D splits. We propose the Hyperbolic Hierarchical Semantic Prototype ($H^2SP$) framework, which represents every node of an action taxonomy in the Lorentz model of hyperbolic space. First, instead of learning a free vector per node, $H^2SP$ synthesizes hierarchy-aware prototypes by recursive parent-child geodesic interpolation under a single tree-wide gate. Second, tangent-space semantic and distributional alignment on seen classes anchor the leaf prototypes in skeleton motion. Third, a soft entailment-cone regularizer encourages directional parent-child inclusion, propagating this motion grounding to ancestor concepts. We evaluate hierarchical concept prediction under independent and cascade decoding, and transfer to an alternative taxonomy without retraining. Across the four splits, $H^2SP$ attains the best mean independent and cascade full-path accuracy among the evaluated methods, at a comparable leaf Top-1 of 72.40%. Transferred to the alternative taxonomy, it outperforms both flat baselines and the same-tree Euclidean control across every split and decoding mode, indicating that its concept-level correspondence extends beyond the training taxonomy.

</details>


### [52] [RELAY: Learning Compatible Representations One Encoder at a Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31882)
*Yu Wang,Chuan Qin,Qiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RELAY：在增量式图库重编码中，用学习到的投影让同一查询向量同时匹配新旧文档向量；先训练新查询编码器、冻结后训练新文档编码器，回填完成后可选查询精炼，使未触碰图库的Recall@1提升8.66-10.23个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 增量重编码过程中新旧文档嵌入共存，导致查询向量与两版文档的匹配不一致，直接重编码会造成检索性能显著下降。

Method: 提出RELAY：先用学习到的投影，针对原始图库训练新的查询编码器；随后冻结查询端与新文档编码器的对应部分，训练新的文档编码器，使同一查询向量在单次重编码过程中同时打分两版文档；回填结束后可选做查询精炼，丢弃投影并复用已存储的文档向量。此外在嵌入固定条件下，对每个文档版本分别做分数校准。

Result: 在COCO与Flickr30K的三次文本到图像检索升级中，未触碰图库的Recall@1较原系统提升8.66-10.23个百分点；直接重编码场景下，RELAY在Qwen-to-Qwen升级中按Recall@1曲线下面积排名第一，在CLIP-to-Qwen中排名第二（原生打分与匹配校准两种设定下均如此）；查询精炼后，三次升级的最终Recall@1与常规联合训练相差均在0.97个百分点以内。

Conclusion: RELAY通过投影桥接新旧嵌入，有效缓解增量重编码期间的双版本共存问题；分版本分数校准可减小回填期Recall@1下降并改变最优回填AUC方法的归属；精炼后性能接近联合训练，验证方案的有效性与实用性。

Abstract: Old and new document embeddings coexist during incremental gallery re-encoding. RELAY trains a new query encoder with a learned projection against the original gallery, then freezes both to train the new document encoder. The same query vector scores both document versions throughout a single re-encoding pass. After backfill, optional query refinement drops the projection and reuses the stored document vectors. Across three text-to-image retrieval upgrades on COCO and Flickr30K, RELAY improves Recall@1 on untouched galleries by 8.66-10.23 percentage points over the original systems. For direct re-encoding, RELAY ranks first in both Qwen-to-Qwen upgrades and second in CLIP-to-Qwen by area under the Recall@1 curve, under both native scoring and matched calibration. With embeddings held fixed, calibrating scores separately for each document version can reduce drops in Recall@1 during backfill and change which method achieves the highest backfill AUC. After query refinement, final Recall@1 is within 0.97 percentage points of conventional joint training in all three upgrades.

</details>


### [53] [Rethinking Video Agents: When and Why Video Harnesses Work](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31883)
*Luoyuan Zhang,Xuanyu Zheng,Yancheng Long,Haoyang Xu,Bin Wen,Tingting Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文以 Video-Relook 为实验框架，研究视频理解中"自适应观察"（智能体式 harness）何时以及为何带来收益，发现其效果取决于任务对细粒度证据的需求与模型把交互转化为可靠答案修订的能力之间的匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频理解不仅取决于模型如何推理视觉证据，还取决于它观察到哪些证据。固定观察策略预先决定了证据范围，而智能体式 harness 允许模型在理解推进过程中主动寻求更多观察，从而有望释放固定输入无法触达的能力。然而"能够请求观察"并不等于"能有效利用观察"，因此需要厘清 harness 在何种条件下、因何机制带来提升，以及任务需求与模型交互能力如何共同塑造其收益。

Method: 提出/采用 Video-Relook 作为实验框架：让模型从统一的默认观察配置出发，自适应调整时间与空间观察范围；在多个模型与多类任务上分析性能表现与交互轨迹（evidence seeking 行为、答案修订的可靠性），并以 Video-MME-v2 作为泛化验证。

Result: 自适应观察在细粒度动作理解任务上带来显著提升，收益亦可延伸至 Video-MME-v2；在同一观察接口下，不同模型间收益差异明显，交互轨迹显示其在证据寻求与答案修订可靠性上存在相应差异；同一模型在不同任务上的收益亦不一致，从大幅提升到有限增益乃至性能回退不等。

Conclusion: harness 的有效性取决于"可获取有效信息的观察机会"与"模型将交互转化为可靠修订的能力"之间的对齐；应设计协调感知与推理、使观察自主性与任务需求和模型能力相匹配的视频 harness。

Abstract: Video understanding depends not only on how models reason about visual evidence, but also on which evidence they observe. Fixed observation policies determine this evidence in advance; agentic harnesses allow models to seek further observations as their understanding develops. This shift creates an opportunity to realize capabilities that fixed inputs leave untapped, yet the ability to request observations does not ensure their effective use. We investigate when and why video harnesses improve understanding, examining how task demands and models' capacity for interaction jointly shape their benefits. Using Video-Relook as an experimental framework, we let models adapt their temporal and spatial observations from a common default configuration, and analyze performance and interaction trajectories across models and tasks. Adaptive observation yields substantial gains on fine-grained action understanding, with benefits also extending to Video-MME-v2. Even under the same observation interface, gains differ substantially across models; interaction traces reveal corresponding differences in evidence seeking and the reliability of answer revision. The benefits also vary across tasks for the same model, ranging from substantial improvements to limited gains and regressions. Together, these findings support an account in which harness effectiveness depends on the alignment between opportunities for informative observation and the model's ability to turn interaction into reliable revisions. They motivate video harnesses that coordinate perception and reasoning, matching observational autonomy to task demands and model capabilities.

</details>


### [54] [Open-OmniCoach: Evidence-Enhanced Trajectory Learning for Omni Video Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31891)
*Weisong Liu,Xiangtai Li,Haochen Wang,Haiquan Wen,Zhaoxiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Open OmniCoach 框架，包含高质量数据集 OmniCoach-240K 与新的智能体强化学习方法 SEPO（步级证据引导策略优化），用于训练"寻找证据"的全模态视频智能体，得到 Open OmniAgent 系列模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全模态视频智能体需要学会在全模态设定下可靠地查找、关联并纠正任务相关证据，而现有多样化 QA 数据虽多却不够；现有数据流水线对任务相关证据的控制能力有限，且对上述能力的监督信号较弱。

Method: (1) 构建 OmniCoach-240K 数据集，含 248.2K 交互轨迹（230.7K 多片段轨迹 + 17.4K 反思轨迹），采用三阶段数据流水线，同时利用细粒度全模态证据与智能体自身错误；(2) 提出 SEPO 强化学习方法，基于证据覆盖率与时序定位的改进提供轮级（turn-level）信用分配。

Result: 在多个视频与视听理解任务上显著优于基线。Open OmniAgent-30B 在九个基准上平均准确率较 Qwen3-Omni 提升 6.3 个百分点，其中 LVOmniBench 提升 12.2 点、LVBench 提升 13.3 点。

Conclusion: 轨迹数据与 SEPO 方法结合可有效训练证据寻求型全模态视频智能体，验证了 Open OmniCoach 框架的有效性。

Abstract: Training capable omni video agents requires more than diverse question-answer pairs: agents must learn to reliably find, connect, and correct task-relevant evidence in omni-setting. However, we argue that existing data pipelines provide limited control over task-relevant evidence and weak supervision for these capabilities. In this work, we present Open OmniCoach, a new framework for evidence-seeking omni video agents. It contains a high-quality dataset, OmniCoach-240K, and a new agentic reinforcement learning (RL) method, Step-Level Evidence-Guided Policy Optimization (SEPO). For the former, OmniCoach-240K comprises 248.2K interaction trajectories, including 230.7K multi-span trajectories and 17.4K reflection trajectories. We design a three-stage data pipeline by leveraging both fine-grained omni-evidence and the agent's own errors. Meanwhile, compared with other RL baselines, SEPO provides turn-level credit based on improvements in evidence coverage and temporal localization. Applying both trajectory data and the SEPO method, we obtain Open OmniAgent models. The resulting models outperform the baselines across multiple video and audio-visual understanding tasks by significant margins, demonstrating the effectiveness of the Open OmniCoach framework. In particular, Open OmniAgent-30B improves average accuracy over Qwen3-Omni by 6.3 percentage points across nine benchmarks, with gains of 12.2 and 13.3 points on LVOmniBench and LVBench, respectively.

</details>


### [55] [Loupe: Reinforcing Knowledge-Intensive Video Generation Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31895)
*Kai Zeng,Ruichuan An,Zhen Fang,Xi Fang,Han Lyu,Moran Li,Yingchen Yu,Yifan Dai,Bohan Zeng,Quanqing Xu,Zhengzhou Zhu,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出视频生成智能体 Loupe，通过检索并验证文本与视觉证据构建证据驱动的时序视频提示；并引入基于量规的步骤级信用（RBC）训练方法，配合自建 Search-VBench 评测，显著提升知识密集型视频生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识密集型视频生成的提示常缺失事实、外观与时序关系，需要检索补充证据；但现有搜索流程依赖人工设计策略、缺乏自适应规划，且传统训练面临视频级反馈昂贵、轨迹级信用分配粗粒度的问题。

Method: 1) Loupe 智能体：检索并验证文本与视觉证据，构建带参考图的证据驱动时序提示；2) Rubric-Based Credit (RBC)：利用提示特定的事实、视觉、时序要求，在视频渲染前评估中间搜索与规划步骤；3) RBC-SFT 按步骤质量对 assistant token 学习加权；RBC-GRPO 在最终规划奖励之外引入步骤级信用，且 RL 阶段无需渲染视频；4) 构建 Search-VBench（150 条、7 类）用于搜索导向评测。

Result: 在三种视频生成器上四个维度均提升，MiniMax-H3 总分由 32.80 升至 70.67，优于未训练搜索流程；完整 RBC 训练比标准 SFT+轨迹级 GRPO 高 5.27 分；人类评测及 Sci-VBench、KIVI-Bench 结果验证生成质量、科学依据、事实精度与有用性提升。

Conclusion: 步骤级量规信用可高效替代昂贵的视频级反馈，实现更细粒度的规划优化，为知识密集型视频生成提供有效的检索-规划框架与评测基准。

Abstract: Knowledge-intensive video generation remains challenging because prompts often omit the facts, appearances, and temporal relationships required for accurate generation. While search-based workflows can retrieve missing evidence, manually designed strategies limit adaptive planning, and conventional training suffers from costly video-level feedback and coarse trajectory-level credit assignment. In this paper, we present Loupe, a video generation agent that retrieves and verifies textual and visual evidence to construct evidence-grounded temporal video prompts with reference images. We further introduce Rubric-Based Credit (RBC), which leverages prompt-specific factual, visual, and temporal requirements to evaluate intermediate search and planning decisions before video rendering. Building on RBC, RBC-SFT reweights assistant-token learning by step quality, while RBC-GRPO incorporates step-level credits alongside a final planning reward, without rendering videos during RL. To address the lack of dedicated search-oriented evaluation, we construct Search-VBench, which contains 150 held-out prompts across seven categories. Experiments show that Loupe improves all four evaluation dimensions across three video generators, raising the overall score of MiniMax-H3 from 32.80 to 70.67 and outperforming an untrained search workflow. Ablation experiments show that full RBC training outperforms standard SFT followed by trajectory-level GRPO by 5.27 points. Human evaluations and results on Sci-VBench and KIVI-Bench further demonstrate improved generation quality, scientific grounding, factual precision, and helpfulness. Our project page is available at https://zengkaiya.github.io/Loupe/.

</details>


### [56] [Beyond Expert Replay: Cross-Instruction Anchoring for Continual Vision-Language-Action Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31903)
*Ziyang Chen,Dehua Chen,Shaoguang Wang,Xu Yan,Hao Huang,Tao Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Cross-Instruction Anchoring (XIA)，在有限经验回放条件下通过指令条件下的响应保持来缓解VLA模型持续学习中的灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型需持续学习新操作技能同时保持旧指令跟随能力。经验回放（ER）可缓解灾难性遗忘，但每任务仅保留少量历史专家帧，导致大量旧指令响应缺乏直接监督，出现"早期指令触发后来学到的竞争目标"的结构性失败模式。

Method: XIA在每个任务边界冻结上一策略作为教师，将旧指令与当前观测配对，并匹配教师对这些配对的响应，从而在无需额外专家回放数据的情况下将监督扩展到存储样本之外，与专家回放联合使用。

Result: 在多种VLA架构与三个LIBERO套件上，相同历史专家数据预算下，成功率提升、遗忘减少；RoboCasa与真实世界实验进一步验证有效性。

Conclusion: 指令条件下的响应保持可作为专家回放的有效补充，在严格回放预算下提升VLA持续学习的稳定性与泛化性。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models need to continually acquire new manipulation skills while retaining the ability to follow previously learned instructions. Experience replay (ER) can mitigate catastrophic forgetting, but retaining only a small number of historical expert frames per task leaves many old-instruction responses without direct supervision. Under limited replay, we observe a structured failure mode in which an earlier instruction can trigger a subsequently learned, competing goal. To address this gap, we propose Cross-Instruction Anchoring (XIA), which supplements expert replay with instruction-conditioned response preservation. At each task boundary, XIA freezes the previous policy as a teacher and matches its responses to old instructions paired with current observations. This extends supervision beyond stored historical examples without requiring additional expert replay data. Experiments across multiple VLA architectures and three LIBERO suites demonstrate improved success rates and reduced forgetting compared with ER under identical historical expert-data budgets. Additional RoboCasa and real-world experiments further support the effectiveness of XIA.

</details>


### [57] [Your VLM Knows What to Inspect: Semantic Probe-Guided Adaptive Focusing for GUI Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31909)
*Ruixiao Shi,Fu Feng,Yucheng Xie,Jing Wang,Xin Geng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练框架SPARK，利用VLM"坐标预测错误但仍能描述可检查证据"的能力，通过语义探针引导自适应区域查看，提升GUI grounding精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GUI grounding需在语义上下文中解析细粒度视觉细节；增加额外视图可改善定位，但其价值取决于视图暴露何种证据及保留多少上下文。作者观察到VLM即使坐标预测错误，仍能描述值得检查的视觉证据，希望将这种"语义理解"转化为定向视觉观测。

Method: SPARK（Semantic Probe-guided Adaptive Region seeKing），免训练框架：探索探针描述相关上下文与区分性目标细节，验证探针识别评估新假设所需证据；利用其空间注意力指导视图放置，依据上下文响应确定裁剪范围，借助观测历史鼓励互补性检查；最终通过不确定性加权投票融合多次预测。

Result: 在ScreenSpot-Pro上，SPARK相较单视图推理分别提升13.9、25.2、16.1个百分点（Qwen3-VL、Qwen2.5-VL、UI-TARS），且在各骨干上优于所有对比的测试时方法；在另外四个基准上取得一致增益，表明跨多样界面与grounding任务有效。

Conclusion: 语义探针是将冻结VLM对"应检查何处"的理解转化为更准确GUI grounding的有效机制。

Abstract: GUI grounding requires resolving fine visual details within the context that gives them meaning. Additional views can improve localization, but their value depends on what evidence they expose and how much context they preserve. We explore a capability of vision-language models (VLMs): even when their coordinate predictions are incorrect, they can describe visual evidence worth inspecting. We introduce \textbf{SPARK}, \textit{\textbf{S}emantic \textbf{P}robe-guided \textbf{A}daptive \textbf{R}egion see\textbf{K}ing}, a training-free framework that turns this semantic guidance into targeted visual observations. Exploration probes describe relevant context and distinguishing target details, while verification probes identify evidence needed to assess emerging hypotheses. Their spatial attention guides view placement, contextual responses determine crop extent, and observation history encourages complementary inspection. The resulting predictions are combined through uncertainty-weighted voting. On ScreenSpot-Pro, SPARK improves grounding accuracy over single-view inference by 13.9, 25.2, and 16.1 percentage points with Qwen3-VL, Qwen2.5-VL, and UI-TARS, outperforming all compared test-time methods for each backbone. Consistent gains across four additional benchmarks demonstrate its effectiveness across diverse interfaces and grounding tasks. These results establish semantic probing as an effective mechanism for turning a frozen VLM's understanding of what to inspect into more accurate GUI grounding.

</details>


### [58] [HiGS: A Hierarchical Architecture for Fast Differentiable 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31918)
*Dawid Pająk,Martin Bisson,Rodolfo Lima,François Trudel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HiGS 解耦 3DGS 中分块、光栅化与并行单元三种角色的粒度：以粗宏块分区、细渲染块光栅化、有界深度排序批次处理，实现负载自适应的渲染与训练，速度更快、内存更省且图像质量持平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS 将同一屏幕 tile 同时用作分区单元、光栅化单元和并行工作单元，但分区偏好大块、光栅化偏好小块；且每 tile 一个工作单元导致最密 tile 决定整体帧时间，负载不均视角效率低下。

Method: 1) 分级分块：Gaussian 在粗粒度宏块上分区，在其内部细粒度渲染块上光栅化，并以有界、深度排序的批次处理，密集区域发出更多批次而非延长单批次；2) 同一批次承载重建：合成过程提供各批次独立求导所需的透射率与颜色；3) 将优化器融合进反向传播，直接存储更新的参数与动量减半，并把外观更新推迟到视角之外。

Result: 在评估的所有已发表方法中渲染速度最快，负载不均视角下优势最明显；重建速度更快、内存占用更低，图像质量持平，并保持精确的前到后 alpha 合成。

Conclusion: 将分区、光栅化与并行粒度分离可消除最密 tile 的瓶颈，批次化设计同时服务于渲染与可微重建，配合优化器与反向融合的显存优化，形成渲染与重建统一的 3DGS 架构。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) makes one screen tile the cell of partitioning, the cell of rasterization, and the unit of parallel work. Partitioning favors large tiles and rasterization small ones, and with one unit per tile the densest tiles set the frame time. We present Hierarchically Tiled Gaussian Splatting (HiGS), a unified architecture for rendering and reconstruction that gives each role its own granularity: Gaussians are partitioned over coarse macro-tiles, rasterized over the fine render tiles within them, and processed in bounded depth-sorted batches. A dense region issues more batches instead of one longer unit, so frame time follows the work in a view rather than its densest tile. The same batches carry reconstruction: compositing supplies the transmittance and color needed to differentiate each one independently, leaving optimizer memory traffic as the dominant training cost. We fuse the optimizer into the backward pass, halve the parameters and moments it stores and updates directly, and defer appearance updates outside the view. HiGS renders scenes faster than every published method we evaluate, by the widest margin on views with uneven load, and reconstructs them faster and in less memory, at equal image quality and with exact front-to-back alpha compositing.

</details>


### [59] [DUALingo: A Dual-Hand Instruction-Guided Dataset for Affordance Grounding on 3D Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31919)
*Ming-Lun Lee,Yi-En Li,Feng-Dian Su,Ming-Han Lee,Kun-Ru Wu,Krzysztof Czarnecki,Yu-Chee Tseng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DUALingo——首个大规模任务条件化、角色感知的双臂3D affordance grounding数据集，用全自动基础模型流水线生成271K任务/71K物体的双臂互补热图标注，微调后模型aIoU从10.3提升至37.8。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D affordance数据集多只标注单一交互区域，而日常操作常需双手互补协同；人工标注双手协同接触区域难以规模化，存在数据缺口。

Method: 构建全自动流水线（基于开源权重基础模型，无需人工标注）：将每个任务表示为两个协同的、手别无关的角色，各在物体表面以热图形式定位；产出271K自由形式任务、71K物体的大规模数据集，并用专家评分与经校验的LLM判官验证标注质量。

Result: 在DUALingo上训练后，现有3D affordance模型可从自然语言指令同时定位两个协同角色；最佳模型aIoU由零样本10.3提升至37.8（三倍以上）；机器人操作演示验证了预测双臂affordance的可执行性。

Conclusion: DUALingo为学习日常双臂affordance提供了可扩展基础，验证了自动生成双角色标注的可行性与下游可操作性。

Abstract: Affordance grounding localizes where an agent should interact with an object to perform a task. Many everyday manipulation tasks, however, require bimanual affordance, in which two hands assume complementary and cooperative roles to accomplish a shared goal. Existing 3D affordance datasets primarily ground single interaction regions, and manually annotating dual-hand, cooperative contacts does not scale. To address this gap, we introduce DUALingo, a large-scale dataset for task-conditioned, role-aware bimanual 3D affordance grounding on diverse everyday objects. DUALingo represents each task as two cooperative, hand-agnostic roles, each grounded as a heatmap on the object surface. It contains 271K free-form tasks on 71K objects, generated by a fully automated pipeline of open-weight foundation models without human labeling. Expert ratings and a validated LLM judge confirm the quality of the resulting annotations. Trained on DUALingo, existing 3D affordance models learn to ground both cooperative roles from natural-language instructions, with the best-performing model achieving a more than threefold improvement in aIoU over the zero-shot baseline, from 10.3 to 37.8. Robotic manipulation demonstrations illustrate the actionability of the predicted bimanual affordances in performing cooperative tasks. These results position DUALingo as a scalable foundation for learning everyday bimanual affordances. Project page: https://nycu-pair-labs.github.io/DUALingo/

</details>


### [60] [TANDEM: Temporal Anchor Densification With Reliability-Aware Memory for Lightweight Sparse Depth Completion in Endoscopic Robotic Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31922)
*Weikuan Qiu,Jiale Hou,Chongzhao Xu,Jinlian Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TANDEM，一种因果式稀疏深度补全框架，利用视频流中历史测量重投影来稠密化内窥镜深度，仅5.58M参数即在两个数据集上刷新SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内窥镜机器人手术需要稠密准确的度量深度以支持导航、器械定位与组织交互，但内窥镜视频缺乏可靠度量深度；通过跟踪、飞行时间传感或器械接触获得的度量点仅覆盖每帧极小比例像素，因此稀疏深度补全成为迫切且未解决的难题。

Method: 提出Temporal Anchor Densification with Reliability-Aware Memory（TANDEM）：将历史测量重投影到当前视角以补回单帧稀疏深度中缺失的度量证据；用显式记忆对每个重投影测量以解析的重观测、时龄与运动视差因子打分，且不覆盖当前测量；再由紧凑网络通过可靠性加权梯度积分与频率门控参考先验重建稠密深度；整体采用轻量、因果设计。

Result: 在C3VD结肠镜与StereoMIS腹腔镜数据集上，以5.58M参数将RMSE相比最强基线分别降低11.7%与32.0%，达到SOTA精度；在内窥镜位姿估计所报告的位姿误差量级下仍保持准确。

Conclusion: 视频时序信息的可靠性感知重投影稠密化可在轻量架构下显著提升内窥镜稀疏深度补全精度与鲁棒性，代码将在接收后开源。

Abstract: For endoscopic robotic surgery, accurate dense depth perception serves as a foundational capability, which enables navigation, instrument localization, and tissue interaction. However, endoscopic video lacks reliable metric depth, while metric measurements obtained via tracking, time-of-flight sensing, or instrument contact only cover a small fraction of pixels in each frame, rendering sparse depth completion an urgent and unsolved challenge. In this work, we propose Temporal Anchor Densification with Reliability-Aware Memory (TANDEM), a causal sparse depth completion framework for endoscopic video. It adopts a lightweight design and leverages the video stream: past measurements re-projected into the current view recover the metric evidence unavailable within a single sparse frame. To keep this evidence interpretable, accurate, and efficient, an explicit memory scores each re-projected measurement with analytic re-observation, age, and motion-parallax factors without overriding current measurements. A compact network then reconstructs depth via reliability-weighted gradient integration and a frequency-gated reference prior. We evaluate TANDEM on the C3VD colonoscopy and StereoMIS laparoscopy datasets. With only 5.58 M parameters, TANDEM reduces the root mean square error by 11.7% and 32.0%, respectively, compared with the strongest baseline, and achieves state-of-the-art accuracy. It also remains accurate under pose errors of the magnitude reported for endoscopic pose estimation. Code will be released at https://github.com/Wk-cpu-art/TANDEM upon acceptance.

</details>


### [61] [Decompose, Verify, Evolve: Physically Grounded Shape Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31924)
*Wenchao Ma,Rui Yu,Fan Zhu,Jiachen Liu,Sharon X. Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PhysDec：利用预训练视觉语言模型的语义与常识推理，从少量图像预测材质感知的材质-基元分解，提升物理合理性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有形状分解方法主要按几何保真度训练，缺乏物理合理性约束，易产生不连通部件或物理不稳定的重建。

Method: 提出agentic框架PhysDec。PhysDec-Loop为智能体流水线，推理部件结构与材质，利用参考点云作几何反馈、物理验证器引导迭代优化；通过自我进化得到PhysDec-Evolved，从历史纠正中学习，推理时无需点云反馈与纠正循环。

Result: PhysDec-Loop在几何保真度、物理合理性与视觉质量上优于以几何为中心的基线；PhysDec-Evolved以显著更低的推理成本接近其性能。

Conclusion: 将VLM语义与常识推理引入形状分解，可在少量图像输入下获得材质感知、物理合理的基元分解，并通过自进化实现低成本部署。

Abstract: Shape decomposition represents 3D objects as a compact set of geometric primitives for interpretable modeling and simulation. However, existing decomposition methods are trained primarily for geometric fidelity, without explicit constraints on physical plausibility, and can therefore produce disconnected parts or physically unstable reconstructions. We present PhysDec, an agentic framework that draws on a pretrained vision-language model's semantic understanding and commonsense reasoning to predict material-aware primitive decompositions from a few images. Within this framework, PhysDec-Loop is an agentic pipeline that reasons about part structure and materials, using a reference 3D point cloud for geometric feedback and physical verifiers to guide iterative refinement. Through self-evolution, we further develop another agentic pipeline, PhysDec-Evolved, which learns from prior corrections to predict primitive decompositions from images without point-cloud feedback or a correction loop at inference. Experiments show that PhysDec-Loop improves geometric fidelity, physical plausibility and visual quality over geometry-focused baselines, while PhysDec-Evolved approaches its performance at substantially lower inference cost.

</details>


### [62] [DraftKV: Protecting Persistent Worlds through Hierarchical Streaming Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31944)
*Xiaoling Zhou,Haopeng Li,Yufa Zhou,Rui Ding,Yu Li,Jun Lin,Enze Xie,Chunhua Shen,Xuan Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DraftKV：一种免训练框架，通过几何锚定+查询相关性的草稿检索、CPU卸载与tile级流式注意力，实现交互式视频世界模型的长程一致性与高效历史KV检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视频扩散模型依赖历史KV缓存维持长期一致性，但随历史增长，KV检索与注意力计算带来显著延迟与显存开销，难以在单卡上支持高质量交互式世界生成。

Method: 1) 基于几何锚定与查询条件相关性检索历史KV（draft relevance），先几何定位再细化；2) CPU卸载降低GPU显存占用；3) streaming CG-Attention：tile级流式注意力，将CPU→GPU的KV传输与注意力计算重叠。理论分析给出草稿相关性精确复现全分辨率块相关性的均值分支（偏差仅限于β加权max分支），并以Top-K选择重叠率界定检索效用损失，同时证明池化描述子相似的块无法仅凭相机位姿区分，故需先几何锚定。

Result: 在多种模型家族上，生成质量与一致性优于其他CPU卸载式KV检索方法，端到端加速3.27×，峰值GPU显存降低42.2%；可在单张H100上用SolarWM-33B实现含历史KV检索的高质量交互式世界生成。

Conclusion: 将几何锚定的草稿检索与流式CG-Attention结合，可在免训练条件下兼顾长程一致性与推理效率，使大规模视频世界模型的历史KV检索在单卡上可行。

Abstract: Interactive video world models enable action- and camera-controlled exploration, yet struggle to maintain consistent spatial geometry and appearance when revisiting previously explored locations. Historical KV caches help auto-regressive video diffusion models maintain long-term consistency, but their retrieval and attention computation introduce substantial latency as history grows. We propose DraftKV, a training-free framework that retrieves historical KV caches based on geometric grounding and query-conditioned relevance, while combining CPU offloading with tile-wise streaming attention to reduce GPU memory usage and inference latency. Theoretically, we show that draft relevance reproduces the mean branch of full-resolution block relevance exactly, so its deviation is confined to the $β$-weighted max branch, and we bound retrieval utility loss in terms of the measured Top-K selection overlap. We further show that draft relevance cannot distinguish blocks with similar pooled descriptors regardless of camera pose, motivating geometric grounding before refinement. Then, we introduce streaming CG-Attention, which overlaps CPU-to-GPU KV transfers with attention computation through tile-wise streaming, hiding transfer latency while maintaining bounded GPU memory usage. Experiments across diverse model families show that DraftKV achieves superior generation quality and consistency while achieving $3.27\times$ end-to-end speedup and 42.2% peak GPU memory reduction over other CPU-offloaded KV cache retrieval methods. Notably, DraftKV enables high-quality interactive world generation with historical KV retrieval on a single H100 GPU using SolarWM-33B.

</details>


### [63] [Beyond Rigid Hands: Deforming Skin into Contact-Consistent Hand Meshes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31950)
*Seonghwan Park,Min-Gyu Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出将刚性手部网格"提升"为接触一致网格的优化方法，通过微调关节/手腕、皮肤形变与时间一致性约束，并训练轻量头预测接触区域引导形变，在不改变手部模型与估计器的前提下同时保持位姿精度与物理合理性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手-物数据集与估计器将手表示为刚性铰接网格，导致网格悬浮于物体之上或穿透物体，而真实手部软组织会贴合物体表面。已有refiner为提升物理合理性保持网格刚性、仅调整位姿，牺牲了位姿精度。

Method: 求解一个联合优化：(i) 轻微调整关节与手腕使手指接触物体；(ii) 对皮肤进行形变，使穿透区域落至物体表面；(iii) 在时间上保持形变一致。由于形变仅作用于已接近物体的皮肤，进一步在提升后的标注上训练一个轻量预测头，预测估计手应接触的位置以引导形变。

Result: 对三个已发表的估计器，该方法使贴合物体表面的皮肤面积增加一倍以上，而位姿几乎不变；学习到的接触预测头通过闭合估计留下的接触间隙，进一步提升了抓握稳定性。

Conclusion: 现有手-物数据集与估计器无需更换手部模型或重新训练估计器，即可获得接触一致的手部表示。

Abstract: Hands grasp objects with soft skin. A fingertip flattens against a mug, and a palm curves around a bottle. Yet hand-object datasets and estimators represent the hand as a rigid articulated mesh, which hovers above or penetrates the object where skin would press against it. Existing refiners keep the mesh rigid and adjust only the pose, trading pose accuracy for physical plausibility. We ask whether a rigid hand can instead touch the object as skin does while staying close to its original pose. We lift annotated or estimated rigid hands to contact-consistent meshes through an optimization that (i) slightly adjusts the joints and wrist to bring the fingers onto the object, (ii) deforms the skin so that penetrating regions settle onto the surface, and (iii) keeps this deformation consistent over time. Since deformation reaches only skin already near the object, we further train a lightweight head on the lifted annotations to predict where an estimated hand should touch and steer the deformation there. Unlike existing refiners, the lift more than doubles the skin lying on the object surface for three published estimators while barely changing the pose. Guidance from the learned head further improves grasp stability by closing contact gaps that the estimate leaves open. Existing hand-object datasets and estimators can thus adopt contact-consistent hands without changing the hand model or retraining the estimators.

</details>


### [64] [VLG-Drive: Unifying Vision-Language and Geometric Priors for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31952)
*Junge Zhang,Jie Zhu,Yanqi Jia,Xiangyu Xu,Zixun Huang,Wenbin He,Xin Li,Jiachen Li,Liu Ren*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VLG-Drive 冻结视觉语言模型与几何基础模型，仅训练融合接口与规划器，即可在 NavHard/NavTest/HUGSIM 上取得领先的闭环规划性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶规划器同时需要场景语义（由 VLM 提供）与空间结构（由几何基础模型从视觉历史中恢复），而针对规划任务微调这两类模型成本高、易破坏其预训练能力。

Method: 保持 VLM 与几何模型完全冻结，仅用轨迹标签训练融合接口与规划器。接口在相机网格每个单元上，通过“几何锚定的残差”将成对的 VLM 特征与几何特征融合为单一联合 token；再由指令（command）条件化的规划器读取这些 token 预测自车轨迹。

Result: 扰动初始状态下 NavHard 达 31.78 EPDMS（对比方法中最高）；仅用相机在 NavTest 达 88.27 PDMS；逐点融合在两个基准上均优于独立 VLM 流与几何流；420 个 HUGSIM 场景闭环驾驶中优于所有对比规划器。

Conclusion: 在冻结的视觉语言与几何基础模型融合特征上训练轻量规划器，是替代“为规划而适配任一模型”的有效方案。

Abstract: A driving planner needs both scene semantics, which vision-language models (VLMs) provide, and spatial structure, which geometric foundation models recover from visual history. We present VLG-Drive, which keeps both models frozen and trains only a fusion interface and a planner from trajectory labels. At each camera-grid cell, the interface fuses the paired VLM and geometry features into one joint token through a geometry-anchored residual, and a command-conditioned planner reads these tokens to predict the ego trajectory. VLG-Drive achieves 31.78 EPDMS on NavHard under perturbed initial states, the highest among the compared methods, and 88.27 PDMS on NavTest from cameras alone. Pointwise fusion outperforms independent VLM and geometry streams on both benchmarks. In closed-loop driving on 420 HUGSIM scenarios, VLG-Drive outperforms all compared planners. These results indicate that a lightweight planner trained on fused features of frozen vision-language and geometric foundation models is an effective alternative to adapting either model for planning.

</details>


### [65] [TACTUS: Unifying Surgical Action Understanding in Time and Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31956)
*Lurui Wang,Hao Ding,Chenhao Yu,Chenyan Jing,Jiru Xu,Ruoqi Zuo,Liwei Mei,Chang Liu,Lalithkumar Seenivasan,Mathias Unberath*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TACTUS：面向完整手术动作的统一标注框架与基准数据集（141视频、22,995片段、290万帧），并配套参考网络TactusNet，评估语义/时序/空间预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手术基准多以任务特定目标定义监督，未能完整刻画动作语义、器械运动与组织交互之间的关系，缺乏对动作全过程时空理解的统一标注。

Method: 提出TACTUS数据集与基准，统一标注器械与动作身份、嵌套的动作/交互时间窗、器械特定关键点轨迹及组织区域跟踪；构建"人工标注+点跟踪+模型生成空间提示"的双层（Gold/Silver）流水线，重标注5个公开来源的141个视频；并设计参考网络TactusNet，从共享视频特征联合预测语义、时序与空间任务。

Result: 共22,995个动作片段、290万帧，其中15,668个Gold片段（人工空间提示+复核）与7,327个Silver片段（人工语义与时间标注+模型空间提示）。在TactusNet及13个已发表基线中，逐头编码器微调下器械-动作准确率达95.8%；最佳时序召回为IoU 0.7下46.6%，接触Dice为0.351；引入Silver将默认TactusNet的pair准确率由93.8%提升至95.5%。

Conclusion: TACTUS为学习与评估器械-组织交互提供了统一基础，但时序定位与接触分割仍具挑战；该工作为未来手术自动化奠定数据与评测基础。

Abstract: Advancing surgical automation requires a comprehensive spatio-temporal understanding of how instruments act on tissue throughout an action. Existing surgical benchmarks, however, largely define supervision through task-specific targets, leaving the relationships between action semantics, instrument motion, and tissue interaction incompletely specified. To address this limitation, we introduce TACTUS (Tool, Action, Contact and Trajectory Understanding in Surgery), a new dataset and benchmark that establishes a unified annotation framework for complete surgical actions. The framework jointly specifies tool and action identities, nested action and interaction windows, instrument-specific landmark trajectories, and tissue-region tracks. To construct these annotations at scale, we develop a two-tier pipeline combining human annotation, point tracking, and model-generated spatial prompts. Using this pipeline, we re-annotate 141 videos from five public sources, producing 22,995 action clips spanning 2.9 million frames. The dataset comprises 15,668 Gold clips with human spatial prompts followed by review and re-checking, and 7,327 Silver clips combining human semantic and temporal annotations with model-generated spatial prompts. We further introduce TactusNet, the companion reference network, to benchmark semantic, temporal, and spatial prediction from shared video features. Across TactusNet and 13 published baselines, tool-action accuracy reaches 95.8% with per-head encoder fine-tuning, while the best temporal recall and contact Dice reach 46.6% at temporal IoU 0.7 and 0.351, respectively. Moreover, adding Silver improves the default TactusNet's pair accuracy from 93.8% to 95.5%. Together, these contributions establish a foundation for learning and evaluating the instrument-tissue interactions underlying future surgical automation.

</details>


### [66] [SPAR: Self-Referenced World-Model Rewards for Physical Alignment in Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31958)
*Junyi Zhang,Qichao Wang,Wenzhe Qian,Yuanchuan Wang,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SPAR框架，用"自参照"方式将世界模型预测用于推理期物理对齐，通过静态片段参考与时空奖励精修提升生成视频的物理合理性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成视觉质量高但物理动态不合理；用潜世界模型做评估时，预测与观测特征的高一致性可能只反映场景易预测性，而非动态更符合物理。

Method: SPAR将每个视频的预测误差与其自身帧重复构造的静态片段误差比较，得到内容匹配的参照；并引入统一时空奖励精修：空间精修强调局部预测差异，时间精修抑制短暂误差尖峰并以预测与观测特征变化的一致性补充预测误差。

Result: 多基准评测显示SPAR提升文生视频与图生视频续写的物理合理性，使生成动态更贴合真实世界事件的物理规律。

Conclusion: 自参照+时空精修的世界模型预测可作为有效的推理期奖励信号，缓解内容难度偏置，提升生成视频物理一致性。

Abstract: Generating videos with physically plausible dynamics remains challenging despite advances in visual quality. Latent world models offer predictive signals for evaluating physical plausibility, but higher agreement between predicted and observed features may reflect easier-to-predict scene content rather than more plausible dynamics. We propose \textbf{S}elf-Referenced \textbf{P}hysical \textbf{A}lignment \textbf{R}ewards (\textbf{SPAR}), a framework that uses world-model predictions for inference-time physical alignment. SPAR compares each video's prediction error with that of static clips constructed by repeating its own frames, providing a content-matched reference for evaluating generated dynamics. To account for the uneven spatial and temporal distribution of physical interactions, we introduce a unified \textbf{spatiotemporal reward refinement} strategy. \textbf{Spatial refinement} emphasizes localized prediction discrepancies, while \textbf{temporal refinement} moderates brief error peaks and supplements prediction errors with agreement between predicted and observed feature changes. Evaluations across multiple benchmarks demonstrate that SPAR improves the physical plausibility of both \textbf{text-to-video generation} and \textbf{image-to-video continuation}, bringing generated dynamics into closer alignment with the physical regularities governing real-world events. Additional results and resources are available at https://gavin-jy.github.io/spar-page/

</details>


### [67] [Visual Thinking: Training-Free Active Perception for Counting and Localizing Hard-to-Find Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31973)
*Emil Kielar,Pratik Chaudhari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 视觉思维：无需训练，VLM与SAM 3迭代主动感知循环，显著降低计数误差并提升定位AP，单GPU约5.91秒/图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉基础模型仅凭文本提示即可精确分割，但难以恢复小目标、被遮挡目标或文本描述刻画不佳的目标；视觉语言模型能结合上下文提出更丰富描述，却缺乏精确分割与定位能力。二者互补，故需设计机制融合其优势。

Method: 提出Visual Thinking——无需训练的框架，将VLM与SAM 3通过迭代主动感知循环结合。每轮VLM基于原图、候选裁剪及历史提出简短名词短语；控制器依据原提示与提案的嵌入相似度、基于熵的验证优先级与提示新颖性排序；SAM 3据短语检测目标；跨轮关联检测并以加权均值更新置信度（窄提示检测权重更大）；将高置信检测作为正视觉样例回馈SAM 3，并引入图像分块以恢复杂乱场景中的小目标。

Result: 在CARPK、FSCD-147、PixMo-Count与OmniCount(Fruits)上评估计数与定位。相比SAM 3，FSC-147上计数MAE降低约18、CARPK约8；FSCD-147与CARPK上box AP提升超过12。固定或无分块时性能与extra-high推理强度的Claude Opus 5.5相当，单桌面GPU约5.91秒/图，而API调用Claude约46.53秒/图。

Conclusion: 将VLM的语义推理与SAM 3的精确分割通过迭代主动感知循环耦合，可在免训练条件下兼顾计数精度与定位质量，并在效率上显著优于大模型API方案，为开放词汇计数与定位提供可行范式。

Abstract: Vision foundation models can precisely segment objects based solely on a text prompt. However, they struggle to recover objects that are small, occluded or whose appearance is poorly captured by the original textual description. Vision language models can examine the image and its context, they can propose richer descriptions of the objects to be detected. But these models struggle with precise segmentation and localization. We introduce Visual Thinking, a training-free framework that combines a vision-language model with Segment Anything Model 3 (SAM 3) through an iterative active perception loop. At each iteration, the VLM proposes short noun phrases using the image, candidate crops and history from past iterations. A controller ranks these phrases using embedding similarity between the original prompt and the proposal, an entropy-based verification priority and the novelty of the prompt. Next, SAM 3 uses prompts to detect objects in the image. We associate detections across iterations and update each object's confidence score using a weighted mean of scores obtained from SAM 3 with a larger weight for detections using narrower prompts. We supply confident detections as positive visual exemplars for SAM 3, and incorporate image tiling to recover small objects in cluttered images. We evaluate counting and localization on CARPK, FSCD-147, PixMo-Count, and OmniCount (Fruits). Compared with SAM 3, Visual Thinking reduces the counting mean absolute error (MAE) by about 18 on FSC-147 and about 8 on CARPK. It improves box average precision (AP) by more than 12 on FSCD-147 and CARPK. Visual Thinking, with fixed or no tiling, performs on par with Claude Opus 5.5 at extra-high reasoning effort, while running on single desktop GPU in about 5.91 seconds per image, compared to about 46.53 seconds per image for Claude through its API.

</details>


### [68] [CodeHOI: Code with Agents for Physically Plausible 4D Hand-Object Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31976)
*Seonghwan Park,Inho Chang,Min-Gyu Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CodeHOI 是首个以程序形式生成物理合理 4D 手-物交互（HOI）的智能体框架，通过多智能体分工显式建模物质物理，并配套提出 CodeHOI-Bench 基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 4D 手-物交互建模对理解操作行为至关重要，且本质依赖物理：手只能通过接触移动物体，不同材质响应各异。现有方法要么忽略物理，要么只仿真很窄的一类物质；程序化方法虽能描述任意物质的物理，但现有编程智能体却忽略了驱动交互的"手"，二者之间存在空白。

Method: 提出 CodeHOI 智能体框架，将 4D HOI 写成程序：1）Object Agent 先按各物质物理属性构建物体；2）HOI Agent 以接触关系指定手部，并借助成熟的物理求解器仿真各类物质；3）Evaluator Agent 依据物理定律校验结果。同时构建 CodeHOI-Bench，含 100 个重建与生成任务，覆盖四类物质。

Result: 使用现成的前沿模型，CodeHOI 能为每一类物质生成合理的交互，且迁移到独立物理引擎后仍保持合理性。

Conclusion: CodeHOI 使编程智能体能够跨多样物质写出物理合理的 4D 手-物交互，弥合了"物理可编程"与"手部驱动交互"之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Modeling 4D hand-object interaction (HOI) is fundamental to understanding manipulation. This requires physics, since hands move objects only by touch and each material responds differently. Yet existing methods ignore this physics or simulate only a narrow range of matter. Programs can instead describe any matter with its physics, but coding agents omit the hand that drives the interaction. We close this gap with CodeHOI, to our knowledge the first agentic framework that writes physically plausible 4D HOI as programs. To write one, an Object Agent first builds each object with the physics of its matter. An HOI Agent then specifies the hands by their contacts and simulates each kind of matter with an established physics solver. Finally, an Evaluator Agent checks each result against physical laws. We also introduce CodeHOI-Bench with 100 reconstruction and generation tasks across four kinds of matter. With off-the-shelf frontier models, CodeHOI writes interactions that are plausible for every kind of matter and remain so when transferred to an independent physics engine. It thereby enables coding agents to write physically plausible HOI across diverse matter.

</details>


### [69] [Distill to Loop: Turning Pretrained Diffusion Transformers into Looped Models with Adjustable Depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31977)
*Chenru Wang,Shengwei An*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Dooop方法，将预训练非循环扩散Transformer蒸馏为共享权重的循环学生模型，把蒸馏建模为向教师的定点迭代，使单模型在测试时可运行任意循环次数，并支持深度维高阶ODE求解与循环引导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有循环扩散Transformer需从头训练，重复承担完整预训练成本；而已有强预训练非循环扩散Transformer未被利用。

Method: 以教师权重初始化循环学生块；将蒸馏形式化为向教师收敛的定点迭代，每次循环输出均对齐教师，且每次循环视为深度方向ODE的一步；测试时无需重训即可运行任意循环数，并可在深度维使用扩散采样的高阶ODE求解器；同时构建不同循环数输出间的循环引导（loop guidance）。

Result: SD3-medium上，约37%教师参数的循环学生用2–4次循环在GenEval、ImageReward、PickScore、HPSv2.1上平均追平教师；2次循环仅用约58%教师算力。四步SD3-medium上，循环引导将四项指标均值从88.2提升至99.2。

Conclusion: Dooop可高效复用预训练扩散Transformer，以更少参数与算力达到教师水平，并提供免重训的灵活循环数控制与低开销的性能增益。

Abstract: Looped transformers reuse one block of shared weights along the depth of the network. Recent looped diffusion transformers are trained from scratch, which repeats the full pretraining cost although strong pretrained non-looped diffusion transformers exist. We propose Distill to Loop (Dooop), which distills a pretrained non-looped diffusion transformer into a looped student whose blocks are initialized from the teacher's. We cast distillation as a fixed-point iteration toward the teacher, in which the output after every loop is trained toward the teacher, and each loop is one step of an ordinary differential equation in depth.
