Conclusion: 证据落地的定律专属指标加 agentic 协调，可有效评估机器人世界模型生成视频的物理可行性，并为不同生成器的物理违规特征提供可比较的诊断视角。

Abstract: Evaluating the physical plausibility of generated videos is critical for reliable robotic planning with world models. These videos inform action proposal and assessment before real-world execution, so undetected physical violations can lead to erroneous assessments of action feasibility and safety. Yet current video generators often produce physically implausible interactions. Detecting such violations directly with vision-language models (VLMs) remains challenging as physical verification requires evidence-grounded spatiotemporal reasoning beyond high-level semantic understanding. We develop five law-specific metrics for evaluating physical plausibility by combining spatiotemporal evidence from segmentation, tracking, 3D geometry, or appearance comparisons with targeted VLM reasoning: Contact fidelity, Object persistence, Solidity, Robot deformation, and Object deformation. Building on this metric suite, we introduce RoboProbe, an agentic evaluation framework that coordinates metric execution and selectively refines evaluations through constrained actions. To evaluate the physical plausibility of robot world models, we further introduce RP-Bench, with 761 human-labeled generated videos and 130 real demonstrations of 239 manipulation tasks from 12 robot datasets. On RP-Bench, all five metrics achieve AUROCs above 0.85 and outperform the evaluated learned judges, prior evaluators, and a frames-only Gemini baseline. Applying these metrics to five video generators reveals distinct patterns of physical violations across models, in both violation types and frequencies.

</details>


### [104] [SoccerNarrate: Event-Grounded Streaming Soccer Commentary with Macro-Window Preference Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32153)
*Zihan Jia,Zhilin Dai,Zhengming Zhang,Min Yang,Zhenpeng Huang,Jiaqi Li,Caixia Sun,Xi Chen,Liang Li,Junlan Feng,Gangshan Wu,Limin Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 SoccerNarrate，从数据、训练、评测三方面将低延迟流式解说与事件驱动的专业足球解说结合，提升全场比赛解说的事件覆盖与事件对齐精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有片段式足球解说模型在未剪辑全场比赛上产生延迟、重复、同步差的解说；流式视频语言模型虽低延迟，但偏向泛化描述，无法及时准确提及进球、红黄牌、换人、越位等关键事件。

Method: 1) 构建 SoccerNarrate-Data：大规模时间锚定数据集，含词级ASR对齐、质量过滤与实体校准；2) 宏窗口偏好对齐（MWPA）：对同一流式前缀的因果多步 rollout 进行比较以对齐完整事件级解说语义，推理仍保持秒级；并用事件错配的人工解说作反事实负样本，强调事件语义而非解说风格；3) 提出事件中心基准 SoccerNarrate-Eval，基于时间约束的事件蕴含。

Result: 全场比赛实验表明，相较强离线与流式基线，SoccerNarrate 在事件覆盖率和事件对齐精度上均有提升；代码与模型已开源。

Conclusion: 通过数据、偏好对齐训练与事件中心评测的协同设计，可在不增加推理延迟的前提下实现事件落地、专业化的低延迟足球流式解说。

Abstract: Existing clip-based soccer commentary models often produce delayed, repeated, or poorly synchronized narration on untrimmed full-match videos. Recent streaming video-language models make low-latency full-match narration feasible, but they often favor general descriptions rather than professional, event-grounded soccer commentary. They may describe nearby actions while failing to mention key events such as goals, cards, substitutions, and offsides correctly and on time. In this paper, we present SoccerNarrate to bridge low-latency streaming narration with event-grounded professional soccer commentary across data, training, and evaluation. First, we construct SoccerNarrate-Data, a large-scale time-anchored dataset with word-level ASR alignment, quality filtering, and entity calibration. Second, we perform macro-window preference alignment (MWPA), which aligns the model with complete event-level commentary semantics by comparing causal multi-step rollouts from the same streaming prefix while keeping second-level inference unchanged. We further use event-mismatched human commentaries as counterfactual negatives to emphasize event semantics over commentary style. Third, we introduce SoccerNarrate-Eval, an event-centric benchmark based on temporally constrained event entailment. Full-match experiments show that SoccerNarrate improves event coverage and event-aligned precision over strong offline and streaming baselines. Code and models are released at https://github.com/MCG-NJU/SoccerNarrate.

</details>


### [105] [Mind the Band: The Hidden Frequency Variable in Geometric RoPE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32171)
*Seonghyun Jin,Youngmin Kim,Jong Chul Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文指出几何RoPE中"频率带"设置未被标准化，不当设置会反转基准排名，并给出频率带与头维度匹配的实用指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多视图几何RoPE（如RayRoPE）把3D相机几何编码为按频率带旋转坐标，但频率带沿用2D RoPE默认值或随头维度指数扩张，缺乏统一规范，可能导致模型规模/头尺寸变化下性能与排名不稳定。

Method: 在RealEstate10K新视图合成与双视图深度估计上做受控实验：固定模型宽度仅改变头尺寸（144通道 vs 48通道），并人为设定频率带范围（如在48通道下以4个频率填充243倍区间替代3倍默认），分析顶部频率（每8像素块0.3–3次旋转）与深度区间平均造成的频率前向阻尼、梯度未阻尼不对称，并验证仅屏蔽高频深度梯度的效果。

Result: 47M模型（144通道）中RayRoPE与最强基线持平，25M模型（48通道）落后1.2 dB；填补宽频率带可挽回0.9 dB。过窄频带普遍模糊对应关系；顶部频率位于每8像素块0.3–3次旋转（256×256下10–90次）时三种投影编码均稳定；屏蔽高频深度梯度可挽回90–93%的PSNR损失。

Conclusion: 频率带应显式设计：在图像坐标下设定频带、在小头中填充频率跨度、避免无条件的同视图阻尼；未来多视图基准应报告并匹配频率带。前向平滑不保证反向稳定。

Abstract: Geometric rotary position encodings (RoPE) incorporate 3D camera geometry into multi-view attention by rotating token features according to viewing rays, depths, and reprojected image coordinates. Each coordinate is rotated across a progression of frequencies--its frequency band--yet existing implementations leave this band to inherited defaults, either retaining standard 2D RoPE's top frequency or letting the frequency span grow exponentially with the attention head dimension. We show that this unstandardized setting can reverse benchmark rankings. In novel view synthesis on RealEstate10K, RayRoPE matches the strongest baseline in the 47M model (144-channel heads), yet trails by 1.2 dB in the 25M model (48-channel heads), and exhibits the same reversal when varying head size alone at fixed model width. Populating a 243x range with four frequencies instead of the 3x default at 48 channels recovers 0.9 dB of that gap. Across view synthesis and two-view depth estimation, overly narrow bands consistently blur correspondence, whereas all three projection-based encodings perform reliably when their top frequency lies within 0.3--3 rotations per 8-pixel patch (10--90 rotations across 256x256 RealEstate10K). At the opposite extreme, overly wide bands degrade RayRoPE through a forward-backward asymmetry: averaging rotations over a predicted depth interval damps high frequencies in the forward pass, yet leaves their gradients with respect to predicted depth undamped. Blocking only those high-frequency depth gradients recovers 90--93% of the lost PSNR, showing that forward smoothing does not guarantee backward stability. Guided by this analysis, we distill practical guidelines--setting bands in image coordinates, populating spans in small heads, and avoiding unconditional same-view damping--and advocate reporting and matching frequency bands in future multi-view benchmarks.

</details>


### [106] [MediaStormBench-CameraLog: Can Multimodal Models Identify a Camera's Log Curve?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32172)
*Ming Ma,Wenhao Hu,Qichao Wang,Yuhao Wang,Junpeng Zhao,Qiyi Wang,Fangrui Zhang,Zhiqian Lai,Shanxin Guan,Haoyi Pei,Hongan Chen,Rui Gong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出多模态基准 MediaStormBench 及其首个子集 CameraLogBench，考察模型能否在无元数据条件下从专业素材中识别 Log 曲线；结果显示 11 个模型在真实素材上的准确率仅略高于多数类猜测，且严重依赖场景与品牌先验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 影视制作流程中，Log 曲线定义于厂商专有的对数压缩公式，正确还色依赖该曲线，但它仅存于易在转码与团队交接中丢失的元数据里。调色师需手工逐一试错匹配波形图，误选会导致整段素材色调偏移。因此需要评估多模态模型能否仅凭画面恢复曲线。

Method: 构建基于专业拍摄素材的 CameraLogBench，并设计四项解耦控制：(1) 去除正确选项的探测项以暴露先验猜测；(2) 由同一套厂商公式实现生成的调色负样本；(3) 同场景合成帧；(4) 上下文规范摘要；(5) 免训练公式基线，用以将语义先验与光度测量分离。评测覆盖 11 个模型。

Result: 真实素材准确率仅略高于始终猜最常见曲线的基线；移除正确选项后模型几乎不弃答，显示对场景与品牌先验的强依赖。提供厂商规范在标定色卡上显著提升识别，但在缺乏光度参考的自然图像上几乎无帮助，暴露出"读规范"与"测像素"之间的鸿沟。

Conclusion: 现有模型尚未真正具备从像素进行光度测量的能力，CameraLogBench 揭示了先验依赖与规范利用之间的脱节，为多模态模型在专业影像流程中的可靠应用设定了诊断性评测基础。

Abstract: MediaStormBench is a multimodal benchmark for real-world film and television production, with each subset drawn from MediaStorm's day-to-day work. We release its first subset, MediaStormBench-CameraLog (CameraLogBench), on a common foundational task in footage ingest and initial color grading: identifying the Log curve of a clip without metadata. Cameras record Log footage by compressing a wide range of scene light into code values with a vendor-defined logarithmic curve, and color can be restored correctly only once that curve is known. The curve, however, is recorded only in file metadata, easily lost in transcoding and team hand-offs. A colorist then has to try candidate curves one by one against a waveform scope, and a wrong choice shifts the tones and colors of the whole clip. Recovering the curve from the image alone is challenging, because vendor curves cluster tightly in code value and are coupled with unknown exposure. CameraLogBench is built on professionally shot footage and tests whether multimodal models can identify the curve, with a probe that removes the correct option to expose guessing from priors. Because every candidate curve is strictly defined by a published vendor formula, we derive four controls from one implementation of these formulas: graded negatives, synthetic frames of identical scenes, in-context specification digests, and a training-free formula baseline, which together decouple semantic priors from photometric measurement. Across 11 models, real-footage accuracy barely exceeds always guessing the most frequent curve; when the correct option is removed, models almost never abstain, revealing heavy reliance on scene and brand priors. Supplying the vendor specification sharply improves recognition on calibrated charts but hardly helps on natural images without a photometric reference, exposing a gap between reading a specification and measuring pixels.

</details>


### [107] [LoopFit: Retrofitting Pretrained Diffusion Transformers with Recurrent Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32175)
*Xiaocheng Lu,Haoxi Li,Dazhao Du,Tao Han,Jian Liu,Hao Chen,Bohai Gu,Siyu Yan,Jingcai Guo,Jie Zhang,Song Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LoopFit 通过在 DiT 中循环复用 Transformer block 增加单步精修深度，并用 Residual Attention Modulation、Residual Relay Loss 与噪声相关深度调度提升推理计算效率，在视觉谜题任务上以相近代价取得优于增加采样步数的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 增加采样步数虽能提升 diffusion transformers 的推理表现，但每步去噪预测的深度不变，单步内的表示细化能力受限；循环复用模块可提升单步计算量，但对预训练模型做改造时，需让原本为固定深度学习的 block 在自我重复应用中有效精修状态，关键在于利用额外传递纠正局部误差而不破坏前序传递建立的结构。

Method: 提出 LoopFit 框架：(1) Residual Attention Modulation (RAM)：利用每个图像 patch 在一次传递中的特征变化，对下一次传递中该 patch 的注意力输入进行缩放与平移；(2) Residual Relay Loss (RRL)：训练时对跨多次传递仍持续存在预测误差的 patch 赋予更高权重；(3) 噪声相关深度调度：高噪声阶段用 4 次传递、低噪声阶段用 1 次传递以加速采样。

Result: 在同等计算代价下，经 4 次传递训练的骨干网络用循环深度比增加采样步数解决更多问题；在 12 个视觉谜题设置中，LoopFit 解出 89.8% 的棋盘，而最强基线为 87.8%；增益主要来自迷宫与冰湖场景中的长路径追踪。

Conclusion: 反馈引导的循环深度机制可有效替代部分采样步数扩容，实现更高效的 DiT 推理计算缩放，且在长程路径推理类任务上优势明显。

Abstract: Scaling inference computation offers a way to improve diffusion transformers (DiTs), but taking more sampling steps leaves the depth of each denoising prediction unchanged. Recurrently reusing Transformer blocks increases computation within each step, allowing further refinement of hidden representations. Retrofitting pretrained models enables this capability without repeating pretraining, but requires blocks learned for fixed-depth computation to refine states produced by their own repeated application. The challenge is to use each additional pass to correct local errors without disrupting the structure established by earlier passes. We introduce LoopFit, which uses feedback from earlier passes to guide refinement at each image patch. Residual Attention Modulation (RAM) uses each patch's feature change in one pass to scale and shift its input to attention in the next. During training, Residual Relay Loss (RRL) gives greater weight to patches whose prediction errors persist across passes. A noise-dependent depth schedule speeds up sampling by using four passes at high noise and one at lower noise levels. At comparable cost, backbones trained with four passes solve more problems using recurrent depth than extra sampling steps. Across 12 visual puzzle settings, LoopFit solves 89.8% of boards, compared with 87.8% for the strongest baseline evaluated on all 12. Most of the gain comes from tracing long paths on mazes and frozen lakes.

</details>


### [108] [DocPristine: Content-Aware Document Appearance Enhancement under Real-World Mixed Degradations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32176)
*Zihan Huang,Junle Liu,Zhonghao Ye,Yuyi Zhang,Peirong Zhang,Hanlin Li,Yongxin Shi,Lianwen Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DocPristine：利用冻结的文档视觉编码器多层特征作为内容感知引导，经AMFF、HGC、SWCA融合注入像素复原网络，实现SOTA文档外观增强并提升下游解析；同时构建DocACE合成/真实数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机拍摄文档常含高光、阴影、模糊等混合退化，扭曲背景并遮挡文字细节。现有方法依赖输入导出的视觉线索（严重退化时不可靠）或基于LLM的退化诊断（缺乏空间引导）。作者发现预训练文档视觉编码器在退化文档中仍能关注文本区域，可提供内容感知引导。

Method: 自适应融合冻结文档视觉编码器的多层特征，并注入像素复原网络；具体由三模块组成：自适应多层特征融合（AMFF）、层次门控条件注入（HGC）、移位窗口交叉注意力（SWCA，跨窗口边界从邻近位置获取结构线索以恢复细粒度文字细节）。另构建DocACE基准：合成混合退化训练集DocACE-Synthetic与含人工修复GT及文档解析标注的真实测试集DocACE-Real。

Result: 在三个基准上的综合实验表明，DocPristine取得SOTA的外观增强质量，并持续改善下游文档解析性能。

Conclusion: 预训练文档编码器的内容感知特征可作为稳健的空间引导，有效支撑混合退化文档的外观增强；所提方法与数据基准将公开，为DAE及下游解析研究提供支撑。

Abstract: Camera-captured documents often exhibit mixed degradations, such as highlights, shadows, and blur, that distort backgrounds and obscure text details. Document appearance enhancement (DAE) aims to mitigate these degradations and improve readability. Existing document appearance enhancement methods rely either on input-derived visual cues that can become unreliable under severe degradation or on LLM-based degradation diagnosis that lacks spatial guidance. Notably, we find that pretrained document visual encoders continue to attend to text regions in degraded documents, providing content-aware guidance. Based on this insight, we propose DocPristine, which adaptively fuses multi-layer features from a frozen document visual encoder and injects them into a pixel restoration network. To integrate these features into a pixel restoration network, DocPristine combines Adaptive Multi-Layer Feature Fusion (AMFF), Hierarchical Gated Conditioning (HGC), and Shifted-Window Cross-Attention (SWCA). Specifically, SWCA draws on structural cues from neighboring locations across window boundaries to recover fine text details.We also introduce DocACE, with a synthetic mixed-degradation training set (DocACE-Synthetic) and a real-world test set (DocACE-Real) containing manually restored ground-truth images and document parsing annotations. Comprehensive experiments across three benchmarks demonstrate that DocPristine achieves state-of-the-art appearance enhancement quality and consistently improves downstream document parsing. Code, models, and data will be publicly available.

</details>


### [109] [ThinkMorph-SSL: Scaling Interleaved Multimodal Reasoning with Pretext Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32183)
*Jiawei Gu,Linjie Li,Yiming Liu,Yunzhuo Hao,Huichen Wang,Guanzheng Chen,Zhichao Peng,Luxin Xu,Xinyu Zhang,Li Luo,Disen Lan,Zican Hu,Mingyang Song,David Valente,Alex Jinpeng Wang,Yafu Li,Ganqu Cui,Zhengyuan Yang,Qizhe Xie,Yejin Choi,Ranjay Krishna,Yu Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ThinkMorph-SSL两阶段训练方案，以图像本身作为监督信号，让模型习得"图文交错推理"（生成中间图像并据此继续推理）的能力，无需人工标注的特定任务示范。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类既用语言也用图像推理。让模型生成中间图像并以此引导后续推理，可形成交错多模态推理能力；但训练此类能力通常依赖任务特定的示范数据，采集成本高。作者希望找到一种不依赖人工示范的监督来源。

Method: 两阶段自监督式训练配方：(1) pretext reasoning（前置任务推理）——把着色、去噪等视觉前置任务转化为无指令的交错轨迹，模型描述受损/变换后的图像、生成恢复或规范化图像、再解释结果，形成"描述—生成—解读"的中间图像推理链；(2) 使用自动构造的示范进行问题驱动的指令微调。

Result: 在相同指令微调预算下，加入前置任务推理训练的模型优于仅做指令微调的模型；完整配方在十二个基准上相对基座模型平均提升11.33个准确率点。

Conclusion: 由图像本身衍生的监督可培养交错推理所需的视觉技能，并提升模型从任务特定示范中的学习效率，为降低多模态推理训练对人工示范的依赖提供了可行路径。

Abstract: People reason with pictures as well as words. Interleaved multimodal reasoning gives models a similar way to solve problems by creating intermediate images and using them to guide further reasoning. Training models with this ability usually relies on task-specific demonstrations, which are difficult to collect. We introduce ThinkMorph-SSL, a two-stage training recipe that uses images themselves as a source of supervision. The first stage, pretext reasoning, converts visual pretext tasks, such as image colorization and denoising, into instruction-free interleaved traces. Models describe a corrupted or transformed image, generate a restored or canonical image, and interpret the result. The second stage uses automatically constructed demonstrations for question-driven instruction tuning. At the same instruction-tuning budget, models trained with pretext reasoning outperform those trained with instruction tuning alone. The full recipe improves over the base model by an average of 11.33 accuracy points across twelve benchmarks. These results show that supervision derived from images can build the visual skills needed for interleaved reasoning and improve learning from task-specific demonstrations.

</details>


### [110] [SolarFilNet-V4: Multi-Scale Transformer Vision with Physics-Informed Adaptive Post-Processing for Solar Filament Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32193)
*Indra Agustian,Ruvita Faurina*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SolarFilNet-V4，一种针对GONG H-alpha日冕/色球数据中太阳暗条分割的框架，结合轻量PVT-v2-B1主干、各向异性曲线条带卷积与双频前馈模块，在MAGFiLO 1.0基准上取得0.4340 OOF Panoptic Quality与0.380公开榜分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 太阳暗条失稳可触发日冕物质抛射与空间天气灾害，但地面GONG H-alpha 2048×2048图像存在对比度低、纤维湍流、精细倒钩结构及标注边界分歧等问题，导致单个暗条实例分割困难。

Method: 以轻量PVT-v2-B1为骨干，配合各向异性曲线条带卷积（k=15）与双频前馈模块跟踪延伸磁绳并捕捉细窄中心脊线；附加1024×1024全分辨率浅层卷积分支保留高频边缘；采用掩码、中心线脊、倒钩边界与像素对比表示的多任务监督；测试时使用8重二面体D4增强、双线性上采样至原生分辨率，并施加活动自适应后处理以区分宁静/活动日面。

Result: 官方基准上折外验证Panoptic Quality为0.4340，公开排行榜得分0.380；模型15.46M参数，单次前向224.34 GFLOPs，参数量较标准ResNet-34 U-Net基线减少36.8%。

Conclusion: 该工作表明轻量Transformer骨干与曲线结构先验卷积及多任务监督的组合，可在参数量显著降低的条件下有效完成暗条实例分割，适用于真实地面观测基准。

Abstract: Solar filaments are cool, dense chromospheric plasma structures held by magnetic field lines above polarity inversion lines. Their destabilization triggers coronal mass ejections and severe space weather hazards. Segmenting individual filaments from ground-based GONG H-alpha observations (2048 x 2048 pixels) is challenging due to low contrast, turbulent fibrils, delicate barb structures, and annotator boundary variance. In this technical report, we describe SolarFilNet-V4, a targeted segmentation framework developed for the MAGFiLO 1.0 benchmark (Solar Filament Segmentation Challenge 2026). Our method couples a lightweight Pyramid Vision Transformer (PVT-v2-B1) backbone with anisotropic curvilinear strip convolutions (k=15) and dual-frequency feed-forward blocks (DFFN) to follow extended flux ropes while capturing thin central spines. A shallow full-resolution convolutional branch operating at 1024 x 1024 preserves high-frequency edge details and mitigates transformer downsampling. We train the network using multi-task supervision over masks, centerline spines, barb boundaries, and pixel-contrastive representations. At test time, we apply 8-fold dihedral test-time augmentation (D4), bilinearly upscale predictions to the native 2048 x 2048 grid, and apply an activity-adaptive post-processing routine that calibrates morphological joining parameters for quiet versus active solar disks. On the official benchmark, SolarFilNet-V4 achieves an out-of-fold validation Panoptic Quality of 0.4340 and a Public Leaderboard score of 0.380. The model contains 15.46M parameters and requires 224.34 GFLOPs per forward pass, reducing parameter count by 36.8% compared to standard ResNet-34 U-Net baselines.

</details>


### [111] [What to Change, What to Keep: Experience-Evolving Agents for Video Recasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32196)
*Shiying Zhang,Yuxia Zhu,Huaze Xue,Shengqi Dang,Yizhen Wu,Yijie Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RecastAgent：一种经验引导的智能体框架，在冻结模型权重下通过"规划器+经验积累"实现源条件视频重铸，并构建了67视频/134任务的基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频重铸需将现有视频的实体与环境改造为新的视觉世界，同时保留核心动作与交互；但协同编辑易破坏原始事件结构，导致变换不连贯。

Method: 提出经验引导的智能体框架，模型权重冻结。Experience-Guided Planner 在定性视觉评判下选择编辑提示与输入视频状态，支持基于部分进展继续或回溯以保留源结构；Experience Accumulation 模块将完成轨迹蒸馏为带条件、证据关联的原则，记录编辑策略及其有效范围。另构建含67个源视频、134个配对规格任务、覆盖6类创意类别的基准。

Result: 在Bernini与Ditto上优于单次生成与智能体重写基线；积累经验可提升留出测试视频表现，并可零样本迁移至FiVE-Bench与VE-Bench等外部基准。

Conclusion: 经验引导的智能体规划与经验积累机制可在不微调模型的前提下有效提升视频重铸的结构一致性与泛化能力。

Abstract: Video recasting aims to transform the entities and environments of existing videos into new visual worlds while preserving their essential actions and interactions. However, achieving coherent transformations remains challenging, as coordinated edits can disrupt the original event structure. In this paper, we introduce RecastAgent, an experience-guided agentic framework for source-conditioned video recasting that operates under frozen model weights. Our framework employs an Experience-Guided Planner to choose edit prompts and input video states under qualitative visual critique, allowing the agent to build upon partial progress or backtrack to preserve source structure. Across tasks, an Experience Accumulation module distills completed trajectories into conditional, evidence-linked principles that record both an editing strategy and its scope of validity. We additionally construct a benchmark of 67 source videos with 134 paired-specification tasks across six creative categories for evaluation. Experiments demonstrate that RecastAgent outperforms single-pass and agentic rewriting baselines across Bernini and Ditto. Furthermore, accumulated experience improves performance on held-out test videos and transfers zero-shot to external benchmarks like FiVE-Bench and VE-Bench.

</details>


### [112] [MoGe-4D: Efficient, Flexible, and Unified Dense 4D Reconstruction and Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32198)
*Ruicheng Wang,Linlin Xin,Sicheng Xu,Jianfeng Xiang,Zelong Lv,Yu Deng,Yue Dong,Guangzhong Sun,Tong Zhang,Jiaolong Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MoGe-4D 是一个前馈式统一几何与运动预测的4D动态场景重建框架，通过“自中心+他中心”成对动态点图的有序查询，一次性实现对相机位姿闭式求解、深度预测、动态配准与稠密3D跟踪，并配套提出 RigIDTrack 数据管线以低成本获得稠密4D监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从RGB视频重建动态场景需同时推理3D几何与跨视角运动，但现有方法要么将重建与跟踪拆成碎片化流水线，要么面临稠密时空解码的昂贵开销；此外监督稠密4D查询需要连续3D对应关系，原始轨迹存储成本过高。

Method: 1) 以有序查询的成对点图（allocentric 与 egocentric dynamic point maps）统一表示：每个查询将源像素同时映射到目标时刻的相机与自中心两套坐标系，从而闭式求解相对相机位姿，并复用同一表征服务深度、动态配准与稠密跟踪；2) 解耦场景建模与查询解码：高容量上下文编码器一次性缓存粗粒度全局时空特征，轻量查询编码器以灵活分辨率快速解码稠密点图；3) 提出 RigIDTrack 合成数据管线与数据集，将表面动态压缩为刚性正则化的仿射体，以约15%的存储开销（相对RGB-D视频）恢复任意帧的精确3D轨迹。

Result: 在多类基准上，MoGe-4D 取得最先进的稠密3D跟踪性能，重建精度与专用3D基础模型相当，且运行速度显著快于既有4D基线。

Conclusion: 以成对动/静态点图查询统一几何与运动，并以解耦编码-解码保证稠密查询效率，是兼顾精度与速度的可行4D重建范式；刚性仿射体压缩为稠密4D监督提供了实用的数据方案。

Abstract: Reconstructing dynamic scenes from RGB video requires reasoning about both 3D geometry and motion across viewpoints, yet existing methods either split reconstruction and tracking into fragmented pipelines or face prohibitive dense spatiotemporal decoding costs. We introduce MoGe-4D, a feed-forward framework that unifies geometry and motion prediction via ordered queries of paired allocentric and egocentric dynamic point maps. Each query maps source pixels to a target time simultaneously in both camera frames, resolving relative camera pose in closed form while flexibly serving depth, dynamic registration, and dense 3D tracking. For efficient dense querying, MoGe-4D decouples scene modeling from query decoding: a high-capacity context encoder caches coarse global spatiotemporal features once, while a lightweight query encoder rapidly decodes dense point maps at flexible resolutions. Furthermore, as supervising dense 4D queries demands continuous 3D correspondences whose raw trajectory storage is prohibitive, we introduce RigIDTrack, a synthetic data pipeline and dataset that compresses surface dynamics into rigidity-regularized affine bodies, recovering exact arbitrary-frame 3D trajectories with $\sim15\%$ storage overhead over RGB-D video. Across diverse benchmarks, MoGe-4D achieves state-of-the-art dense 3D tracking, matches dedicated 3D foundation models in reconstruction, and runs substantially faster than prior 4D baselines.

</details>


### [113] [SplatTok: Data-Driven Compression of 3D Gaussian Splats with Scene-Level Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32202)
*Sai Karthikey Pentapati,Shashank Nelamangala Sridhara,Birendra Kathariya,Peng Yin,Guandao Yang,Alan C. Bovik*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SplatTok 是一种学习式的场景重合成编解码器，利用"不同基元布局可产生近似相同渲染视图"的自由度，在解码端重新合成高斯基元，从而实现高效压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D高斯泼溅支持实时渲染但存储开销大。已有压缩方法要么编码给定泼溅的基元，要么为每个场景独立优化紧凑表示；而一个高斯泼溅仅是场景的一种"实现"，不同基元排布可渲染出几乎相同的视图，这一冗余未被利用。

Method: 在数千真实场景上训练：transformer 将现有泼溅编码为紧凑的、空间锚定的隐令牌，共享解码器合成一组新的标准高斯基元，且不保持与输入的对应关系；表示通过渲染监督而非基元重建来学习。压缩时采用量化感知的隐变量搜索，仅优化场景码、冻结网络，以弥补编码器残差。单一模型在 DL3DV 上训练。

Result: 仅用输入泼溅的渲染图进行隐变量搜索（不用原始训练图像）时，SplatTok 以 4–6.5 倍更小的文件达到与前馈编解码器 FCGS 相当的 PSNR，压缩率约 75–110 倍；使用训练图像时，以 13–41% 更小的文件超越逐场景方法 HAC++ 的 PSNR。

Conclusion: 该模型可泛化到含数百万高斯的新场景，并支持多种目标码率而无需重训练，验证了解码端场景重建范式的有效性与通用性。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian splats enable real-time rendering but require substantial storage. Existing compression methods either encode the primitives of a given splat or optimize a compact representation independently for each scene. Yet a Gaussian splat is only one realization of a scene: different arrangements of primitives can produce nearly identical rendered views. We exploit this freedom on the decoding side with SplatTok, a learned scene-resynthesis codec. Trained across thousands of real-world scenes, a transformer encodes an existing splat into compact, spatially anchored latent tokens, and a shared decoder synthesizes a new set of standard Gaussian primitives without preserving correspondence to the input. The representation is learned through rendering supervision rather than primitive reconstruction. At compression time, a quantization-aware latent search refines only the scene code, keeping the networks frozen, and closes most of the encoder's remaining error. On standard benchmarks, using only renders of the input splat for the latent search, without the original training images, SplatTok matches the PSNR of the feed-forward codec FCGS with 4 to 6.5 times smaller files, compressing input splats about 75 to 110 times. With the training images, it exceeds the PSNR of the per-scene method HAC++ with 13 to 41% smaller files. A single model trained on DL3DV generalizes to unseen scenes with millions of Gaussians and supports multiple target rates without retraining.

</details>


### [114] [Seeing Is Not Reaching: Instruction Shortcuts Override Visual Grounding in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32206)
*Qing Zhang,Xuesong Li,Zhaoyuan Yang,Changlin Song,Xinyu Tian,Jing Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文发现视觉-语言-动作模型(VLA)在物体被移动后仍伸向训练位置，其根源是"场景捷径"与"指令捷径"两条快捷通路；作者用通路掩码实验证明视觉grounding本已存在但被捷径覆盖，并提出无需训练的Pathway-Separated Guidance (PSG)放大grounding、抑制捷径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA被认为应从VLM继承视觉grounding能力，但实践中物体被移动后模型仍去抓取训练时的位置。作者希望厘清这种失败是"grounding缺失"还是"grounding被覆盖"，即"Seeing Is Not Reaching"现象的机理。

Method: 在6个VLA上识别两类捷径：场景捷径（机械臂遵循场景原有任务而非新指令）与指令捷径（指令直接检索物体训练位置）。以π_{0.5}为例，尽管对物体名称的注意力跟随移动后的目标，指令捷径仍然持续存在。随后分别对"图像中介的grounding通路"和"指令直达动作的通路"进行掩码消融，以判定grounding未被使用还是被压制。基于此提出Pathway-Separated Guidance (PSG)，在无需训练、不依赖外部目标位置的情况下放大grounding信号并抑制捷径。

Result: 掩码grounding通路后对准移动目标的伸取消失，掩码指令直达通路后伸取增加，说明grounding已在引导动作但被经直达通路的指令捷径覆盖。在LIBERO-PRO Object的Position扰动下，PSG将目标到达率由22.0%提升至52.6%，任务成功率由16.6%提升至40.8%，同时基本保持原任务性能。

Conclusion: VLA的失效并非缺乏视觉grounding，而是其被指令捷径经直达动作通路压制；通过通路分离式引导可在不训练的前提下显式激活已有grounding并抑制捷径，显著改善位置泛化，但绝对性能仍有限，说明捷径问题尚未彻底解决。

Abstract: Vision-language-action models (VLAs) are expected to inherit visual grounding from vision-language models, yet often reach for an object's training location after it has been moved. Across six VLAs, we identify two shortcuts: a scene shortcut, where the arm follows the scene's original task despite a new instruction, and an instruction shortcut, where the instruction retrieves the requested object's training location. In $π_{0.5}$, the instruction shortcut persists even though object-name attention follows the moved target. We call this Seeing Is Not Reaching. To determine whether this grounding is unused or overridden, we separately mask an image-mediated grounding pathway and a direct instruction-to-action pathway. Masking the grounding pathway eliminates reaches to moved targets, whereas masking the direct pathway increases them. Thus, grounding already guides action but is often overridden by the instruction shortcut, which acts through the direct pathway. We introduce Pathway-Separated Guidance (PSG), which amplifies grounding and suppresses the shortcut without training or external target locations. On LIBERO-PRO Object under Position perturbations, PSG raises the target-reaching rate from 22.0% to 52.6% and task success from 16.6% to 40.8%, while largely preserving performance on the original tasks.

</details>


### [115] [FlashSegmentator: High-Throughput TotalSegmentator Inference without Loss of Accuracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32208)
*Yosuke Yamagishi,Tomohiro Kikuchi,Shouhei Hanaoka,Takeharu Yoshikawa,Osamu Abe*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FlashSegmentator 通过静态形状编译、流水线、批量滑窗推理和 GPU 后处理，在无需重训练的前提下显著提升 TotalSegmentator 的推理吞吐，同时在全部结构上保持 Dice 等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TotalSegmentator 在多器官分割中应用广泛，但参考实现推理吞吐低、云成本高；已有加速方案（降低分辨率或 FastSegmentator）往往牺牲精度或提升有限，因此需要一个无需重训练、不损失精度的开源高效推理管线。

Method: 回顾性研究，使用公开数据集（TotalSegmentator 测试集 73 例 CT、BTCV 30 例腹部 CT、512 例训练集 CT 用于并发进程分析、55 例 MRI）。在参考实现基础上加入静态形状编译、流水线化、批量滑窗推理与 GPU 后处理，并在 GH200、A100、T4 三种 GPU 上对比参考实现（含其低分辨率模式）与 FastSegmentator 的吞吐量。精度采用双单侧检验（Dice 等效性界值 ±0.01）。

Result: GH200、A100、T4 上分别达到每小时 3782、1810、270 例，为参考实现的 57.7、33.9、6.4 倍，为编译版 FastSegmentator 的 3.20、2.50、1.22 倍。所有 117 个结构在各 GPU 上 Dice 等效（平均差异 ≤1.2×10⁻⁵，P<.001），BTCV 检查层面亦等效。参考实现低分辨率模式在 A100 上仅 181/213 例每小时，且平均 Dice 下降 0.116/0.241。MRI 上吞吐提升 94 倍，50 个结构全部等效。A100 云成本由每万例 997 美元降至 29 美元。

Conclusion: FlashSegmentator 在数据中心级与入门级 GPU 上均可显著提升 TotalSegmentator 的吞吐量，且相比参考实现无分割精度损失，可作为开源方案公开使用。

Abstract: Purpose: To develop, evaluate, and publicly release FlashSegmentator, an open-source inference pipeline that increases the throughput of TotalSegmentator without retraining or loss of segmentation accuracy. Materials and Methods: This retrospective study used public datasets: 73 CT examinations from the TotalSegmentator test split (median age, 65 years; 37 male, 35 female, one unknown), 30 abdominal CT examinations from the Beyond the Cranial Vault (BTCV) dataset, 512 training-split CT examinations for concurrent-process analysis, and 55 MRI examinations. FlashSegmentator adds static-shape compilation, pipelining, batched sliding-window inference, and GPU postprocessing. Throughput was compared with the reference implementation (including its lower-resolution modes) and FastSegmentator on GH200, A100, and T4 GPUs. Dice agreement was tested with two one-sided tests (margin, +/-0.01). Results: FlashSegmentator processed 3782, 1810, and 270 examinations per hour on the GH200, A100, and T4 (57.7, 33.9, and 6.4 times the reference implementation and 3.20, 2.50, and 1.22 times compiled FastSegmentator). Dice scores were equivalent for all 117 structures on every GPU (mean difference <= 1.2 x 10^-5; P < .001) and at the examination level on BTCV. In contrast, the lower-resolution modes of the reference implementation processed 181 and 213 examinations per hour on the A100 but reduced mean Dice by 0.116 and 0.241. On MRI, throughput increased 94-fold with all 50 structures equivalent. Estimated A100 cloud cost per 10,000 examinations decreased from 997 to 29 US dollars. Conclusion: FlashSegmentator increased the throughput of TotalSegmentator on data center and entry-level GPUs without loss of segmentation accuracy compared with the reference implementation.

</details>


### [116] [EvoSearch: Self-Evolving Active Perception for Wide-Area Geospatial Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32214)
*Zhenghao Xie,Jing Xiao,Zihao Yu,Wanyi Yu,Liang Liao,Gui-Song Xia,Mi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出EvoSearch自进化主动感知框架，通过协同进化感知策略与探索程序压缩地理空间搜索轨迹，并构建GeoSearchBench基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地理空间搜索需在大范围、多尺度上发现证据。现有主动感知方法虽能自适应选择观测位置与尺度，但随着搜索区域扩大，搜索轨迹越来越长、代价越来越高；其关键局限在于仅以最终答案正确性为信号，无法指导"保留哪些观测"与"何时停止"，从而阻碍高效广域搜索。

Method: 提出EvoSearch框架，协同进化感知策略与探索程序：利用共享证据下已完成分支之间的质量-代价比较来训练策略，使其获取有用观测、避免冗余观测，并在证据充分时停止；再由更新后策略的执行反馈指导探索程序修订，使经验生成适配策略不断变化的观测需求。此外构建GeoSearchBench基准，通过关系搜索与区域搜索评估搜索质量与视觉处理效率。

Result: 实验表明，EvoSearch在保持整体搜索质量的同时缩短了搜索轨迹并显著减少视觉处理量，支持可扩展的广域地理空间主动感知。

Conclusion: 以质量-代价信号驱动策略与探索程序协同进化，可有效缓解广域搜索中轨迹冗长的问题，为可扩展的主动感知地理空间搜索提供可行路径。

Abstract: Geospatial search is fundamental to resource surveys and environmental monitoring, requiring the discovery of relevant evidence across large geographic areas and multiple spatial scales. Existing active perception methods adaptively select observation locations and scales to improve search efficiency, yet their search trajectories become increasingly long and costly as the search area expands. A key limitation is that final-answer correctness alone provides little guidance on which observations to retain and when to stop, hindering efficient wide-area search. To address this challenge, we propose EvoSearch, a self-evolving active perception framework that co-evolves the perception policy and exploration program to compress search trajectories while preserving answer quality. Specifically, quality-cost comparisons between completed branches from shared evidence train the policy to acquire useful observations, avoid redundant ones, and stop when sufficient evidence is available. Execution feedback from the updated policy then guides exploration-program revision, adapting experience generation to the policy's changing observation needs. We further introduce GeoSearchBench, a benchmark for evaluating search quality and visual processing efficiency through relational search and region search. Experiments demonstrate that EvoSearch shortens search trajectories and substantially reduces visual processing while maintaining overall search quality, supporting scalable active perception for wide-area geospatial search.

</details>


### [117] [NativeDrive: Native Multimodal Foundations for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32216)
*Zehua Fan,Qi Jiang,Wei Li,Kai Tang,Yue Jiang,Yujian Li,Bailin Li,Feng Gao,Yan Wang,Kun Zhan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出NativeDrive：一种无编码器的原生多模态自动驾驶框架，用统一骨干同时做策略学习与稠密监督，在NAVSIM v1/v2上取得统一理解-生成模型中的竞争力规划性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶需语义理解与细粒度场景感知以精确预测动作。VLA模型继承语义先验，但低维动作标签提供的结构监督有限；用视觉生成任务增强VLA或采用视频世界-动作模型（WAM）都会引入外观重建开销，而外观重建与规划关联较弱。

Method: 基于原生多模态模型在单一骨干内统一理解与生成。提出Native Action Interface：复用理解专家的token预测接口实现离散策略，并适配生成专家实现连续策略；提出Driving Semantic Editing：训练时复用生成专家的编辑能力做稠密场景结构监督，推理阶段仅运行所选规划策略。

Result: 在NAVSIM v1和v2上，NativeDrive在统一理解-生成模型中达到有竞争力的规划性能；消融实验验证了各组件有效性。

Conclusion: 以原生统一骨干替代"外观重建式"辅助任务，可在避免重建开销的同时获得稠密结构监督，为自动驾驶策略学习提供有效范式。

Abstract: Autonomous driving requires semantic understanding and fine-grained scene perception for precise action prediction. Vision-language-action models (VLAs) inherit semantic priors, but low-dimensional action labels provide limited structural supervision. Some studies extend VLAs with visual generation tasks or adopt video-based world-action models (WAMs), but both approaches introduce appearance-reconstruction overhead, which is less relevant to planning. Native multimodal models offer an alternative to unify understanding and generation within one backbone. On this basis, we propose NativeDrive, a native encoder-free multimodal driving framework adapting the unified backbone for policy learning and introduce a dense supervision task tailored to driving. The Native Action Interface reuses the understanding expert's token-prediction interface for the discrete policy and adapts the generation expert for continuous policy. Driving Semantic Editing reuses the generation expert's editing capability for dense scene-structure supervision during training, while inference runs only the selected planning policy. NativeDrive achieves competitive planning performance among unified understanding-and-generation models on NAVSIM v1 and v2, with ablations validating its components' effectiveness. Code will be released.

</details>


### [118] [FaceNet versus VGG Features in Wasserstein Distortion for Face Image Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32220)
*Xinyu Wang,Jona Ballé*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在Cool-Chic编解码器中将Wasserstein失真目标里的VGG特征替换为FaceNet中间特征，在约0.15 bpp匹配码率下对20张LFW人脸图像进行主观成对比较，FaceNet重建在72.5%的判断中被偏好，表明特征网络的选择会影响人脸图像感知质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Wasserstein失真（WD）目标依赖为通用图像分类训练的VGG等网络特征，而这类特征未必能反映压缩人脸图像的感知质量；作者因此探究人脸识别网络特征是否更适合作为人脸感知压缩的度量。

Method: 在Cool-Chic编解码器中，用FaceNet的中间特征图替换WD目标中的VGG特征，构建两种目标函数；在码率匹配（平均约0.15 bpp）条件下，对20张LFW人脸图像进行初步成对主观评测。

Result: FaceNet重建在72.5%的主观判断中被偏好，且在20张图像中有16张获得多数被试偏好。

Conclusion: WD中特征网络的选择会影响人脸图像的感知质量，人脸识别特征是人脸感知压缩的有前景方向。

Abstract: Wasserstein distortion (WD) compares local statistics of deep features and has been used to optimize learned image codecs for perceptual quality, but existing WD objectives rely on features from networks trained for generic image classification, such as VGG. We ask whether features from a face recognition network better reflect the perceived quality of compressed faces. In the Cool-Chic codec, we replace the VGG features in a WD-based objective with intermediate feature maps of FaceNet, and compare the two objectives on 20 LFW face images at a matched average bitrate of about 0.15 bits per pixel. In a preliminary pairwise human study, FaceNet-based reconstructions were preferred in 72.5% of judgments and by a majority of participants on 16 of the 20 images. These results suggest that the choice of feature network inside WD affects the perceived quality of face images, and that face recognition features are a promising direction for face-aware perceptual compression.

</details>


### [119] [Relation Before Action: Explicit Relation Modeling for Latent Action Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32226)
*Boning Zhang,Junyu Gao,Changsheng Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RELACT关系优先框架，将"过渡关系建模"与"预测性动作抽象"显式解耦，得到可迁移的隐动作表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐动作预训练将视觉过渡建模与动作推断耦合，直接把成对观测或过渡线索映射为隐动作码，使"刻画过渡的关系结构"与"将其抽象为紧凑动作表示"两个任务纠缠，限制了表示的预测性与可复用性。

Method: 两阶段关系优先设计：先构建结构化关系表示R，刻画视觉元素及其交互在观测间的演化；再推断紧凑的、以状态为条件的随机隐动作U，其预测效果通过动作条件隐动力学学习。

Result: 在域内与域外图像目标导航、目标条件操作及下游策略适配实验中，预测性动作表示更优，规划与控制性能一致提升。

Conclusion: 显式分离关系建模与动作抽象的关系优先预训练有效且通用；所得隐动作可接地到原生控制空间用于基于模型的规划，也可用于监督下游具身策略。

Abstract: Learning latent actions from unlabeled video sequences offers a promising way to acquire reusable action representations without relying on embodiment-specific control annotations. However, existing latent-action pretraining approaches commonly couple the modeling of visual transitions with action inference, directly mapping paired observations or derived transition cues into latent action codes. This formulation leaves two distinct tasks entangled: characterizing the relational structure of an observed transition and abstracting it into a compact action representation. We propose RELACT, a relation-first framework that explicitly separates transition relation modeling from predictive action abstraction. RELACT first constructs a structured relation representation $R$ that captures how visual elements and their interactions evolve across observations. It then infers a compact, state-conditioned stochastic latent action $U$, whose predictive effects are learned through action-conditioned latent dynamics. The resulting action representation can be grounded in native control spaces for model-based planning or used to supervise downstream embodied policies. Extensive experiments across in-domain and out-of-domain image-goal navigation, goal-conditioned manipulation, and downstream policy adaptation demonstrate improved predictive action representations and consistent gains in planning and control, supporting the effectiveness and generality of relation-first latent action pretraining.

</details>


### [120] [Relational Bottleneck Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32227)
*Shin'ya Yamaguchi,Kosuke Nishida*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出无需训练的Relational Bottleneck Models (RBMs)，将现成LVLM即插即用地转换为可解释模型，兼顾忠实可解释性与零样本性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CBM具内在可解释性但需任务特定训练、且依赖孤立视觉概念无法捕捉高阶视觉语义关系；LVLM的CoT具零样本能力但生成理由与输出间缺乏严格因果依赖，忠实性不足。

Method: 两阶段：(i) Structured Rationale Generation (SRG)，以主-谓-宾三元组显式建模视觉概念间语义交互；(ii) Rationale-Grounded Decoding (RGD)，通过重要性加权专家乘积(product-of-experts)聚合以理由为条件的下一词分布，并将图像输入严格排除在解码上下文之外，从而强制理由与输出间的严格因果依赖。

Result: 在多种基准与LVLM骨干上的广泛零样本评测显示，RBMs可解释性优于CBM基线，同时提升任务性能，且完全无需任务特定微调。

Conclusion: RBMs以即插即用、免训练方式弥合CBM可解释性与LVLM零样本能力之间的鸿沟，实现忠实且高性能的可解释推理。

Abstract: Achieving both interpretable and high-performance inference is a key challenge in deep learning. While Concept Bottleneck Models (CBMs) provide inherent interpretability, they are constrained by task-specific training and rely on isolated visual concepts that fail to capture higher-order visual semantic relationships. Conversely, Large Vision Language Models (LVLMs) demonstrate zero-shot capabilities through Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning. However, standard CoT lacks a strict causal dependency between the generated rationale and the final output, compromising faithfulness. To bridge this gap, we propose Relational Bottleneck Models (RBMs), a novel training-free framework that transforms any off-the-shelf LVLMs into inherently interpretable models in a plug-and-play manner. RBMs consist of two stages: (i) Structured Rationale Generation (SRG), which explicitly models semantic interactions between visual concepts as subject-predicate-object triplets, and (ii) Rationale-Grounded Decoding (RGD), which enforces a strict causal dependency between the rationales and the final output. RGD aggregates rationale-conditional next-token distributions via an importance-weighted product-of-experts strategy, strictly excluding the image inputs from the decoding context. Extensive zero-shot evaluations across diverse benchmarks and LVLM backbones demonstrate that RBMs achieve superior interpretability while improving task performance over CBM baselines, entirely without task-specific fine-tuning.

</details>


### [121] [ST-SfM: Spatio-Temporal Structure-from-Motion for Initialising 4D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32231)
*Harshit,Sami Brandt*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ST-SfM：面向同步标定多相机视频的稀疏时空SfM，将场景分成静态与动态两条分支，分别输出静态3D点与带亚帧时间的动态4D点(x,y,z,t)，用于初始化4D高斯泼溅。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态高斯泼溅通常用随机点或首帧SfM点云初始化，二者都不包含"什么在动、在哪动、何时动"的信息，缺少运动先验。

Method: 静态分支：每相机在时间中值图像上检测结构，经时间稳定性门控后沿对极线匹配。动态分支：在视频体中提取时空兴趣点(STIPs)，用HOG3D描述，仅在同时满足对极约束与时间一致的候选间匹配。用拒绝不一致合并的并查集构建轨迹，并以RANSAC在所有视角对上三角化，得到静态3D点与亚帧时间精度的动态4D点。

Result: 在N3DV场景上，以ST-SfM稀疏4D点云初始化4D高斯泼溅，相较随机初始化在PSNR、SSIM、LPIPS上均有提升。

Conclusion: 将时空稀疏重建作为4D高斯泼溅的初始化，可提供显式运动先验并改善重建质量；但摘要未报告具体数值与消融细节。

Abstract: Dynamic Gaussian splatting is usually initialised either with random points or with a structure-from-motion (SfM) cloud of the first frame. Neither says anything about what moves, where and when. We introduce ST-SfM, a sparse spatio-temporal SfM for synchronised, calibrated multi-camera video that splits the scene into two branches. Static structure is detected once per camera on a temporal-median image, gated for temporal stability and matched along epi-polar lines. Motion is detected as space-time interest points (STIPs) in the video volume, described with HOG3D and matched only between candidates that agree both epipolarly and in time. Union-find tracks that refuse inconsistent merges are triangulated with RANSAC over all view pairs, giving static 3D points and dynamic 4D points (x, y, z, t) with sub-frame times. We use ST-SfM derived sparse 4D cloud to initialise 4D Gaussian splatting. In comparison with random initialisation on N3DV scenes we get improvements in PSNR, SSIM and LPIPS.

</details>


### [122] [MYSTIQUE: Few-Shot Facial Identity Transfer with Performance Preservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32232)
*Shihan Cheng,David Hyde,Junting Deng,Michael Bao,Paul Debevec,Roshni Cooper,Soshi Shimada*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MYSTIQUE：基于扩散模型的视觉特效身份迁移方法，通过多流输入与多阶段训练解耦身份与其他信息，抑制自重建捷径导致的身份泄漏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 身份迁移需把驱动图像中的人物身份替换为目标人物，同时保留驱动图像的表情、光照、姿态与场景。由于成对真值稀缺，主流方法采用自重建训练，模型会通过复制驱动图像中的身份线索走捷径，在推理时（驱动图像为他人）造成身份泄漏。作者的关键观察是：泄漏可经由任何读取驱动图像的输入通道进入。

Method: 提出扩散式身份迁移方法 MYSTIQUE：身份来自目标人物的多张照片集合，表情、光照与场景经独立流读取；采用多阶段训练学习身份与其他流的解耦，并在各输入上施加解耦策略——(1) 随机块掩码，其边界不贴合面部轮廓；(2) 自增强，随机改变表情输入中的脸型但保留表情；(3) 人脸随机化，替换表情与光照输入中的人物而真值仍为驱动图像。推理阶段将光照输入形变至首轮生成的脸型上，以减少经光照流的脸型泄漏。评估同时采用人脸识别分数与人类判断，并在已知形状的渲染人脸（rendered faces of known shape）上评测脸型指标。

Result: 用户研究中，评测者认为 MYSTIQUE 的结果比各对比方法更接近目标人物；在对比方法中，MYSTIQUE 对目标脸型恢复最准确，且随目标照片数量增加，其识别分数的提升幅度最大。

Conclusion: 将身份与表情、光照、场景分流通路并结合针对输入的显式解耦策略，可有效抑制自重建捷径带来的身份泄漏；识别分数与人类判断各有局限，需与脸型指标联合评估。

Abstract: In visual effects, identity transfer replaces the identity in a driving image with that of a target person, while preserving the expression, lighting, pose and scene of that image. Paired ground truth is rarely available, so many methods are trained by self-reconstruction, where the model can learn a shortcut by copying identity cues from the driving image. This shortcut causes identity leakage at inference, where the driving image shows a different person. Our key observation is that leakage can enter through any input that reads the driving image. We therefore present MYSTIQUE, a diffusion-based identity transfer method that takes identity from a set of the target's photos and reads expression, lighting and scene through separate streams. It learns to disentangle identity from the other streams through multi-stage training, with disentanglement strategies at their inputs: a random block mask whose boundaries do not follow the face outline, self-augmentation, which randomly changes the face shape in the expression input and keeps its expression, and face randomization, which replaces the person in the expression and lighting inputs while the ground truth stays the driving image. At inference, we also warp the lighting input onto the face shape of a first pass, which reduces face-shape leakage through the lighting stream. Since face-recognition scores and human judgments each have limits, we evaluate identity with both, together with face-shape metrics on rendered faces of known shape. In a user study, raters judge MYSTIQUE's results more like the target than those of each compared method. Among these methods, MYSTIQUE also recovers the target's face shape most accurately, and its recognition score gains the most as more photos of the target are given.

</details>


### [123] [Can You Find the Edit When Nothing Looks Wrong? A Paired Benchmark for Text-Driven Image Manipulation Localization Beyond Semantic Abnormality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32242)
*Peiyu Zhuang,Jianquan Yang,Zhuoying Cai,Yi Jiang,Yingquan Chen,Xinle Wang,Zeqin Yu,Dongdong Lin,Ruitao Xie,Kaige Li,Xiaochun Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文构建了成对的图像编辑基准（同一目标的“合理”与“异常”替换），发现局部化模型存在“语义合理性捷径”：编辑结果越像正常照片，定位性能越差（27组模型-数据集对比中26组合理编辑IoU更低）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准缺少针对同一目标的、匹配的合理编辑与异常编辑，难以衡量定位性能如何随语义合理性变化。

Method: 对每张源图同一目标分别生成语义合理与异常的替换，二者使用同一编辑器、语言和生成设置；对每个输出单独估计掩码，仅保留掩码重叠度足够的配对，从而在两种语义条件下直接比较定位性能。数据集含6,500张照片生成47,747个双语三元组，覆盖4种编辑器、3个源域、6类目标违规类型。

Result: 在9个公开定位模型上，27组模型-数据集对比中有26组在合理编辑上平均IoU更低；该差距在像素变化控制和区域尺寸分析下依然存在。

Conclusion: 揭示了语义合理性捷径现象——编辑结果看起来普通时定位能力常被削弱；该基准为量化此差距、开发在合理编辑上仍有效的定位器提供了受控实验平台。

Abstract: Instruction-driven image editing can alter a photograph while keeping the result coherent with the scene. Existing benchmarks lack matched plausible and anomalous edits of the same target, making it difficult to measure how localization performance changes with semantic plausibility. We construct a paired benchmark by generating semantically plausible and anomalous replacements of the same target in each source photograph. Both edits use the same editor, language, and generation settings. We estimate a separate mask for each output and retain pairs with sufficient mask overlap, enabling direct comparison of localization performance under the two semantic conditions. The release contains 47,747 bilingual triplets from 6,500 photographs, spanning four editors, three source domains, and six intended violation types. Across nine public localizers, editor-averaged IoU is lower on plausible edits in 26 of 27 model-dataset comparisons. This gap persists under pixel-change controls and region-size analyses. These findings reveal a semantic-plausibility shortcut: localization often weakens when an edited result looks ordinary. The benchmark provides a controlled setting for measuring this gap and developing localizers that remain effective on plausible edits.

</details>


### [124] [CaveBench: Benchmarking Abductive Physical Reasoning from Internal Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32251)
*Guanxin Lin,Chen Yang,Shenxiang Zeng,Honghui Zhu,Xin Li,Wenbo Deng,Jiansheng Fan,Chen Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 CaveBench 基准，用于考察视觉语言模型能否从装置内部观测反推隐藏的外部控制操作（溯因物理推理），包含 5 个物理域、20 个装置场景、10,200 个仿真 episode，对 27 种 VLM 配置评测显示最强闭源模型六事件完全匹配率仅 68.0%，最强开源模型仅 12.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM 在物理推理上已有进展，但"能否从内部观测理解外部物理驱动因素"这一设定尚未被专门基准覆盖；现有基准未针对该溯因推理场景，因此需要构建专门评测。

Method: 构建 CaveBench 基准：模型仅获得装置描述与内部图像，外部控制信息被隐藏，需推断导致观测响应的操作。数据覆盖 5 个物理域、20 个装置场景，共 10,200 个仿真 episode（9,000 训练 / 1,000 验证 / 200 测试）；问题涉及 1–6 个事件，每个事件均要求给出完整控制集合，以检验解释是否覆盖全过程；同步的观测与控制记录分别作为输入与参考答案。评测 27 种 VLM 配置，并设置 7 个自适应基线。

Result: 六事件完全匹配准确率：最强闭源模型 68.0%，最强开源权重模型 12.5%。7 个自适应基线表明，单事件上的性能提升并不必然带来完整序列上的提升。错误分析将失败归为四类：视觉识别、物理推断、证据整合与动作组合。

Conclusion: 当前 VLM 在从内部现象回溯外部成因的溯因物理推理上仍显著不足，尤其难以处理多事件长序列；CaveBench 通过分类错误定位了模型在视觉识别、物理推断、证据整合与动作组合上的具体薄弱环节。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are advancing in physical reasoning, but whether they can understand external physical drivers from internal observations remains an open question. Existing benchmarks have not specifically targeted this setting. To this end, we introduce CaveBench, a benchmark for abductive physical reasoning from internal observations. Models receive apparatus descriptions and internal images while the external controls remain hidden, and must infer the operations responsible for the observed responses. CaveBench spans 20 apparatus scenes in five physical domains and contains 10,200 simulated episodes, split into 9,000 training, 1,000 validation, and 200 test episodes. Questions span one to six events and require the complete control set at every event, testing whether an explanation accounts for the entire process. Synchronized observations and control records provide the inputs and reference answers. Evaluating 27 VLM configurations yields six-event exact-match accuracies of 68.0% for the strongest closed-source model and 12.5% for the strongest open-weight model. Seven adaptation baselines show that single-event gains need not improve complete sequences. Error analysis identifies failures in visual identification, physical inference, evidence integration, and action composition, specifying where current models struggle to connect internal phenomena with their external causes.

</details>


### [125] [StreamCoach: Proactive Sports Coaching via Continuous Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32281)
*Xiaoye Liang,Yiming Huang,Jialu Wu,Yushe Cao,Ruxiang Duan,Yi Sun,Tie Liu,Lai Jiang,Mai Xu,Shikun Feng,Yiheng Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出流式动作质量评估框架 StreamCoach，耦合连续思维链推理与"证据门控"的主动式教练反馈，并构建 EE4D-Stream 数据集与 TS Alignment RL 训练策略，实现及时、全面的技术错误反馈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 AQA 方法只评估已完成动作，无法及时反馈；现有流式方法推理能力有限，难以利用细微动作线索与运动领域知识，且反馈碎片化，仅关注单一身体部位，忽略其他部位细节与动作阶段间的过渡。

Method: 在 OnCueCoaching 范式下构建 StreamCoach：Technique-Aware Reasoning 依据运动专项标准追踪各身体部位运动与动作阶段转换；Holistic Coaching 整合累积证据输出及时的优缺点反馈。基于 Ego-Exo4D 的 1213 段序列，经 LLM 辅助重标注与人工复核构建 EE4D-Stream（密集 CoT 标注 + 证据门控语音内容），并用 Temporal-Semantic Alignment Reinforcement Learning 联合优化反馈时机与内容。

Result: 在 EE4D-Stream 上与 11 个离线/流式基线（含 3 个闭源模型）对比，StreamCoach 在干预时机、动作评估、教练质量三方面均达 SOTA，超过 GPT-6.1 Sol 与 Claude Opus 5.5。

Conclusion: 将连续思维链推理与证据门控的主动式教练相结合，可有效解决流式 AQA 的推理不足与反馈碎片化问题，验证了时机与内容联合优化的价值。

Abstract: In fitness and sports training, timely action quality assessment (AQA) is essential for learners to identify and correct technical errors during movement execution. However, most AQA methods assess completed performances, limiting their ability to provide timely feedback. Streaming methods alleviate this latency, but limited reasoning hinders their use of subtle movement cues and sport-specific knowledge. Moreover, their fragmented feedback focuses on one body part at a time, overlooking movement details of other body parts and transitions between action phases. To address these limitations, we propose StreamCoach, a streaming AQA framework under the OnCueCoaching paradigm that couples continuous chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning with proactive, evidence-gated coaching. Technique-Aware Reasoning tracks body-part movements and action-phase transitions against sport-specific criteria, while Holistic Coaching integrates accumulated evidence to deliver timely, comprehensive feedback on strengths and technical errors. To support training and benchmarking, we construct EE4D-Stream from 1,213 sports video sequences in Ego-Exo4D through LLM-assisted reannotation and manual review of selected claims, pairing dense CoT annotations with evidence-gated speech content. Building on this dataset, we train StreamCoach using Temporal-Semantic Alignment Reinforcement Learning to jointly optimize feedback timing and content. We benchmark 11 offline and streaming baselines on EE4D-Stream, including three proprietary models. StreamCoach achieves state-of-the-art results across intervention timing, action assessment, and coaching quality, surpassing both GPT-6.1 Sol and Claude Opus 5.5.

</details>


### [126] [SofaSpace: A Benchmark for Executable Visual Spatial Reasoning in Continuous Motion and Environment Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32285)
*Ze Rong,Kun Shi,Zhedong Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SofaSpace基准，用连续平面刚体搬运任务检验视觉空间推理，并用几何验证器检查整条运动轨迹，揭示端点一致性与连续有效性的巨大差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刚体在其起点和目标位姿都可能不发生碰撞，但在两者之间的运动过程中却可能撞墙。受"移动沙发问题"启发，作者希望用可执行几何验证来区分"端点能对上"与"运动真正可行"，以及"几何可行"与"功能满足"。

Method: 构建SofaSpace基准，含3000个场景，围绕三类挑战组织：几何兼容性、非局部机动规划、交互与时间协调。设四种任务视角：规划运动、重建布局、设计满足搬运需求的环境、联合生成布局与运动；另设534个SofaSpace-Hard任务测试规划与判断。提交的每条运动轨迹由几何验证器对刚体整体进行全过程校验。

Result: 静态规划任务中，同一批提交方案里端点一致率与连续有效率差距显著：Claude 97.2%→52.8%，DeepSeek 100.0%→30.6%，Kimi 97.2%→29.4%；最强配置仍保98.3%完全有效性。环境构建差距更大：最强配置生成的295个可行布局中有181个满足全部评估功能要求；Kimi的172个中仅1个满足。

Conclusion: 可执行几何检查能有效区分端点一致性valid运动、几何可行性与功能合规性；现有模型在此类连续空间推理上仍有明显短板。

Abstract: A rigid body can fit at its start and goal yet collide with a wall as it moves between them. Inspired by the moving sofa problem, we introduce SofaSpace, a benchmark for visual spatial reasoning through continuous planar rigid-body transport and environment construction. SofaSpace contains 3,000 scenes organized around three challenges: geometric compatibility, nonlocal maneuver planning, and interactive and temporal coordination. Four task views ask models to plan a motion, reconstruct a layout, design an environment that meets transport requirements, or jointly generate a layout and a motion. A separate set of 534 SofaSpace-Hard tasks tests planning and judgment. A geometric verifier checks the entire body throughout each submitted motion. On static planning tasks, endpoint agreement and continuous validity differ substantially within the same submitted plans: Claude drops from 97.2% to 52.8%, DeepSeek from 100.0% to 30.6%, and Kimi from 97.2% to 29.4%, while the strongest configuration retains 98.3% full validity. Environment construction reveals a further gap. Of 295 feasible layouts generated by the strongest configuration, 181 satisfy all evaluated functional requirements; for Kimi, one of 172 does. These results show how executable checks distinguish endpoint agreement from valid motion and geometric feasibility from functional compliance.

</details>


### [127] [$ε$-Seg++: Ensembling Across Hierarchy and Training Time for Label-Efficient EM Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32290)
*Edoardo Giacomello,Sheida Rahnamai Kordasiabi,Mehdi Seifi,Florian Jug*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ε-Seg++ 在层级 VAE 每层设置语义专家并用无参专家乘积融合，支持 3D 输入与无泄漏子体积切分；在稀疏标注下 2D/3D 均优于基线，且推理显著更快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EM 体数据的体素级语义分割标注代价极高，只能依赖稀疏标注；此前的 ε-Seg 仅从最高层潜变量分类且只处理 2D 图像块，未利用层级信息，也无法与 3D 方法公平比较；同时验证标签同样稀缺，checkpoint 选择本身成为难题。

Method: 1) 在层次化 VAE 的每一层潜变量处放置语义专家，并用无参数乘积专家（product of experts）整合各层预测；2) 提出无泄漏的子体积切分协议，使 2D 与 3D 模型可在相同稀疏监督下比较；3) 将 checkpoint 选择纳入方法本身，对排名靠前的多个 checkpoint 做跨训练时间的集成。

Result: BetaSeg FIB-SEM 上，2500 个标注时多层专家使 2D 平均 Dice 提升约 0.1；≤600 标注时 2D 模型以约 1/11 的推理代价持平或超过 3D ε-Seg；>1000 标注时 3D 占优，等标注成本下 3D ε-Seg++ 平均 Dice 0.905（D3PM 0.880），推理快 5.4–27 倍；checkpoint 集成平均持平单最优选择，小验证集下显著改善最差情况。

Conclusion: 多层语义专家与无参专家乘积能更充分利用层级特征；2D 与 3D 的适用性随标注量切换（少标注偏 2D、多标注偏 3D）；checkpoint 集成是应对验证标签稀缺的稳健策略，整体在精度与推理效率间取得更优折中。

Abstract: Semantic segmentation of electron microscopy (EM) volumes is a central step in quantitative cell biology, but dense voxel-wise annotation is prohibitively expensive. $ε$-Seg showed that a hierarchical variational autoencoder trained on sparsely annotated voxels can yield competitive segmentations, but it classifies from its topmost latent level only and operates exclusively on 2D patches. We introduce $ε$-Seg++, which places a semantic expert at every level of the hierarchy and consolidates their predictions with a parameter-free product of experts. We further enable 3D inputs through a leakage-free sub-volume splitting protocol, which allows 2D and 3D models to be compared under identical sparse supervision. Because validation labels are just as scarce as training labels, we treat checkpoint selection as part of the method and average the top-ranked checkpoints, a second ensemble, this time across training time. On the BetaSeg FIB-SEM dataset, the multi-level experts improve 2D mean Dice by about 0.1 at 2,500 training labels. With up to 600 training labels, the 2D model matches or exceeds 3D $ε$-Seg at about one eleventh of its prediction cost. Above roughly a thousand labels, 3D inputs dominate, and at matched annotation cost 3D $ε$-Seg++ reaches a mean Dice of 0.905 versus 0.880 for D3PM, while predicting between 5.4 and 27 times faster (with our checkpoint ensemble or a single model, respectively). Checkpoint ensembling matches single-best selection on average and markedly improves the worst case when the validation set is small.

</details>


### [128] [MaskedVGGT: Masked Multi-View Transformers Are Scalable 3D Learners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32297)
*Zichen Zou,Xiaosong Jia,Wen-Ming Chen,Zuxuan Wu,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MaskedVGGT 冻结预训练 2D 编码器，仅用掩码自蒸馏预训练新增的多视图 Transformer，学习跨视图交互，无需几何标注，在多规模（至 1B）下优于单阶段监督 VGGT-Ω 及 E-RayZer、CroCo、MuM、Muskie。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多视图前馈模型依赖昂贵的几何标注，限制数据扩展；现有 3D 自监督（CroCo、MuM、Muskie 的跨视图补全，RayZer、E-RayZer 的新视图合成）从零联合学习图像表征与跨视图交互，无法复用 DINO、CLIP、前沿 VLM 等 2D 基础模型的进展，而从头学单图表征对 3D 预训练并非必要。

Method: 冻结强预训练 2D 编码器，只随机初始化并预训练一个附加的多视图 Transformer，使其专注跨视图交互；采用无标注掩码自蒸馏：学生以完整锚视图与重度掩码的其余视图为输入预测被掩码位置的目标，EMA 教师在未掩码输入上提供预测目标。

Result: 在至 1B 参数的多个模型规模上，MaskedVGGT 预训练+微调在相同训练数据、架构与总优化步数下优于单阶段监督 VGGT-Ω；亦优于 E-RayZer、CroCo、MuM、Muskie；用额外无标注视频延长预训练进一步提升泛化，所学表征可迁移到多种下游任务。

Conclusion: 结果支持一种有效的学习分工：复用 2D 预训练的强图像表征，并从无标注多视图数据中学习跨视图交互，无需依赖几何标注。

Abstract: Multi-view feed-forward models show strong scalability, but their reliance on costly geometric annotations limits data scaling. Existing 3D self-supervised approaches reduce this reliance through cross-view completion (e.g., CroCo, MuM, Muskie) or novel-view synthesis (e.g., RayZer, E-RayZer), typically learning image representations and cross-view interactions jointly. We argue that learning single image representations from scratch is unnecessary for 3D pretraining methods, as doing so fails to benefit from the continuing advances in 2D foundation models (e.g., DINO, CLIP, frontier VLMs). We introduce MaskedVGGT, which freezes a strong pretrained 2D encoder and pretrains only an added, randomly initialized multi-view transformer, focusing on learning interactions across views. MaskedVGGT pretrains the multi-view transformer through masked self-distillation without annotations: a student predicts targets at masked locations based on complete anchor views and heavily masked remaining views, while an exponential-moving-average teacher provides prediction targets based on unmasked inputs. In experiments across model sizes up to 1B parameters, MaskedVGGT pretraining followed by fine-tuning outperforms single-stage supervised VGGT-$Ω$ training with the same training data, model architecture, and total number of optimization updates. MaskedVGGT also outperforms E-RayZer, CroCo, MuM, and Muskie, providing evidence for the effectiveness of building multi-view pretraining on strong 2D representations. Finally, extending pretraining with additional unlabeled videos further improves generalization, and the learned representations transfer to diverse downstream tasks. These results demonstrate an effective division of learning: reuse strong image representations from 2D pretraining and learn cross-view interactions from unlabeled multi-view data without relying on geometric annotations.

</details>


### [129] [Towards a Multimodal Aesthetic Agent for Photographic Reference Grading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32299)
*Kunjie Lin,Haitao Zhang,Bosheng Chen,Lanhui Xu,Gaofeng Pan,Yuxin Lin,Tianjiao Liu,Yi Yang,Yue Huang,Xinghao Ding,Zixu Lin,Yunlong Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 JarvisAura，一个多模态审美智能体，将参考图引导的照片调色形式化为可编辑参数空间中的结构化决策，通过三阶段训练实现可解释、可控的调色，在 Ref-Bench 上取得最优自动指标与 74% 人工成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参考图引导调色需理解用户想采纳哪些色彩与影调特征并转化为可控编辑。但纯图像参考方法只能对齐整体外观、缺乏指令化选择；像素空间生成式编辑器输出的图像扁平、无可编辑参数，难以二次精修。

Method: 基于审美智能体范式，融合视觉属性感知、策略级推理与参数化执行；构建从推理到执行的统一流水线，连接审美规划与后端渲染及参数迭代；采用三阶段训练：参数-外观对齐的持续预训练、参考图引导的监督微调、以及带多维奖励与动态数据派生的 Reference-Aligned Group Relative Policy Optimization。

Result: 在 Ref-Bench 上，JarvisAura 在全部报告的自动指标中均取得最优，人工评测任务成功率达 74%，比 GPT-Image-2.5 高 9 个百分点。

Conclusion: 将调色建模为参数空间中的可解释决策并由智能体执行，可在保持可编辑性的同时优于像素空间生成式编辑方案。

Abstract: Reference-guided photographic color grading helps users realize their aesthetic intent by selectively drawing on the color and tonal characteristics of a reference photograph. Doing so requires understanding which characteristics users wish to adopt and translating these preferences into controllable edits. However, image-only reference methods align overall appearance without instruction-grounded selection, while pixel-space generative editors typically produce flattened images without editable parameters, limiting subsequent refinement. To address these challenges, we introduce JarvisAura, a multimodal aesthetic agent that formulates photographic color grading as structured decision-making in an editable parameter space. JarvisAura offers three key advantages: (1) an aesthetic agent paradigm that integrates visual attribute perception, strategy-level reasoning, and parameterized execution for interpretable and controllable photographic color grading; (2) a unified reasoning-to-execution pipeline that connects aesthetic planning with backend rendering and editable parameter refinement; and (3) a three-stage training strategy comprising Parameter-Grounded Continual Pre-Training for parameter-appearance grounding, Reference-Grounded Supervised Fine-Tuning for reference-guided reasoning and parameter decisions, and Reference-Aligned Group Relative Policy Optimization for rendered-outcome refinement with multidimensional rewards and dynamic data derivation. On Ref-Bench, JarvisAura achieves the best scores among methods with results across reported automatic metrics and a 74% human-rated task-success rate, exceeding GPT-Image-2.5 by 9 percentage points.

</details>


### [130] [Query-Guided Visual Minimization for Selective Disclosure to Cloud VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32302)
*Pedram Zaree,Kleomenis Katevas,Vandana Rajan,Nael Abu-Ghazaleh,Hamed Haddadi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出QGVM框架，在本地对图像做查询引导的最小化处理，区分"视觉相关性"与"披露必要性"，在保护黑盒云VLMs隐私的同时保持任务效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云端VLM需将图像传出用户信任边界，可能泄露查询并不需要的敏感信息；而查询无关的匿名化无法在查询确实需要该信息时予以保留。

Method: QGVM将查询grounding与独立的敏感项清单结合，在类型、实例、文本内容三个层级判定披露必要性，并渲染最小化图像供黑盒下游VLM使用；评测覆盖任务效用、可直接恢复的敏感信息与推断性地理定位泄露，并用配对查询验证披露的查询依赖性。

Result: GPT-6.1 Sol下DoxBench正确率85.3%（PGP 77.5%）；1km内地理定位攻击成功率5.8%（未保护图像25.7%）；Scene-Privacy人脸再识别由27.7%降至4.5%；BIV-Priv-Seg私有文本恢复由33.9%降至15.3%；配对查询的披露切换正确率69.6%（仅grounding裁剪29.1%）。

Conclusion: 区分视觉相关性与披露必要性具有实际价值，同时揭示了选择性视觉披露存在隐私—效用权衡。

Abstract: Cloud-hosted vision-language models (VLMs) require sending images beyond the user's local trust boundary, potentially exposing sensitive information the query does not need. We study query-guided visual minimization: a trusted local transformation preserves query-relevant visual content and reduces unnecessary disclosure before transmission. Unlike query-agnostic anonymization, it preserves the same sensitive content when the query requires it and suppresses it otherwise. We introduce QGVM, a framework that combines query grounding with an independent sensitive-item inventory, determines disclosure necessity at the type, instance, and text-content levels, and renders a minimized image for a black-box downstream VLM. We evaluate QGVM on complementary benchmarks measuring task utility, directly recoverable sensitive information, and inferential geolocation leakage. With OpenAI GPT-6.1 Sol, QGVM achieves 85.3% DoxBench answer correctness versus 77.5% for PGP, while a separately prompted Sol geolocation attacker succeeds within 1 km on 5.8% of QGVM images versus 25.7% of unprotected images. On Scene-Privacy, QGVM reduces face re-identification from 27.7% (PGP) to 4.5%. On held-out BIV-Priv-Seg, unnecessary private-text recovery falls from 33.9% (PGP) to 15.3%. To verify that disclosure is query-dependent, we evaluate paired queries that either require or do not require the same sensitive information. QGVM achieves 69.6% correct disclosure switching, compared with 29.1% for grounding-only cropping. These findings highlight the value of distinguishing visual relevance from disclosure necessity, while revealing the privacy-utility trade-offs of selective visual disclosure.

</details>


### [131] [CREDIT: Compositional Reliability Learning for Noisy Composed Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32310)
*Tiancai Zhou,Yongxiang Li,Suimei Liu,Zhong Yuan,Dezhong Peng,Xi Peng,Xu Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CREDIT框架，用证据不确定性学习量化三元组对应可靠性，并用扩散引导细化参考表征，抑制噪声监督传播，提升组合图像检索鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实CIR训练数据存在噪声三元组对应（NTC），图-文-目标语义对应不可靠；现有方法用固定决策规则做二值判定，无法刻画不同程度（非二值）的语义可靠性，导致对不可靠三元组过度自信的监督，并在组合表征学习中逐步放大噪声语义。

Method: CREDIT含两互补模块：(1) Evidential Compositional Uncertainty Learning (ECUL)：基于证据学习显式建模对应不确定性，实现不确定性感知的可靠性估计以抑制噪声监督；(2) Diffusion-enhanced Reference Refinement (DRR)：在ECUL提供的可靠监督基础上，通过文本条件扩散细化参考图像表征，得到更鲁棒的组合查询特征。

Result: 大量实验表明，在多种设置下CREDIT持续优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 以不确定性量化替代确定性二值判定来处理NTC是有效路径；证据学习与扩散细化的组合可抑制噪声语义传播并增强组合查询表征，为噪声条件下的组合图像检索提供了可靠方案。

Abstract: Composed Image Retrieval (CIR) aims to retrieve a target image that satisfies user intent based on a multimodal query consisting of a reference image and a modification text. However, real-world training data are often corrupted by Noisy Triplet Correspondence (NTC), where the semantic correspondence among the reference image, modification text, and target image becomes unreliable. Existing methods implicitly assume that the correctness of triplet correspondence can be determined by fixed decision rules. However, under NTC, the authenticity of semantic correspondence is difficult to determine accurately, and different triplets exhibit varying degrees of semantic reliability rather than binary correctness. Consequently, deterministic confidence is insufficient to faithfully characterize correspondence reliability, often leading to overconfident supervision for unreliable triplets and progressively reinforcing noisy semantics during compositional representation learning. To address this challenge, we propose a Compositional Reliability learning framework with Evidential uncertainty and DiffusIon-guided refinemenT (CREDIT), which explicitly quantifies the uncertainty of triplet correspondence to estimate its reliability and suppress the propagation of noisy supervision. CREDIT consists of two complementary components: The Evidential Compositional Uncertainty Learning (ECUL) module explicitly models correspondence uncertainty via evidential learning, enabling uncertaintyaware reliability estimation to suppress noisy supervision. Building upon the reliable supervision provided by ECUL, the Diffusion-enhanced Reference Refinement (DRR) module further refines reference representations through textconditioned diffusion, producing more robust compositional query features. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CREDIT consistently outperforms existing methods under various settings.

</details>


### [132] [SynAVID: A Synthetic Audio-Visual Immersive Dataset of Dynamic Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32311)
*Jun Xu,Sujing Li,Chunhao Bi,Zhengxue Cheng,Ronghua Wu,Qunshan Gu,Li Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出合成视听沉浸数据集SynAVID（8个室内外场景、分钟级片段），将固定相机阵列与移动轨迹结合，生成同步且随视角变化的双耳音频，并系统评测视觉重建、双耳音频重建与视听编辑方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 沉浸式场景建模需要视角与场景条件变化下同步的视觉与听觉观测，但获取此类观测及可控场景变化的参考数据十分困难。

Method: 构建SynAVID数据集：包含8个室内外场景，每个分钟级episode融合固定相机阵列与移动轨迹；由共享的场景动态、声源事件与观察者位姿共同驱动视觉渲染与几何感知的声学合成；并提供几何与实例标注及成对参考，用于评测视觉重建、双耳音频重建和视听编辑。

Result: 混合观测配置通常能提升固定与移动测试视角下的视觉重建质量，但瞬态占据与稀疏观测区域仍具挑战；各方法在频谱、双耳与感知音频指标上的排序不一致；几何与实例标注揭示了不均衡的观测支持及局部高斯基元表示问题；成对参考可评估目标编辑与周边内容，受控几何变化会产生依赖听者的声学响应。

Conclusion: SynAVID为视听沉浸场景建模提供了带同步双耳音频与精细标注的可控基准，揭示了现有方法在观测不足区域和音频评价维度上的局限。

Abstract: Immersive scene modeling requires synchronized visual and auditory observations under changing viewpoints and scene conditions. Obtaining such observations and references for controlled scene changes remains challenging. We introduce SynAVID, a synthetic audio-visual immersive dataset comprising eight indoor and outdoor scenes. Each minute-scale episode combines fixed camera arrays and moving trajectories with synchronized, viewpoint-dependent binaural audio. Shared scene dynamics, sound-source events, and observer poses drive visual rendering and geometry-aware acoustic synthesis. We evaluate existing methods for visual reconstruction, binaural audio reconstruction, and audiovisual editing. Mixed-observation configurations generally improve visual reconstruction on fixed and moving test views, while transiently occupied and sparsely observed regions remain challenging. Methods rank differently on spectral, interaural, and perceptual audio metrics. Geometric and instance annotations expose uneven observation support and local Gaussian representations beyond aggregate scores. Paired references assess target edits and surrounding content, while controlled geometry changes produce listener-dependent acoustic responses. The project page is available at https://avery-xu.github.io/synavid-project/.

</details>


### [133] [ASTERISM: Do Multimodal LLMs Actually Look at All the Images?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32316)
*Chanseul Cho,Dongwoo Kim,Noori Bae,Seungjae Moon,Seongsu Lee,Youngmin Ro*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文审计19个多图基准（约33k题）发现24.2%题目可由单张图像或问题本身解决，提出单图捷径概念；据此构建ASTERISM系列审计基准，并提出跨图像信用分配（CICA）方法以消除RLVR对单图捷径的奖励。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多图基准本意测试MLLM跨图像整合证据的能力，但许多题目仅凭单张图像即可作答。现有评测无法区分"真正跨图推理"与"单图捷径"，且RLVR训练同样按答案正确性给奖励，导致单图可解题目获得与跨图推理相同的奖励，模型缺乏跨图整合的激励。

Method: 1）对19个公开基准约33k题进行审计与两阶段单图过滤，量化单图捷径比例；2）分析模型在单图充分/不充分题目上的行为，统计错误答案重复单图答案的比率；3）保留需多图才能解决的题目构建ASTERISM（18.7k题，2.0k题经修正或重写，统一格式、统一评分器、七类任务），并发布ASTERISM-Train（49k训练题）与人工逐题验证的ASTERISM-Lite；4）提出CICA，作为RLVR的扩展，将信用从"答案正确"重新分配到真正需要多图信息的token上。

Result: 审计显示24.2%的题目可由单张图像或问题单独确定；在这些题目上，单图可恢复全图相对纯文本增益中位数的78%准确率。模型即使在单图不足时仍走捷径，错误答案重复单图答案的比率显著高于随机。最强闭源模型在ASTERISM-Lite上得分68%。现有RLVR目标对ASTERISM得分提升甚微；CICA在三个开源MLLM上使ASTERISM得分提升2.6–6.0分，超过同数据下通用与视觉专用RL目标增益的两倍以上。

Conclusion: 现有多图基准存在系统性的单图捷径污染，且RLVR的答案级奖励会激励该捷径。ASTERISM通过评测层面的过滤提供可信的跨图推理度量，CICA通过token级跨图信用分配在训练层面与评测保持一致，显著提升多图推理能力，为多图基准构建与MLLM后训练提供可复用范式。

Abstract: Multi-image benchmarks aim to test whether a multimodal large language model (MLLM) combines evidence across images, but many of their items can be answered from a single image. Across about 33k items from 19 public benchmarks, our audit and a two-stage single-image filter find that one image or the question alone settles 24.2% of them. On these items, MLLMs recover from one image a median 78% of the accuracy that all images add over the text alone, so these items reward answering from one image, which we call the single-image shortcut. MLLMs take this shortcut even on items where one image is not enough. On such items, most of their wrong answers repeat an answer they give from one image, at a rate well above chance. We build ASTERISM, to our knowledge the largest audited multi-image benchmark, from these 19 benchmarks by keeping their items that need more than one image. Its 18.7k items, 2.0k of them corrected or rewritten, share one format, one scorer and seven task categories. We also release ASTERISM-Train with 49k training items and ASTERISM-Lite, a hard subset whose items we manually verify one by one. On ASTERISM-Lite, the strongest proprietary MLLM scores 68%. Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), now common in MLLM post-training, rewards the shortcut as well, because a correct answer read off one image earns as much reward as one that combines images. In line with this, existing RLVR objectives add little to the ASTERISM score. To stop rewarding the shortcut in training as ASTERISM does in evaluation, we propose cross-image credit assignment (CICA), an extension of RLVR that moves the credit toward the tokens that need more than one image. On three open-source MLLMs, CICA raises the ASTERISM score by 2.6 to 6.0 points, more than twice the gain of each general and vision-specific RL objective trained on the same data.

</details>


### [134] [A recursive self-improvement framework for electron microscopy analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32325)
*Yaotian Yang,Sijia Tao,Yuping Zhang,Jiangjie Qiu,Qinghua Zhang,Xiao Chen,Fei Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出物理引导、专家反馈的递归自我改进框架 PhyEvo-Mic-RSI，将一次性人工修正转化为可复用、可验证的能力，实现缺陷与界面等局域原子结构的自动化解析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从电镜衬度解析缺陷、界面等局域原子结构高度依赖研究者主观判断，效率低且标准不统一；现有模型和脚本为一次性、静态的，无法积累和继承专家经验。

Method: 以版本化继承机制更新可执行算子、参数与路由规则；给定研究者定义的任务后，系统依据图像特征在无专家标签条件下选择分析路径，并在固定预算下测试与继承代码更新；融合物理先验与专家反馈。

Result: 原子点缺陷检测性能超过更大规模模型；在10%面积预算内将跨材料界面覆盖率由5.13%提升至72.11%；迁移至未见研究的显微图像时，通过对齐优化与颗粒级测量，实现粘连纳米颗粒的分离。

Conclusion: 该工作推动计算显微学从独立模型与静态脚本走向可自我进化、持续积累人类科学专业知识的平台。

Abstract: Resolving local atomic structures such as defects and interfaces from electron microscopy contrast relies heavily on researcher judgement, leading to low efficiency and subjective standards. Here we present PhyEvo-Mic-RSI, a physics-guided and expert-informed recursive self-improvement framework that turns one-off human corrections into reusable, verifiable capabilities by updating executable operators, parameters and routing rules with versioned inheritance. Given researcher-defined tasks, the system selects analysis paths from image features without expert labels, while testing and inheriting code updates under fixed budgets. Combining physical priors with expert feedback improves atomic point-defect detection over larger models and raises cross-material interface coverage from 5.13% to 72.11% within a 10% area budget. Adapting the framework to micrographs from unseen studies further separates touching nanoparticles by aligning optimization with particle-level measurements. This work moves computational microscopy beyond standalone models and static scripts towards self-evolving platforms that accumulate human scientific expertise

</details>


### [135] [Longitudinal Characterization of Personalized Hydration Dynamics Using a Facial-Video Digital Hydration Biomarker](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32326)
*Janindu Athukorala,D. M. J. Prathapa,Thanveer Ahamed,Yudara Kularathne*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究利用面部视频水合评分对5名成人进行12小时纵向追踪（250段视频）及500 mL电解质挑战测试，发现饮水后评分平均上升4.0分（p<0.001），低起点（<50）者平均上升11.3分，但个体差异显著（≥50分时长占比16%–100%），提示需个性化校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水合状态常被单时点评估，但体水持续变化；面部视频生物标志物虽能追踪水合状态转换，其纵向行为尚不清楚，且个体液体响应差异与个人校准需求未被量化。

Method: 5名成人分别完成"饮水日"与"电解质日"，采用相同的2.0 L补液方案，每30分钟采集一次面部视频，共12小时250段视频；另于单独早晨进行500 mL电解质挑战，采集基线与2小时内4次随访视频（25段）。分析指标包括轨迹、饮后变化、变异性、评分≥50的时间占比，并探索起始评分与饮料日类型的效应，采用置换检验（shuffled-time comparison）作为对照。

Result: 10天中有7天首扫为当日最低值；50次摄入的饮后平均变化为+4.0分，显著高于时间打乱对照（p<0.001）。21次起始低于50分的摄入后无评分下降，平均上升+11.3分（置换期望+5.7分，p<0.001）；起始≥50分者无超随机上升。500 mL电解质后2小时内5名受试者平均变化均为正，峰值增幅7.2–22.6分（30–90分钟）。相同补液方案下，评分≥50的时间占比在16%–100%之间。电解质日相对晨间基线的平均增幅高于饮水日（+7.7分），但日序固定。

Conclusion: 面部视频水合评分呈现与饮水相关的纵向变化及显著个体差异；作为探索性结果，支持在更大规模对照研究中进一步考察个人校准与水合方案。

Abstract: Background. Hydration is often assessed at a single time point despite continuous changes in body water. A facial-video biomarker has tracked hydration-state transitions, but its longitudinal behavior is unclear.
  Objective. To assess within-person hydration score changes, individual differences in fluid response, and implications for personal calibration.
  Methods. Five adults completed water and electrolyte days with an identical 2.0 L fluid schedule, using facial videos every 30 minutes over 12 hours (250 videos). On a separate morning, each completed a 500 mL electrolyte challenge with baseline and four follow-up videos over 2 hours (25 videos). Analyses assessed trajectories, post-drink changes, variability, time at or above score 50, and exploratory effects of starting score and beverage day.
  Results. The first scan was the day's lowest on 7 of 10 days. Across 50 intakes, the mean post-drink change was +4.0 points, exceeding a shuffled-time comparison (p less than 0.001). For 21 intakes starting below 50, no subsequent score fell; the mean rise was +11.3 points versus +5.7 expected under permutation (p less than 0.001). Intakes starting at 50 or above showed no rise beyond chance. After 500 mL electrolytes, mean change over 2 hours was positive for all five participants, with peak increases of 7.2 to 22.6 points at 30 to 90 minutes. Despite identical fluid schedules, time at or above 50 ranged from 16% to 100%. The electrolyte day showed a greater mean increase from morning baseline than the water day (+7.7 points), although day order was fixed.
  Conclusion. Facial-video hydration scores showed longitudinal changes associated with drinking and marked individual differences. These exploratory findings support further research on personal calibration and hydration protocols in larger controlled studies.

</details>


### [136] [FlowVecFont: Scalable Vector Font Generation via Latent Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32327)
*Takumu Fujioka,Gouhei Tanaka*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 FlowVecFont：仅用矢量轮廓训练的纯矢量字体生成模型，通过扩展简单模型规模实现高质量生成，无需外部预训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有矢量字体生成依赖专用生成/精修机制、位图表示或外部预训练模型，导致系统复杂度高、计算与实现成本增加，并引入对外部资源的依赖。作者希望验证：仅扩展一个简单模型即可实现高质量纯矢量字体生成。
